### [40] [Tokenizer Fertility and Zero-Shot Performance of Foundation Models on Ukrainian Legal Text: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14890)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper benchmarks seven LLMs on Ukrainian legal text, finding tokenizer efficiency varies 1.6x across models, Nemotron Super 3 (120B) outperforms much larger Mistral Large 3 despite lower API cost, and few-shot prompting actually degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points for morphologically rich languages.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain.

Method: Benchmarked seven models from five providers on 273 validated Ukrainian court decisions (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks.

Result: (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x; Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) outperforms Mistral Large 3 (675B/41B active) with 5.6x fewer parameters at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26pp due to Ukrainian-language demonstrations' intrinsic properties.

Conclusion: Tokenizer analysis should precede model selection; zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

Abstract: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain. We benchmark seven models from five providers on 273 validated court decisions from Ukraine's state registry (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks. Three findings emerge. (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x: Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family models on identical input, directly reducing API cost. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) achieves the highest composite score (83.1), outperforming Mistral Large 3 (675B total, 41B active) -- a model with 5.6x more total parameters and 3.4x more active parameters per token -- at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points; stratified and prompt-sensitivity ablations confirm this is intrinsic to Ukrainian-language demonstrations, not an artifact of example selection. For practitioners: tokenizer analysis should precede model selection, and zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [41] [Chain-of-Procedure: Hierarchical Visual-Language Reasoning for Procedural QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14928)
*Guanhua Chen,Yutong Yao,Shenghe Sun,Ci-Jun Gao,Shudong Liu,Lidia S. Chao,Feng Wan,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉过程问答（VP-QA）任务系统评估VLM性能，发现当前模型存在跨模态检索能力不足和图像序列粒度与文本步骤分解不对齐两大关键问题，提出Chain-of-Procedure（CoP）分层推理框架，通过视觉线索检索、语义分解细化和步骤生成三阶段提升推理能力，实验验证该框架可带来最高13%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在标准图像-文本任务上表现优异，但其解决视觉过程问答（VP-QA）的潜力尚未被充分探索。VP-QA是一个实际应用场景，用户通过上传复杂过程的中间状态图像来查询下一步操作，现有模型在此任务上面临独特挑战。

Method: 提出ProcedureVQA基准测试用于系统评估视觉过程推理能力；设计Chain-of-Procedure（CoP）分层推理框架，包含三阶段：1）基于视觉线索检索相关指令；2）通过语义分解进行步骤细化；3）生成下一步动作。

Result: 跨六个VLM的实验表明，CoP框架相比标准基线实现了最高13%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: Chain-of-Procedure框架能有效解决视觉过程推理问题，在6个VLM上实现最高13%的绝对提升，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on standard image-text tasks, yet their potential for visual procedure question answering (VP-QA) remains largely unexplored. VP-QA presents unique challenges where users query next-step actions by uploading images for intermediate states of complex procedures. To systematically evaluate VLMs on this practical task, we propose ProcedureVQA, a novel multimodal benchmark specifically designed for visual procedural reasoning. Through comprehensive analysis, we identify two critical limitations in current VLMs: inadequate cross-modal retrieval of structured procedures given visual states, and misalignment between image sequence granularity and textual step decomposition. To address these issues, we present Chain-of-Procedure (CoP), a hierarchical reasoning framework that first retrieves relevant instructions using visual cues, then performs step refinement through semantic decomposition, and finally generates the next step. Experiments across six VLMs demonstrate CoP's effectiveness, achieving up to 13% absolute improvement over standard baselines.

</details>


### [42] [Performance-Driven Policy Optimization for Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Windowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14978)
*Jie Jiang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PPOW通过强化学习将投机解码的draft模型优化从token级提升到window级，通过成本感知加速奖励、分布邻近性奖励和自适应发散感知窗口化三个机制，在多个模型和基准上实现了3.39-4.36倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型draft模型仍使用token级监督目标进行优化，但投机解码的效用本质上是窗口级且对前缀敏感的，导致hard-to-draft位置成为瓶颈——早期不匹配会截断接受的前缀并使剩余投机窗口失效。

Method: 提出PPOW（Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing），结合三个核心组件：1）Cost-Aware Speedup Reward（成本感知加速奖励）；2）Distribution-Based Proximity Reward（基于分布的邻近性奖励）；3）Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing（自适应发散感知窗口化），优先选择高置信度加权draft-target发散的信息性窗口。

Result: PPOW在统一解码协议下，在多个模型家族和基准测试上达到平均接受长度6.29-6.52和3.39-4.36倍加速。

Conclusion: PPOW通过在多个模型家族和基准测试上实现平均接受长度6.29-6.52和3.39-4.36倍加速，证明了性能驱动的窗口级优化是提高投机解码效率的实用方法。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a lightweight draft model propose speculative windows of candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. In practice, speculative efficiency is often bottlenecked by hard-to-draft positions, where an early mismatch truncates the accepted prefix and invalidates the rest of the speculative window. Most learning-based drafters are still optimized with token-level supervised objectives, even though speculative utility is inherently window-level and prefix-sensitive. We propose PPOW (Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing), a reinforcement learning framework that shifts drafter optimization from token-level imitation to window-level optimization. PPOW combines a Cost-Aware Speedup Reward, a Distribution-Based Proximity Reward, and Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing, which prioritizes informative windows with high confidence-weighted draft-target divergence. PPOW achieves average acceptance lengths of 6.29-6.52 and speedups of 3.39-4.36$\times$ across multiple model families and benchmarks under a unified decoding protocol. These results show that performance-driven window-level optimization is a practical approach to improving speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [43] [Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000)
*Rebecca Handler,Suhana Bedi,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究定义并系统评估了医疗LLM中的"过早终止"现象——即在信息不足时的不当回答倾向。五个前沿模型在此问题上均表现出高风险：错误选择率达55-82%，开放式不当作答率30-78%。Safety-oriented prompting虽有帮助但不足以根本解决，提示当前LLM缺乏对"不确定性边界"的认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过早终止（premature closure）是人类诊断错误的重要因素，但在LLM领域缺乏充分研究。医疗场景中，模型在信息不足时的不当承诺（如给出错误建议）可能造成严重后果，亟需系统性地量化和理解这一问题。

Method: 实验设计采用结构化问答（MedQA、AfriMed-QA删除正确答案后测试）与开放式评估（HealthBench、医师设计的对抗性查询）相结合，对五个前沿LLM进行多维度测评。Safety-oriented prompting作为干预手段被纳入以验证缓解效果。

Result: 在信息缺失条件下，模型错误选择率高达55-81%（MedQA）和53-82%（AfriMed-QA）。开放式评估中，30%的HealthBench问题与78%的对抗性查询出现不当作答。Safety-oriented prompting虽能降低发生率，但无法彻底消除问题。

Conclusion: 研究强调了医疗LLM安全性评估中一个关键缺口：不仅需要评估模型"知道什么"，更需评估"何时应拒绝回答"。Safety-oriented prompting虽可降低风险，但残余失败率表明当前模型在不确定性管理上仍有根本性局限，建议在医疗部署前将"适当时机拒绝能力"纳入核心评估指标。

Abstract: Premature closure, or committing to a conclusion before sufficient information is available, is a recognized contributor to diagnostic error but remains underexamined in large language models (LLMs). We define LLM premature closure as inappropriate commitment under uncertainty: providing an answer, recommendation, or clinical guidance when the safer response would be clarification, abstention, escalation, or refusal. We evaluated five frontier LLMs across structured and open-ended medical tasks. In MedQA (n = 500) and AfriMed-QA (n = 490) questions where the correct choice had been removed, models still selected an answer at high rates, with baseline false-action rates of 55-81% and 53-82%, respectively. In open-ended evaluation, models gave inappropriate answers on an average of 30% of 861 HealthBench questions and 78% of 191 physician-authored adversarial queries. Safety-oriented prompting reduced premature closure across models, but residual failure persisted, highlighting the need to evaluate whether medical LLMs know when not to answer.

</details>


### [44] [The Scientific Contribution Graph: Automated Literature-based Technological Roadmapping at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15011)
*Peter A. Jansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper presents the Scientific Contribution Graph (SCG), a large-scale resource containing 2 million scientific contributions extracted from 230k papers with 12.5 million prerequisite edges, and introduces the scientific prerequisite prediction task. Models achieve 0.48 MAP on this task, demonstrating potential for scientific impact assessment and automated discovery.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation but build upon prior discoveries. There is a need for automated technological roadmapping to understand and predict how existing technologies enable future discoveries.

Method: Extract scientific contributions from scholarly articles and link them to their prerequisites using NLP techniques, constructing a large-scale knowledge graph (SCG) with 2 million contributions and 12.5 million prerequisite edges.

Result: Contemporary AI/NLP models are rapidly improving on scientific prerequisite prediction, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting.

Conclusion: The Scientific Contribution Graph (SCG) resource, containing 2 million scientific contributions from 230k papers with 12.5 million prerequisite edges, enables technological roadmapping for scientific impact assessment and automated discovery. Contemporary models achieve 0.48 MAP on this task.

Abstract: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation, but instead build upon prior discoveries. We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [45] [COTCAgent: Preventive Consultation via Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15016)
*Zihan Deng,Xiaozhen Zhong,Chuanzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出COTCAgent，一种面向纵向电子病历的分层推理框架，通过时序统计适配器、思维链补全层和有界补全模块三部分设计，解决了大语言模型在临床统计推理和时间依赖建模方面的缺陷，在医疗诊断任务上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在纵向电子病历推理中存在两大关键缺陷：(1) 缺乏细粒度统计推理能力，在文本暗示定量证据时产生临床趋势幻觉，导致诊断偏差；(2) 纵向病历的非均匀时序特性和标注稀缺性阻碍模型捕捉长程时间依赖关系，限制了可靠临床推理的实现。

Method: COTCAgent框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 时序统计适配器(TSA)将分析计划转化为可执行代码，输出标准化趋势；(2) 思维链补全(COTC)层利用症状-趋势-疾病知识库进行加权评分评估疾病风险；(3) 有界补全模块通过标准化查询和迭代评分约束获取结构化证据。三模块协同实现纵向病历的严谨推理。

Result: COTCAgent在自建数据集上达到90.47%的Top-1准确率，在HealthBench上达到70.41%，优于现有医疗智能体和主流大语言模型。

Conclusion: COTCAgent通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，实现了高效纵向病历分析，显著降低计算开销，同时保持较高的诊断准确率，为临床决策支持系统提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: As large language models empower healthcare, intelligent clinical decision support has developed rapidly. Longitudinal electronic health records (EHR) provide essential temporal evidence for accurate clinical diagnosis and analysis. However, current large language models have critical flaws in longitudinal EHR reasoning. First, lacking fine-grained statistical reasoning, they often hallucinate clinical trends and metrics when quantitative evidence is textually implied, biasing diagnostic inference. Second, non-uniform time series and scarce labels in longitudinal EHR hinder models from capturing long-range temporal dependencies, limiting reliable clinical reasoning. To address the above limitations, this work presents the Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion Agent (COTCAgent), a hierarchical reasoning framework for longitudinal electronic health records. It consists of three core modules. The Temporal-Statistics Adapter (TSA) converts analytical plans into executable code for standardized trend output. The Chain-of-Thought Completion (COTC) layer leverages a symptom-trend-disease knowledge base with weighted scoring to evaluate disease risk, while the bounded completion module acquires structured evidence through standardized inquiries and iterative scoring constraints to ensure rigorous reasoning. By decoupling statistical computation, feature matching, and language generation, the framework eliminates reliance on complex multi-modal inputs and enables efficient longitudinal record analysis with lower computational overhead. Experimental results show that COTCAgent powered by Baichuan-M2 achieves 90.47% Top-1 accuracy on the self-built dataset and 70.41% on HealthBench, outperforming existing medical agents and mainstream large language models. The code is available at https://github.com/FrankDengAI/COTCAgent/.

</details>


### [46] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态RAG系统中粗粒度检索与细粒度查询不匹配的问题，提出GranuVistaVQA基准（含元素级标注的地标多视角数据集）和GranuRAG框架（元素级检测→多粒度对齐→归因约束生成的三阶段方法），实现透明化错误诊断并在基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Multimodal RAG 系统在粗粒度（整个图像或场景）进行证据检索，与用户细粒度查询存在粒度不匹配问题，且失败结果难以验证和诊断。

Method: 本文提出GranuRAG框架，采用三阶段方法：(1) 元素级检测与分类；(2) 多粒度跨模态对齐用于证据检索；(3) 归属约束生成。核心创新在于将视觉元素作为一等检索单元，通过显式对齐而非隐式注意力机制实现检索。

Result: GranuRAG在GranuVistaVQA基准上相比6个强基线模型实现最高29.2%的性能提升。

Conclusion: GranuRAG通过元素级检索策略，在GranuVistaVQA基准上相比6个强基线模型实现了最高29.2%的性能提升，同时支持透明化的错误诊断。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [47] [Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15077)
*Guangyu Feng,Huanzhi Mao,Prabal Dutta,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AsyncFC框架，通过异步解耦LLM解码与函数执行，实现解码-执行重叠和函数间并行，无需微调或修改现有接口，在保持准确率的同时大幅降低延迟。核心发现是LLM具备对符号未来（未解析执行结果）进行推理的原生能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的函数调用（工具使用）采用同步执行语义，导致模型解码被阻塞直到函数调用完成，造成端到端延迟随调用数量线性增长。

Method: AsyncFC是一个纯执行层框架，通过将LLM解码与函数执行解耦实现异步并行。具体包括：(1)允许模型解码与函数执行重叠；(2)在依赖允许时实现函数间并行执行；(3)兼容现有模型和未修改的函数实现，无需微调，也不改变标准同步函数调用协议。

Result: 在标准函数调用基准测试和改编的软件工程基准测试中，AsyncFC显著降低端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确性。

Conclusion: LLMs具备原生能力，可对代表未解决执行结果的符号未来进行推理，从而实现模型-工具交互的异步范式。

Abstract: Function calling, also known as tool use, is a core capability of modern LLM agents but is typically constrained by synchronous execution semantics. Under these semantics, LLM decoding is blocked until each function call completes, resulting in increasing end-to-end latency. In this work, we introduce AsyncFC, a pure execution-layer framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution, enabling overlap between model decoding and function execution as well as inter-function parallelism when dependencies permit. AsyncFC layers over existing models and unmodified function implementations, requiring no fine-tuning or changes to the standard synchronous function-calling protocol. Across standard function-calling benchmarks and adapted software engineering benchmarks, AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy. Furthermore, these results reveal that LLMs possess a native capability to reason over symbolic futures that represent unresolved execution results, enabling an asynchronous paradigm for model-tool interaction.

</details>


### [48] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ML-Embed模型套件，基于3D-ML框架解决高质量文本嵌入的三大壁垒（计算成本、语言覆盖、透明度）。通过整合MRL、MLL、MEL三种学习策略，在保持参数效率的同时支持多语言，在430个任务上于17个MTEB基准中的9个创下新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高质量文本嵌入模型面临三大壁垒：(1)计算成本过高；(2)语言覆盖狭窄，忽视大多数世界语言；(3)闭源或开源模型缺乏透明度，阻碍研究进展。

Method: 提出3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML)框架，集成三种学习策略：Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL)解决存储效率、Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL)提供灵活推理深度、Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL)增强参数效率；构建大规模多语言数据集，训练140M至8B参数规模的模型。

Result: 在430个任务上进行了广泛评估，在17个MTEB基准中的9个上创下新纪录，在低资源语言上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了3D-ML框架和ML-Embed模型套件，在17个MTEB基准中的9个上创下新纪录，尤其在低资源语言上表现突出，并承诺全面开源（模型、数据、代码），为构建全球公平且计算高效的AI系统提供了可复现的蓝图。

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [49] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SRT是一种无需外部模块的多轮对话系统框架，通过自召回链机制使模型能够选择性回忆和推理历史上下文，在提升准确率的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多轮对话系统在处理非相邻轮次间的依赖关系时存在困难，导致一致性和可扩展性下降。随对话长度增加，有效信息变得稀疏且被无关上下文淹没，而完整处理对话历史会带来严重的效率瓶颈。现有方案依赖高延迟的外部记忆或通过迭代摘要丢失细粒度细节。

Method: SRT框架包含三个核心模块：依赖构建（生成并转换为自召回链）、能力初始化（通过召回标记训练推理链）、推理改进（利用可验证奖励优化召回和推理）。该方法无需外部模块即可实现端到端的上下文选择性和推理能力。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，SRT相比先前方法将F1分数提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，实现了推理延迟与准确率之间的良好平衡，并超越了最优基线方法。

Conclusion: SRT通过内部推理机制实现了可解释的上下文回忆，在提升准确率的同时降低了延迟。实验证明该方法在多个数据集上优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [50] [From Text to Voice: A Reproducible and Verifiable Framework for Evaluating Tool Calling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15104)
*Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Xue-Yong Fu,Seyyed Saeed Sarfjoo,Quinten McNamara,Jonas Robertson,Shashi Bhushan TN*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出将文本工具调用基准转换为音频评估的数据集无关框架，评估7个多模态模型发现性能存在模型和任务依赖性，文本到语音的性能差距1.8-4.8分，主要源于语音参数理解错误，开源评判器可替代专有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流工具调用基准仍基于文本，无法评估语音输入场景下的工具调用能力。研究者希望验证是否能在不重新标注的情况下，将文本基准转换为受控的音频评估。

Method: 提出数据集无关的框架，通过文本转语音、说话人变化和环境噪声创建配对的文本-音频实例，同时保留原始数据集标注。包含无参考的LLM评判协议和基于歧义的压力测试。

Result: 7个多模态模型在转换后的Confetti和When2Call数据集上表现差异显著：Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live在Confetti上最高(70.4)，GPT-Realtime-1.5在When2Call上最优(71.9)。文本到语音的性能差距因模型而异，Qwen3-Omni仅差1.8分，GPT-Realtime-1.5差距达4.8分。失败案例多因语音中参数值理解错误。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个可验证且可复现的音频工具调用评估框架，作为专用音频语料库的补充。该框架发现开源模型（如Qwen3-8B及以上）可作为隐私保护的评估器，与专有评判器一致性超过80%。

Abstract: Voice agents increasingly require reliable tool use from speech, whereas prominent tool-calling benchmarks remain text-based. We study whether verified text benchmarks can be converted into controlled audio-based tool calling evaluations without re-annotating the tool schema and gold labels. Our dataset-agnostic framework uses text-to-speech, speaker variation, and environmental noise to create paired text-audio instances while preserving the original dataset annotations. Based on extensive evaluation of 7 omni-modal models on audio-converted versions of Confetti and When2Call, our framework demonstrates that the performance is strongly model- and task-dependent: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live obtains the highest Confetti score (70.4), whereas GPT-Realtime-1.5 performs best on When2Call (71.9). On Confetti, the text-to-voice gap ranges from 1.8 points for Qwen3-Omni to 4.8 points for GPT-Realtime-1.5. A targeted analysis of failure cases demonstrates that degradations most often reflect misunderstandings of argument values in the speech. Considering real-world deployment scenarios, we further report text-only results, an ambiguity-based reformulation stress test, and a reference-free LLM-as-judge protocol validated against human preferences. Notably, we find that open-source Qwen3 judges with at least 8B parameters exceed 80% agreement with proprietary judges, supporting privacy-preserving evaluation. Overall, our framework provides a verifiable and reproducible first-stage diagnostic that complements purpose-built audio corpora.

</details>


### [51] [MeMo: Memory as a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15156)
*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeMo is a modular knowledge augmentation framework that encodes new information into a dedicated memory model, enabling LLMs to access up-to-date knowledge without fine-tuning or modifying core model parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models remain frozen after pretraining, creating a gap when real-world applications require timely, domain-specific knowledge updates. Existing methods for incorporating new knowledge face limitations including catastrophic forgetting, dependency on model weights, and retrieval costs scaling with corpus size.

Method: MeMo encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping LLM parameters frozen; it captures cross-document relationships, handles retrieval noise robustly, and supports plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary LLMs.

Result: MeMo achieves strong performance on three benchmarks (BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue) across diverse settings.

Conclusion: MeMo framework enables efficient knowledge incorporation for LLMs through a dedicated memory model without modifying core parameters.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [52] [Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15168)
*Sayantan Kumar,Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Juyong Kim,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检索增强多模态对齐框架，通过整合非结构化临床叙述与结构化EHR数据重建精确临床时间线。该方法采用图式多步骤流程：构建时间骨架、相对放置事件、EHR证据校准。在MIMIC-III/IV数据集上验证显著提升时间戳精度，并发现34.8%文本事件在结构化数据中缺失，证明多模态对齐优于单一数据源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的临床时间线重建面临两难：非结构化临床叙述语义丰富但时间精度不足且事件时间模糊；结构化EHR数据时间精确但遗漏大量临床重要事件。需要一种方法桥接两种模态以提升时间线精度。

Method: 提出检索增强多模态对齐框架，采用图式多步骤流程重建时间线：(1)从叙述文本提取中心锚点事件构建初始时间骨架；(2)将非中心事件相对于该骨架进行放置；(3)使用检索到的结构化EHR行作为外部时间证据进行校准。在i2m4基准（MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV）上使用指令微调大语言模型进行评估。

Result: 多模态管道一致提升绝对时间戳精度（AULTC）和近所有模型的时序一致性，且不损害事件匹配率。实证分析揭示34.8%的文本衍生事件在表格记录中完全不存在。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持事件匹配率的同时显著提升了时间戳精度（34.8%的文本事件在结构化数据中缺失），证明了多模态对齐能产生比单一数据源更忠实、临床信息更丰富的患者轨迹重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.

</details>


### [53] [Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184)
*Sahil Sen,Akhil Kasturi,Elias Lumer,Anmol Gulati,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Empirical study comparing grep vs vector retrieval in LLM agentic workflows across multiple harnesses (Chronos, Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI) and tool-calling paradigms. Findings show grep generally outperforms vector retrieval, while agent harness choice significantly impacts performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing literature lacks systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm in RAG-based agentic systems. Critical practical dimensions—tool output presentation and performance under increasing irrelevant text—are under-explored in agent loops.

Method: Two-factorial experimental design: Experiment 1 compared grep vs vector retrieval on 116-question LongMemEval sample across Chronos, Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI, testing inline vs file-based tool result presentations. Experiment 2 evaluated grep-only vs vector-only retrieval under progressively increasing unrelated conversation history (distracting material).

Result: Grep yielded higher accuracy than vector retrieval across experiments; overall scores strongly depended on harness and tool-calling style selection, even when underlying conversation data remained constant.

Conclusion: Grep generally outperforms vector retrieval in agentic RAG systems; however, overall performance is heavily dependent on harness choice and tool-calling paradigm, even with identical underlying data. These findings highlight the importance of considering agent architecture alongside retrieval strategy.

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled complex agentic workflows where models autonomously retrieve information, call tools, and reason over large corpora to complete tasks on behalf of users. Despite the growing adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in agentic search systems, existing literature lacks a systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy choice interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm. Important practical dimensions, including how tool outputs are presented to the model and how performance changes when searches must cope with more irrelevant surrounding text, remain under-explored in agent loops. This paper reports an empirical study organized into two experiments. Experiment 1 compares grep and vector retrieval on a 116-question sample from LongMemEval, using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI), for both inline tool results and file-based tool results that the model reads separately. Experiment 2 compares grep-only and vector-only retrieval while progressively mixing in additional unrelated conversation history, so that each query is embedded in more distracting material alongside the passages that matter. Across Chronos and the provider CLIs, grep generally yields higher accuracy than vector retrieval in our comparisons in experiment 1; at the same time, overall scores still depend strongly on which harness and tool-calling style is used, even when the underlying conversation data are the same.

</details>

### [54] [Neural Code Translation of Legacy Code: APL to C#](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13896)
*Abdulrahman Ramadan,Hanen Borchani,Iben Lilholm,Mikkel Almind,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种将APL代码自动翻译为C#的新框架，通过对比自然语言描述中介、检索增强和迭代细化三种引导策略与直接翻译基线，证明了神经翻译能有效跨越APL与C#之间的语言鸿沟，且额外的上下文引导能显著改善翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APL语言语法高度简洁且专业化，导致传统自动翻译面临挑战，具体困难包括：APL语法稀疏、缺乏大规模并行语料库、以及理解APL程序需要专业知识。

Method: 构建了多层次复杂度的功能等价代码对数据集；开发了自动评估流水线，验证生成代码的句法编译正确性和功能执行正确性；对比了三种引导策略（自然语言描述中介、检索增强、迭代细化）与直接翻译基线模型的性能差异。

Result: 实验表明神经代码翻译能够成功桥接APL与C#，适用于多种程序；引入额外上下文和引导能显著提升模型性能，其中引导策略均优于直接翻译基线。

Conclusion: 研究证明了神经代码翻译能够成功桥接APL与C#之间的鸿沟，验证了额外上下文和引导策略对提升翻译质量的有效性，为特殊语言到主流语言的自动翻译提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic translation between programming languages remains a challenging problem, particularly when the source language is highly concise and specialized. This paper investigates the translation of APL into C# using large language models. The task is difficult due to APL's sparse syntax, the scarcity of large-scale parallel corpora, and the requirement for specialized knowledge to interpret APL programs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework for APL-to-C# translation by comparing three guided strategies, namely natural language description-mediated, retrieval-augmented, and iterative refinement, against a baseline direct translation model. We constructed multiple datasets of functionally equivalent code pairs spanning various levels of complexity, and to rigorously assess translation quality, we developed an automated evaluation pipeline that verifies both syntactic compilation and functional execution of the generated C# code. Our results demonstrate that neural code translation can successfully bridge the gap between APL and C# for a wide range of programs, and that incorporating additional context and guidance significantly improves model performance.

</details>


### [55] [Bidirectional Empowerment of Metamorphic Testing and Large Language Models: A Systematic Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13898)
*Zheng Zheng,Zenghui Zhou,Yinwang Xu,Daixu Ren,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了 metamorphic testing (MT) 与大型语言模型 (LLMs) 之间的双向赋能关系，通过对93篇研究的分类分析，建立了"MT用于测试LLMs"和"LLMs用于辅助MT"两个互补方向的分类体系，为AI质量保证提供理论框架和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型因其生成性、概率性和开放性特征，为软件质量保证带来了巨大挑战——oracle问题加剧，传统测试方法的适用性受限。

Method: 系统综述方法，对93篇主要研究进行分类分析，提出涵盖两个互补方向的分类体系：MT用于测试LLMs，以及LLMs用于辅助MT。

Result: 识别出MT在LLMs测试中的多个应用领域（幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性、代码可靠性、RAG、对话系统、自主智能体），以及LLMs在MT各阶段的自动化应用（ metamorphic relation discovery、输入转换与合成、可执行测试实现、agentic闭环测试）。

Conclusion: 该综述构建了一个系统的分类框架，整合了MT与LLMs之间的双向赋能关系，为构建更严格、可扩展且可信的AI质量保证方法论奠定了基础，同时指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have introduced substantial challenges to software quality assurance due to their generative, probabilistic, and open-ended nature, which intensifies the oracle problem and limits the applicability of traditional testing methods. Metamorphic testing (MT), which checks necessary relations among multiple related executions rather than relying on exact expected outputs, has emerged as a promising approach for testing LLMs and other oracle-deficient systems. At the same time, the strong semantic understanding, reasoning, and code generation capabilities of LLMs create new opportunities to automate the traditionally labor-intensive phases of MT. This survey systematically reviews 93 primary studies and characterizes this reciprocal relationship as the bidirectional empowerment of MT and LLMs. We propose a taxonomy spanning two complementary directions: MT for LLMs, which uses MT to verify, validate, assess, and understand LLMs and LLM-based systems across issues such as hallucination, fairness, robustness, code reliability, retrieval-augmented generation, dialogue, and autonomous agents; and LLMs for MT, which leverages LLMs to support metamorphic relation discovery, input transformation and synthesis, executable test implementation, and agentic closed-loop testing. By synthesizing these developments, this survey provides a structured foundation for understanding the evolving synergy between MT and LLMs and highlights future directions for building more rigorous, scalable, and trustworthy AI quality assurance methodologies.

</details>


### [56] [A Non-Destructive Methodological Framework for Modernizing Legacy Clinical Reporting Systems for AI-Driven Pharmacoinformatics: A SAS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13905)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种非破坏性框架，通过添加元数据层（桥接映射、类型化IR、编排器）将遗留临床报告系统转化为AI就绪状态。在558组件SAS库验证，该框架支持共存模式（立即可用）和增量整合模式（92%代码减少）。14份III期报告等效验证中11份达到80%以上，CDISC基准100%通过，证明该框架可实现监管兼容的AI集成临床报告现代化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物开发和药物警戒常受制于遗留临床报告管道。这些单体系统虽编码了监管级逻辑，但产生的输出不透明、缺乏机器可读中间层，导致AI集成困难。现有现代化方法迫使组织在完全重写（风险高、周期长）和增量重构（仍保留结构障碍）之间做出选择。

Method: 该研究提出了一种非破坏性的方法论框架，通过添加元数据层（包含桥接映射、类型化中间表示IR和编排器）来实现AI驱动的药物信息学就绪状态，而不改变legacy源代码。该元数据层将现有组件包装起来，将其输出重新暴露为LLM可消费的结构化数据。框架支持可选的增量整合，用元数据配置的核心程序替换选定的legacy组件，其余部分保持不变。在558组件的SAS报告库（373,000行代码）上进行了验证。

Result: 共存模式下实现了即时AI就绪，产生机器可读输出。在选择整合的场景中，现代化核心实现了92%的专有代码减少。14种III期研究报告的等效验证显示，11份报告达到80%以上单元格级等效（均值82.7%，最佳99.2%）。使用CDISC CDISCPilot01数据的基准测试在5份报告中达到100%等效。LLM实验确认IR支持自动化药物警戒、表格摘要和试验配置生成。

Conclusion: 该框架为临床报告系统提供了一条监管兼容的AI集成路径，可在不中断监管提交的情况下加速药物开发。实验表明，该方法在保持与legacy系统共存的同时，能够显著减少专有代码量并实现高精度报告生成。

Abstract: Drug development and pharmacovigilance are frequently bottlenecked by legacy clinical reporting pipelines. These monolithic systems encode regulatory-grade logic but resist AI integration by producing opaque output with no machine-readable intermediate layer. Existing modernization approaches force a choice between full rewrites and incremental refactoring that preserves structural barriers. We present a non-destructive methodological framework achieving AI-driven pharmacoinformatics readiness without altering legacy source code. A metadata layer--comprising a bridge map, a typed Intermediate Representation (IR), and an orchestrator--wraps existing components and re-exposes their outputs as structured data consumable by LLMs. It enables optional incremental consolidation, replacing selected legacy components with metadata-configured core routines while the remainder operates unchanged. Validated on a 558-component SAS reporting library (373,000 lines of code), the framework demonstrated immediate AI-readiness under coexistence mode, yielding machine-readable output. Where consolidation was elected, the modernized core achieved a 92% reduction in proprietary code. Parity validation on 14 report types from a Phase III study achieved cell-level parity of 80% or above on 11 reports (mean 82.7%, best 99.2%). A benchmark using CDISC CDISCPilot01 data achieved 100% parity across 5 reports. LLM experiments confirmed the IR enables automated pharmacovigilance, table summarization, and trial configuration generation. The framework offers a regulation-aware path to AI-integrated clinical reporting, accelerating drug development without interrupting regulatory submissions.

</details>


### [57] [CRANE: Constrained Reasoning Injection for Code Agents via Nullspace Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14084)
*Mingzhi Zhu,Michele Merler,Raju Pavuluri,Stacy Patterson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CRANE is a training-free method that merges Thinking and Instruct model checkpoints by treating their delta as a pool of reasoning edits, applying magnitude thresholding, a Conservative Taylor Gate, and Graduated Sigmoidal Projection to selectively inject reasoning capabilities while preserving tool-use efficiency. Achieves significant improvements on code agent benchmarks (Roo-Eval, SWE-bench, Terminal-Bench) while maintaining Instruct-level efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The fundamental challenge is that Instruct models and Thinking models exhibit complementary but misaligned capabilities: Instruct models are concise and tool-disciplined but lack strong planning/reasoning, while Thinking models have superior planning and recovery behavior but tend to over-deliberate, degrading agent performance. There's a need to combine the strengths of both checkpoint types without inheriting their weaknesses.

Method: CRANE is a training-free parameter-editing method that operates on three key mechanisms: (1) Magnitude thresholding to denoise the Instruct-Think delta, (2) Conservative Taylor Gate to retain edits beneficial for both reasoning transfer and tool-use preservation, and (3) Graduated Sigmoidal Projection to suppress format-critical update directions. The method treats the Thinking-Instruct delta as a directional pool of candidate reasoning edits and applies constrained nullspace editing to the Instruct backbone.

Result: CRANE delivers substantial gains over both individual models while preserving Instruct-level efficiency. On Roo-Eval: 66.2% (+19.5%) for Qwen3-30B-A3B and 81.5% (+8.7%) for Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B. On SWE-bench-Verified: resolves 14 additional instances at both scales (122/500 and 180/500). On Terminal-Bench v2: up to 2.3%/7.8% improvement in pass1/pass5, reaching 7.6%/17.9% and 14.8%/30.3%. Consistently outperforms alternative merging strategies across all three benchmarks.

Conclusion: CRANE successfully merges Instruct and Thinking model checkpoints to achieve both strong reasoning capabilities and tool-use discipline. The method achieves state-of-the-art results across multiple benchmarks (Roo-Eval, SWE-bench-Verified, Terminal-Bench v2) with significant improvements: 66.2% pass1 on Roo-Eval for Qwen3-30B-A3B (+19.5%), 81.5% pass1 for Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B (+8.7%), and resolves up to 14 additional instances on SWE-bench-Verified, consistently outperforming alternative merging strategies.

Abstract: Code agents must both reason over long-horizon repository state and obey strict tool-use protocols. In paired Instruct/Thinking checkpoints, these capabilities are complementary but misaligned. The Instruct model is concise and tool-disciplined, whereas the Thinking model offers stronger planning and recovery behavior but often over-deliberates and degrades agent performance. We present CRANE (Constrained Reasoning Injection for Code Agents via Nullspace Editing), a training-free parameter-editing method that treats the Thinking-Instruct delta as a directional pool of candidate reasoning edits for the Instruct backbone. CRANE combines magnitude thresholding to denoise the delta, a Conservative Taylor Gate to retain edits that are jointly beneficial for reasoning transfer and tool-use preservation, and Graduated Sigmoidal Projection to suppress format-critical update directions. By merging paired Instruct and Thinking checkpoints, CRANE delivers strong gains over either individual model while preserving Instruct-level efficiency: on Roo-Eval it achieves pass1 of 66.2% (+19.5%) for Qwen3-30B-A3B and 81.5% (+8.7%) for Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B; on SWE-bench-Verified it resolves up to 14 additional instances at both scales (122/500 and 180/500); and on Terminal-Bench v2 it improves pass1/pass5 by up to 2.3%/7.8%, reaching 7.6%/17.9% and 14.8%/30.3%, respectively, consistently outperforming alternative merging strategies across all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [LLM-Based Robustness Testing of Microservice Applications: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14202)
*Hrushitha Goud Tigulla,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于使用大语言模型为微服务API生成鲁棒性测试的实证研究。研究通过在两个不同架构的微服务系统上测试3个LLM和7种提示策略，发现提示策略对测试多样性的影响超过模型规模，并提出将变异分类法与少样本示例结合的GuidedFewShot策略，可实现最高单次运行覆盖率（5/9和8/14故障模式），同时维持测试多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务API中的格式错误、缺失或边界值输入会级联传播到依赖服务，威胁系统可靠性。现有的鲁棒性测试需要系统性地触发这些异常输入以暴露服务器端故障，但生成多样化、有效的测试仍然具有挑战性。虽然大语言模型可以从API规范生成此类测试，但不同模型和提示策略是否产生多样化还是趋同的失败集合仍是未知问题。

Method: 采用受控实验方法，将7种提示策略应用于3个开源LLM（14B-70B参数），针对两个架构不同的微服务系统（一个Java单语系统6服务/9故障模式，一个多语言系统27服务/14故障模式），生成663个测试用例。关键创新是提出 Guided 和 GuidedFewShot策略，将先前鲁棒性测试研究中的变异分类法作为领域上下文嵌入提示中。

Result: 在Java单语系统上，最佳单次运行覆盖率从2/9提升至5/9故障模式；在多语言系统上，从3/14提升至8/14。提示策略的差异解释了比模型规模更大的多样性变化：结构化提示完全消除了多样性，而单一模型配合三种不同提示策略即可在一个系统上达到完全故障模式覆盖，优于任何固定提示下的多模型集成。GuidedFewShot策略在保持低跨模型相似性的同时实现了最高覆盖率。

Conclusion: 研究的主要结论是：提示策略比模型规模更能解释测试多样性的变化；taxonomy规则单独使用不足够，需要具体示例才能帮助LLM区分不同类型的变异； GuidedFewShot策略在两个系统上都达到了最高的单次运行覆盖率（5/9和8/14故障模式），同时保持了低跨模型相似性。

关键教训：taxonomy规则本身不足以生成有效的测试，LLM需要具体示例才能区分key-absent和value-empty等变异类型。

Abstract: Malformed, missing, or boundary-value inputs in microservice APIs can cascade across dependent services, threatening reliability. Robustness testing systematically exercises such inputs to expose server-side failures, but generating diverse, effective tests remains challenging. Large Language Models can generate such tests from API specifications; however, it is unknown whether different models and prompt strategies produce diverse failure sets or converge on the same failures. We report a controlled experiment applying 7 prompt strategies to 3 open-source LLMs (14B-70B parameters) targeting 2 architecturally distinct microservice systems: one Java monolingual (6 services, 9 failure modes) and one polyglot (27 services, 14 failure modes), yielding 38 valid runs and 663 generated tests. We find that prompt strategy explains more variation in diversity than model size: a Structured prompt collapses diversity entirely, while a single model varied across three prompt strategies achieves complete failure-mode coverage on one system, outperforming any multi-model ensemble under a fixed prompt. We introduce two strategies, Guided and GuidedFewShot, that embed a mutation taxonomy from prior robustness testing research as domain context. GuidedFewShot achieves the highest single-run coverage on both systems (5 of 9 and 8 of 14 failure modes) while maintaining low cross-model similarity. A key lesson is that taxonomy rules alone are insufficient: LLMs cannot distinguish key-absent from value-empty mutations without concrete examples. Findings replicate across both systems.

</details>


### [59] [Failure-Guided Fuzzing for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14219)
*Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了对混合量子经典算法的失败引导模糊测试方法，通过两阶段策略（先找非收敛种子，再局部模糊）能更有效地暴露HQC程序中的故障配置，实验表明失败信息复用是有价值的，但concolic种子发现的效果因应用场景而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子经典（HQC）算法（如VQE和QAOA）是近期量子计算的核心，但测试困难。现有的采样基础模糊测试在现实执行预算下可能遗漏经典优化器设置和量子电路参数联合空间中易出错的区域，因此需要更有效的测试方法。

Method: 该研究采用两阶段策略：首先搜索非收敛种子，然后围绕这些种子对电路参数进行局部模糊测试。具体实现了五种预算化策略进行比较：随机混合测试、无模糊的经典枚举、随机种子局部模糊、枚举种子局部模糊和concolic种子局部模糊。实验在Qiskit上针对VQE实例和QAOA MaxCut实例进行实现。

Result: 失败引导的局部模糊测试是相对于随机测试的主要改进驱动因素；concolic种子发现对VQE有额外收益，但在QAOA上不够稳定。这表明失败信息的复用具有前景，但其价值与具体工作负载相关。

Conclusion: 复用失败信息是HQC测试的有前途方向，但concolic种子发现的价值取决于具体工作负载。该研究为量子计算程序的可靠性验证提供了新思路。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical (HQC) algorithms, such as the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), are central to near-term quantum computing but remain challenging to test. Sampling-based fuzzing can expose faulty or non-convergent configurations, but under realistic execution budgets, it may miss failure-prone regions in the joint space of classical optimizer settings and quantum circuit parameters.
  This paper studies failure-guided fuzzing for HQC programs. It models a hybrid input as a pair of classical optimizer hyperparameters and quantum circuit parameters, and evaluates a two-phase strategy that first searches for non-convergent seeds and then locally fuzzes circuit parameters around those seeds. To understand where the gains come from, five budgeted strategies are compared: random hybrid testing, classical enumeration without fuzzing, random-seed local fuzzing, enumeration-seed local fuzzing, and concolic-seed local fuzzing. The study is implemented on a VQE instance and a QAOA MaxCut instance in Qiskit. The results show that failure-guided local fuzzing is the main driver of improvement over random testing, while concolic seed discovery provides additional benefits on VQE but is less stable on QAOA. These findings suggest that reusing failure information is a promising direction for HQC testing, but that the value of concolic seed discovery is workload-dependent.

</details>


### [60] [Making OpenAPI Documentation Agent-Ready: Detecting Documentation and REST Smells with a Multi-Agent LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14312)
*Rayfran Rocha Lima,Davi G. Assunção Pinheiro,Thiago Medeiros de Menezes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The growing adoption of AI agents and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) has motivated organizations to expose existing REST APIs as agent-consumable tools. In our industrial context, this initiative targeted an ecosystem of 16 production APIs comprising approximately 600 endpoints. Although these APIs were stable and widely used within a microservice architecture, early proof-of-concept experiments revealed systematic failures in task planning, tool selection, and payload construction when accessed through MCP-based agents. Rather than attributing these failures to model limitations alone, we conducted an ecosystem-scale empirical assessment of the underlying OpenAPI documentation. We developed Hermes, a multi-agent LLM-based system that detects documentation and REST-related smells at the endpoint level and generates explainable diagnostic reports. The large-scale evaluation identified 2,450 smells across 600 endpoints, with deficiencies present in all analyzed operations. Practitioner validation confirmed high agreement with the detected issues while also revealing contextual trade-offs in remediation decisions. The findings suggested that structural validity within microservice environments does not guarantee semantic readiness for agent-based consumption. Based on this evidence, the organization revised its adoption strategy, prioritizing selective endpoint adaptation, redefining documentation standards, and integrating automated documentation assessment into API governance workflows. This case illustrates how systematic artifact-level evaluation can function as a strategic decision-support mechanism, reducing technological risk and guiding evidence-based AI adoption in industrial software ecosystems.

</details>


### [61] [SWE-Chain: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Chained Release-Level Package Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14415)
*Man Ho Lam,Chaozheng Wang,Hange Liu,Jingyu Xiao,Haau-sing Li,Jen-tse Huang,Terry Yue Zhuo,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Chain是一个评估LLM智能体在链式包发布升级场景下持续维护能力的基准测试，通过分而治之合成管道生成基于实际代码变更的升级需求。该基准包含1660个可执行的升级任务，揭示当前智能体在保持代码功能连续性方面仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法捕捉包发布粒度的连续软件维护任务，变化被捆绑、发布并由后续版本继承，智能体需要在此场景下展现持续维护能力。

Method: 提出SWE-Chain基准，评估智能体在链式发布级包升级任务上的表现。采用分而治之的合成管道，将发布说明与代码diff对齐，为每个版本转换生成升级规范。基准包含9个Python包的12条升级链，共155个版本转换和1660个升级需求。

Result: 在Build+Fix模式下，九种前沿智能体模型平均达到44.8%解析率、65.4%精确率和50.2% F1；Claude-Opus-4.7（Claude Code）表现最佳，达到60.8%解析率、80.6%精确率和68.5% F1。

Conclusion: SWE-Chall基准是可行且有区分性的；当前智能体在跨链式包发布进行正确升级时仍会破坏现有功能，需要在维护连续性方面取得突破。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models are increasingly expected to perform realistic software maintenance tasks beyond isolated issue resolution. Existing benchmarks have shifted toward realistic software evolution, but they rarely capture continuous maintenance at the granularity of package releases, where changes are bundled, shipped, and inherited by subsequent versions. We present SWE-Chain, a benchmark for evaluating agents on chained release-level package upgrades, where each transition builds on the agent's prior codebase. To produce upgrade specifications, we design a divide-and-conquer synthesis pipeline that aligns release notes with code diffs for each version transition, ensuring the requirements are grounded in actual code changes, informative to agents, and feasible to implement. SWE-Chain contains 12 upgrade chains across 9 real Python packages, with 155 version transitions and 1,660 grounded upgrade requirements. Across nine frontier agent-model configurations, agents achieve an average of 44.8% resolving, 65.4% precision, and 50.2% F1 under the Build+Fix regime, with Claude-Opus-4.7 (Claude Code) leading at 60.8% resolving, 80.6% precision, and 68.5% F1. These results show that SWE-Chain is both feasible and discriminative, and reveal that current agents still struggle to make correct upgrades across chained package releases without breaking existing functionality.

</details>


### [62] [FuzzAgent: Multi-Agent System for Evolutionary Library Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14431)
*Yunlong Lyu,Peng Chen,Fengyi Wu,Junzhe Yu,Kit Long Hon,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FuzzAgent是一个多智能体进化式模糊测试系统，通过多个专业智能体协作并基于运行时反馈进行迭代优化，解决了现有库模糊测试方法中环境设置复杂、API约束难处理、漏洞识别不准确的问题。实验表明该方法在20个C/C++库上显著优于现有最佳基线，覆盖率提升45%至191%，并发现102个真实漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前库模糊测试面临三大挑战：环境设置成本高、难以生成符合复杂API约束的测试工具套件、以及难以区分真实库漏洞与工具套件导致的崩溃。现有基于LLM的系统通常作为一次性代码生成器，忽略运行时反馈，限制了代码覆盖深度和漏洞报告的有效性。

Method: 提出FuzzAgent多智能体系统，将库模糊测试转变为进化过程。多个专业智能体在整个模糊测试生命周期中协作，基于运行时证据做出决策，通过多轮迭代逐步优化测试工具套件（harness），实现更深层代码覆盖和更高保真的崩溃分析。

Result: FuzzAgent在20个真实C/C++库上完成全生命周期无人干预测试，覆盖179,619个分支，比OSS-Fuzz、PromptFuzz、PromeFuzz和OSS-Fuzz-Gen分别高出45.1%、73.2%、92.1%和191.2%。此外发现102个真实库漏洞，其中78个已被上游维护者确认并修复。

Conclusion: FuzzAgent通过多智能体协作和迭代优化的方式，显著提升了自动化库模糊测试的效果，在分支覆盖率上大幅领先现有方法，并成功发现了102个真实库漏洞（78个已获修复），证明了基于运行时反馈的进化式模糊测试框架的有效性。

Abstract: Library fuzzing is essential for hardening the software supply chain, but adopting it at scale remains expensive. Practitioners still spend substantial effort on environment setup, struggle to generate harnesses that respect intricate API constraints, and lack reliable means to tell genuine library bugs from harness-induced crashes. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet they typically operate as one-shot code generators that ignore runtime feedback, which limits both the depth of code they reach and the validity of the bugs they report. We argue that effective library fuzzing is iterative by nature: each campaign exposes new coverage bottlenecks and crashes, and the next campaign should evolve from these signals rather than restart from scratch. Building on this insight, we present FuzzAgent, a multi-agent system that turns library fuzzing into an evolutionary process, in which a team of specialized agents collaborates over the full fuzzing lifecycle and grounds every decision in concrete runtime evidence, so that the harness suite is successively refined toward deeper coverage and higher-fidelity crash analysis across rounds.
  We evaluate FuzzAgent on 20 real-world C/C++ libraries against four state-of-the-art baselines (OSS-Fuzz, OSS-Fuzz-Gen, PromptFuzz, and PromeFuzz). FuzzAgent completes the full fuzzing lifecycle for all 20 libraries without human intervention and reaches 179619 branches, exceeding OSS-Fuzz, PromptFuzz, PromeFuzz, and OSS-Fuzz-Gen by 45.1%, 73.2%, 92.1%, and 191.2%, respectively. FuzzAgent also identifies 102 genuine library bugs, 78 of which have already been acknowledged and fixed by upstream maintainers.

</details>


### [63] [When Retrieval Hurts Code Completion: A Diagnostic Study of Stale Repository Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14478)
*Haojun Weng,Qianqian Yang,Hao Fu,Haobin Pan,Xinwei Lv*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过17个样本的受控实验证明，检索增强代码生成中上下文的时间有效性是影响模型生成质量的关键因素——过时上下文不仅降低检索有效性，还会主动诱导模型生成与当前仓库状态不兼容的代码，建议在代码RAG系统中将时间新鲜度作为核心评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强代码生成依赖跨文件仓库上下文，但检索片段可能来自过时项目状态，需要研究过时仓库片段是否仅产生无害噪声或主动诱导与当前状态不兼容的代码。

Method: 在来自5个Python仓库的17个生产辅助签名变更样本上，对当前上下文、过期上下文、无检索和混合检索四种条件进行受控诊断研究，提示词隐藏了提交新鲜度和当前签名。

Result: Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct中过时检索在15/17样本中诱导过时辅助引用，gpt-4.1-mini中为13/17；相比仅当前检索分别提升88.2和76.5个百分点。无检索产生零过时引用但仅1/17通过完成。两模型在过时触发样本上Jaccard重叠达75%，混合条件表明添加有效当前证据可大部分挽救仅过时检索的失败。

Conclusion: 时间有效性是代码RAG稳健性的关键诊断变量：过时上下文不仅移除有用证据，还会主动引导模型偏向过时仓库状态。

Abstract: Context: Retrieval-augmented code generation relies on cross-file repository context, but retrieved snippets may come from obsolete project states.
  Objectives: We study whether temporally stale repository snippets act as harmless noise or actively induce current-state-incompatible code.
  Methods: We conduct a controlled diagnostic study on a curated 17-sample set of production-helper signature changes from five Python repositories. For each sample, we compare current-only, stale-only, no-retrieval, and mixed current/stale retrieval conditions under prompts that hide commit freshness and expected current signatures.
  Results: Under neutralized prompts, stale-only retrieval induces stale helper references on 15/17 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct samples and 13/17 gpt-4.1-mini samples, corresponding to 88.2 and 76.5 percentage-point increases over current-only retrieval. No retrieval produces zero stale references but only 1/17 passing completions. The two models share 75.0% Jaccard overlap among stale-triggering samples, and mixed conditions show that adding valid current evidence largely rescues stale-only failures.
  Conclusion: Temporal validity of retrieved repository context is a distinct diagnostic variable for Code RAG robustness: stale context can actively bias models toward obsolete repository state rather than merely removing useful evidence.

</details>


### [64] [Not All RAGs Are Created Equal: A Component-Wise Empirical Study for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14503)
*Qiang Ke,Yanjie Zhao,Hongjin Leng,Shengming Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统性地评估超过21种不同模型和方法，深入剖析了RAG系统的各组件贡献度，发现检索侧组件（特别是检索算法）对性能的影响大于生成器选择，且经典BM25在多种任务中表现稳健，为软件工程领域的RAG系统构建提供了数据驱动的优化路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在实际应用中的组件配置缺乏系统性的指导依据，导致实践者不得不依赖昂贵的反复试错。

Method: 构建了一套包含3个核心软件工程任务（代码生成、摘要生成、代码修复）的测试基准；在检索侧系统评估了4种查询处理技术、7种检索模型（稀疏、稠密、混合范式）和4种上下文优化方法；在生成侧评估了6种不同的生成器模型。

Result: 检索侧组件——尤其是检索算法的选择——对最终系统性能的影响往往超过生成器模型的选择。

Conclusion: 研究为软件工程领域的RAG系统构建提供了数据驱动的实用路线图，明确指导研究者和实践者如何有效分配优化资源。

Abstract: While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly adopted to ground Large Language Models (LLMs) in software artifacts, the optimal configuration of its components remains an open question for software engineering (SE) tasks. The lack of systematic guidance forces practitioners into costly, ad-hoc experimentation. This paper presents a comprehensive, component-wise empirical study that dissects the RAG pipeline, evaluating over 21 distinct models and methods. Our study systematically isolates and evaluates 4 query processing techniques, 7 retrieval models spanning sparse, dense, and hybrid paradigms, 4 context refinement methods, and 6 distinct generators. We test these components on a suite of 3 core SE tasks: code generation, summarization, and repair. Our empirical findings reveal a crucial insight: the retriever-side components, particularly the choice of the retrieval algorithm, often exert a more significant influence on final system performance than the selection of the generator model. Strikingly, the classic lexical retriever BM25 demonstrates exceptionally robust performance across diverse tasks. Our analysis provides a practical, data-driven roadmap for researchers and practitioners, offering clear guidance on prioritizing optimization efforts when constructing effective RAG systems for software engineering contexts.

</details>


### [65] [Remember Your Trace: Memory-Guided Long-Horizon Agentic Framework for Consistent and Hierarchical Repository-Level Code Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14563)
*Suyoung Bae,Jaehoon Lee,Changkyu Choi,YunSeok Choi,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: Method extraction failed

Abstract: Automated code documentation is essential for modern software development, providing the contextual grounding that both human developers and coding agents rely on to navigate large codebases. Existing repository-level approaches process components independently, causing redundant retrieval and conflicting descriptions across documents while producing outputs that lack hierarchical structure. Therefore, we propose MemDocAgent, a long-horizon agentic framework that generates documentation within a single, integrated context spanning the entire repository. It combines two components: (i) Dependency-Aware Traversal Guiding that predetermines a traversal order respecting dependency and granularity hierarchies; (ii) Memory-Guided Agentic Interaction, in which the agent interacts with RepoMemory, a shared memory accumulating prior work traces through read, write, and verify operations. Through an in-depth multi-criteria evaluation, MemDocAgent achieves the best performance over both open and closed-source baselines and demonstrates practical applicability in real software development workflows.

</details>


### [66] [Mining Subscenario Refactoring Opportunities in Behaviour-Driven Software Test Suites: ML Classifiers and LLM-Judge Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14568)
*Ali Hassaan Mughal,Noor Fatima,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究针对BDD测试套件中重复步骤子序列的重构问题，提出了一种释义鲁棒的子场景发现pipeline。通过对339个仓库的Gherkin语料库进行分析，训练了XGBoost分类器(F1=0.891)来预测切片是否值得提取及应采用的重构机制。该方法显著优于规则基线和LLM判断器，并揭示了75%的场景存在重构机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为驱动开发(BDD)软件测试套件积累了大量重复的步骤子序列。虽然存在三种重构模式(within-file Background、within-repo可复用场景调用、跨组织共享高级步骤)，但尚无方法自动判断哪些重复子序列值得提取或应采用哪种重构机制。

Method: 使用SBERT进行语义嵌入、UMAP降维、HDBSCAN层次密度聚类以识别释义等价的切片；从339个仓库/276个上游所有者的Gherkin语料库中提取所有连续L步窗口(L∈[2,18])；三名标注者依据书面评分标准标注200个分层切片池；使用XGBoost分类器在5折交叉验证下进行训练，并与调优的规则基线和两个开源LLM判断器进行对比。

Result: 挖掘器生成5,382,249个切片，归并为692,020个重复模式。三名标注者的一致性Fleiss' kappa为0.56(是否值得提取)和0.79(重构机制)。分类器达到out-of-fold F1=0.891 (95%CI [0.852, 0.927])，优于规则基线(F1=0.836, p=0.017)和更优的LLM判断器(F1=0.728, p<1e-4)。75.0%、59.5%和11.7%的场景分别包含within-file Background、within-repo可复用场景或跨组织共享步骤候选。

Conclusion: 本文方法实现了BDD重构机会的语料库级普查；pipeline、分类器预测、标注池和评分标准均以Apache-2.0许可证开源发布。

Abstract: Context. Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) software test suites accumulate duplicated step subsequences. Three published refactoring patterns are available (within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario invocation, cross-organisational shared higher-level step), but no prior work automates which recurring subsequences are worth extracting or which mechanism applies. Objective. Rank recurring step subsequences ("slices") by refactoring suitability (extraction-worthy), pre-map each to one of the three patterns, and quantify prevalence across the public BDD ecosystem. Method. Every contiguous L-step window (L in [2, 18]) in a 339-repository / 276-upstream-owner Gherkin corpus is keyed by paraphrase-robust cluster identifiers and counted under three scopes. Sentence-BERT (SBERT) / Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) / Hierarchical Density-Based Clustering (HDBSCAN) recovers paraphrase-equivalent slices. Three authors label a stratified 200-slice pool against a written rubric. An eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) extraction-worthy classifier trained under 5-fold cross-validation is compared with a tuned rule baseline and two open-weight Large Language Model (LLM) judges. Results. The miner produces 5,382,249 slices collapsing to 692,020 recurring patterns. Three-author Fleiss' kappa = 0.56 (extraction-worthy) and 0.79 (mechanism). The classifier reaches out-of-fold F1 = 0.891 (95% CI [0.852, 0.927]), outperforming both the rule baseline (F1 = 0.836, p = 0.017) and the better LLM judge (F1 = 0.728, p < 1e-4). 75.0%, 59.5%, and 11.7% of scenarios carry a within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario, or cross-organisational shared-step candidate. Conclusion. Paraphrase-robust subscenario discovery yields a corpus-wide census of BDD refactoring opportunities; pipeline, classifier predictions, labelled pool, and rubric are released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [67] [Agentic AI in Industry: Adoption Level and Deployment Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14675)
*Spyridon Alvanakis Apostolou,Jan Bosch,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过16名从业者访谈揭示：大多数企业agentic AI采用仍处于初级阶段（Level 1-2），核心瓶颈并非能力不足，而是"能力-部署验证鸿沟"——企业能实验但无法量产，根源在于输出验证机制缺失，表现为信息不对称与资质认证缺失两大维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统正进入软件工程工作流，但工业界实际 adoption 的实证证据仍然匮乏，需要系统性地理解当前企业采纳现状与核心障碍。

Method: 对12家公司16名从业者进行定性访谈，采用基于成熟AI组织改编的六级成熟度框架，评估企业当前agentic AI的采用状态。

Result: 12家公司中7家处于一级（AI助手），4家处于二级（AI补偿器），仅1家达到三级（多智能体编排）。发现"能力-部署验证鸿沟"：4家公司展示了更高阶实验性AI能力，但因缺乏充分的输出验证机制而无法集成到生产流程。四项核心障碍：LLM上下文窗口限制（知识聚合场景）、专有编程语言/协议性能不足、非确定性不符合资质标准、数据保密顾虑。该鸿沟可归纳为两个相互依存维度：信息不对称与资质认证缺失。

Conclusion: Agentic AI在工业软件工程中的落地面临"能力-部署验证鸿沟"，本质上是信息不对称与资质认证缺失两个相互依存的维度问题。大型企业和受监管行业虽走在前沿，但当前缺乏有效的输出验证机制，导致高阶能力仍依赖人工兜底。未来需在验证机制、专有语言优化及确定性保证等方面取得突破。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are entering software engineering workflows, yet empirical evidence on how industrial organizations actually adopt them remains sparse. We present a qualitative interview study with sixteen practitioners across twelve companies of varying size and domain. This study characterizes the current agentic AI adoption state of these companies, employing a six-level maturity framework adapted from established AI-driven organizations. The findings reveal that seven companies operate at Level~1 (AI Assistants), four companies at Level~2 (AI Compensators), and only one in Level~3 (Multi-Agent Orchestration), with large and safety-regulated organizations among the most advanced adopters. The primary finding is a capability-deployment verification gap, four companies demonstrated higher-level experimental AI capabilities but cannot integrate them into production workflows because adequate output verification mechanisms are absent, leaving human-in-the-loop as the only trusted verification mechanism. This gap is shaped by four recurring barriers: context window of LLMs constraints especially when diverse knowledge aggregation is needed, under-performance on proprietary programming languages and protocols, non-determinism incompatible with qualification standards, and data confidentiality concerns. Two interdependent dimensions of this gap emerge from these findings (information asymmetry and qualification absence) framing a core open problem for industrial agentic integration.

</details>


### [68] [Towards In-Depth Root Cause Localization for Microservices with Multi-Agent Recursion-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14866)
*Lingzhe Zhang,Tong Jia,Kangjin Wang,Chiming Duan,Minghua He,Rongqian Wang,Xi Peng,Meiling Wang,Gong Zhang,Renhai Chen,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCLAgent，一种用于微服务系统的深度根因定位框架，采用多智能体递归思维和并行推理架构，通过沿trace图分解任务并递归组织agents来克服上下文爆炸和串行推理的限制，实验证明其在准确性和效率上均优于现有最佳方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务系统因动态交互和演化运行时环境而日益复杂，故障频发。现有传统ML/DL方法的解释性和迁移性有限；LLM方法存在上下文爆炸导致关键证据稀释、定位精度下降，以及串行推理结构阻碍深度因果探索、影响推理效率的问题。

Method: 提出RCLAgent框架，通过沿trace图分解诊断过程，将每个span分配给专门的Dedicated Agent，并按图拓扑结构递归组织和并行运行agents，最终通过综合Root-Level Diagnosis Report和Global Evidence Graph获得诊断结果。

Result: 在多个公开基准测试上的实验表明，RCLAgent在定位准确性和推理效率两方面均持续优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCLAgent通过多智能体递归思维和并行推理机制，有效解决了上下文爆炸和串行推理结构的问题，显著提升了根因定位的准确性和推理效率。

Abstract: As modern microservice systems grow increasingly complex due to dynamic interactions and evolving runtime environments, they experience failures with rising frequency. Ensuring system reliability therefore critically depends on accurate root cause localization (RCL). While numerous traditional machine learning and deep learning approaches have been explored for this task, they often suffer from limited interpretability and poor transferability across deployments. More recently, large language model (LLM)-based methods have been proposed to address these issues. However, existing LLM-based approaches still face two fundamental limitations: context explosion, which dilutes critical evidence and degrades localization accuracy, and serial reasoning structures, which hinder deep causal exploration and impair inference efficiency. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive study of both how human SREs perform root cause localization in practice and why existing LLM-based methods fall short. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RCLAgent, an in-depth root cause localization framework for microservice systems that realizes multi-agent recursion-of-thought with parallel reasoning. RCLAgent decomposes the diagnostic process along the trace graph by assigning each span to a Dedicated Agent and organizing agents recursively and in parallel according to the graph topology, with the final diagnosis obtained by synthesizing the Root-Level Diagnosis Report and the Global Evidence Graph. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that RCLAgent consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both localization accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [69] [Viverra: Text-to-Code with Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14972)
*Haoze Wu,Rocky Klopfenstein,Keith Farkas,Nina Narodytska*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Viverra是一个自动生成带形式化验证注解代码的系统，通过LLM同步生成C程序和候选安全/正确性断言，并利用有界模型检查器组合式验证这些断言，从而帮助用户理解和验证LLM生成的代码，解决Text-to-Code缺乏正确性保证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-Code系统无法保证生成代码的正确性，开发者仍需人工审查、测试和维护生成的代码。然而阅读LLM生成的代码既繁琐又耗时，可能会抵消AI编程工具带来的生产力提升。

Method: 1) 使用LLM同时合成C程序和候选断言（表达安全性和正确性属性）；2) 通过一组有界模型检查器以组合式和尽力而为的方式验证这些断言；3) 在18个多样化编程任务上进行评估，并进行超过400名参与者的用户研究来验证断言对代码理解任务的帮助。

Result: 实验表明Viverra能够高效生成带有验证断言的代码，用户研究结果显示这些断言能够显著提升用户代码理解的准确性和效率。

Conclusion: 实验评估和用户研究表明Viverra能够高效生成带验证断言的代码，且验证断言能够显著提升用户在代码理解任务上的表现（400+参与者）。该研究为解决LLM生成代码的可验证性问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: A fundamental limitation of Text-to-Code is that no guarantee can be obtained about the correctness of the generated code. Therefore, to ensure its correctness, the generated code still has to be reviewed, tested, and maintained by developers. However, parsing through LLM-generated code can be tedious and time-consuming, potentially negating the productivity gains promised by AI-coding tools. To address this challenge, we present Viverra, a system that automatically produces formally verified annotations alongside generated code to aid user's understanding of the generated program. Given a natural-language task description, Viverra prompts an LLM to synthesize a C program together with candidate assertions expressing safety and correctness properties. It then verifies those assertions in a compositional and best-effort manner via a portfolio of bounded model checkers. Evaluation on 18 diverse programming tasks suggests that Viverra can efficiently generate code with verified assertions, and that these assertions improve users' performance on code-comprehension tasks in a user study with more than 400 participants.

</details>


### [70] [Veritas: A Semantically Grounded Agentic Framework for Memory Corruption Vulnerability Detection in Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15097)
*Xinran Zheng,Alfredo Pesoli,Marco Valleri,Suman Jana,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Detecting memory corruption vulnerabilities in stripped binaries requires recovering object semantics, interprocedural propagation, and feasible triggers from low-level, lossy representations. Recent LLM-based approaches improve code understanding, but reliable detection still requires grounding in memory-relevant semantics and runtime feasibility evidence. We present Veritas, a semantically grounded framework for binary memory corruption vulnerability detection. Veritas combines a static slicer over RetDec-lifted LLVM IR, a dual-view LLM detector that reasons step by step over grounded flows using decompiled C and selective LLVM IR, and a multi-agent validator that checks hypotheses against debugger-visible artifacts and runtime evidence. The slicer reconstructs value-flow relations from LLVM-IR facts, including def-use, calls, returns, globals, and pointer operations, and emits compact witness-backed flow objects. The detector uses these artifacts to reason about control flow, bounds, and object correspondence without rediscovering whole-binary propagation. The validator confirms or rejects candidates through guided debugging, breakpoint inspection, and memory-checking oracles. We implement Veritas as a modular pipeline and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of real-world binary vulnerability cases. Veritas achieves 90\% recall. For false-positive assessment, we exhaustively validate and manually verify 623 detector candidates and audit additional candidates from larger cases. The exhaustive subset produces no false positives, while the additional audit identifies two confirmed false positives. In a real-world application, Veritas discovered a previously unknown Apple vulnerability that was confirmed and assigned a CVE. These results support semantic grounding as an operational design principle for practical binary vulnerability detection.

</details>
