### [71] [Ready from Day 1: Population-Aware Coordination for Large-Scale Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13900)
*Angel Wang,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alvaro Maggiar,Carson Eisenach,Dean Foster*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In large-scale multi-agent systems with shared resource constraints, an upstream planner must iteratively evaluate candidate resource plans -- assessing feasibility, aggregate response, and marginal cost -- before committing to one. Lagrangian relaxation separates local decisions through a broadcast cost signal, but the planner still needs the cost-to-utilization response map to explore plan space, and this map depends on population composition that changes across planning cycles. We propose \emph{population-aware coordination interfaces}: learned primal and dual maps, conditioned on compact population summaries, that the planner queries inside its iterative loop. The primal map predicts aggregate utilization under a proposed cost trajectory; the dual map predicts the cost trajectory for a target plan. By encoding response-relevant population structure, these maps remain reliable across evolving populations without per-cycle retraining, and support coordination of large populations from compact subsamples. We additionally cast Sim2Real transfer as a backtestable procedure, enabling evaluation before deployment. In a supply-chain capacity-control case study, population-aware interfaces reduce forecast error by 16--19\% and capacity violations by 20--51\% relative to population-unaware baselines under composition shift; 20K-agent cohorts support accurate coordination of 500K-agent populations; and simulator-trained primal maps achieve 11.1\% MAPE on real observations versus 13--24\% for baselines.

</details>


### [72] [Decision-Level Fusion for Robust Wearable Affect Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14878)
*Lokesh Singh,Athina Georgara,Jayati Deshmukh,Tan Viet Tuyen Nguyen,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文针对可穿戴生理信号情感状态识别的鲁棒性问题，提出基于FBSE和EWT的时频特征提取方法，以及基于预测不确定性和模态可靠性的决策级多模态融合策略，在WESAD数据集上验证了该方法在异构传感条件下的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定基底的频谱特征（如FFT带功率和Welch PSD）会过度平滑短时 discriminative模式，在非平稳动态、伪影和传感器缺失的实际部署中存在鲁棒性问题。

Method: 1. 采用傅里叶-贝塞尔级数展开(FBSE)结合经验小波变换(EWT)进行数据驱动的频谱分割，提取瞬态特征描述子；2. 在多模态融合方面，使用决策级聚合，通过预测不确定性和模态可靠性对各模态进行加权。

Result: 在WESAD数据集的15名受试者、三分类任务中，决策级融合在约84%的情况下至少与特征级融合性能相当，约48%的情况下明显更优。

Conclusion: 在WESAD数据集上，决策级融合在约84%的情况下至少与特征级融合性能相当，约48%的情况下明显更优，表明该方法在异构和部分可靠传感条件下具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automatic recognition of affective state from wearable physiology has clear societal impact for public health, preventive care, and stress-aware interventions, but real deployments require robustness to non-stationary dynamics, artefacts, and missing sensors. We study this problem on WESAD, using baseline, stress, and amusement conditions, where common fixed-basis spectral features such as FFT bandpower and Welch PSD can oversmooth short-lived discriminative patterns. We propose a non-stationary pipeline that combines Fourier-Bessel Series Expansion (FBSE) with EWT data-driven spectral segmentation to extract mode-wise transient descriptors. For multimodal integration, we adopt decision-level aggregation over per-modality predictors and weight each modality by predictive uncertainty and modality reliability. Results on WESAD, using 15 subjects and ECG, EDA, BVP, EMG, and ACC signals across three classes, indicate that decision-level aggregation is approximately 84 percent of the time at least as good as feature-level aggregation, and approximately 48 percent of the time strictly better, suggesting improved robustness under heterogeneous and partially reliable sensing.

</details>


### [73] [Temporal Fair Division in Multi-Agent Systems: From Precise Alternation Metrics to Scalable Coordination Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14879)
*Nikolaos Al. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出旋转周期（RP）和滑动窗口（ALT）两类时间公平划分度量，形成统一框架。RP将时间公平性分解为RS和WPE两个子度量，时间复杂度为O(nu+n)，优于ALT的O(nu*n)。实验验证了RP的12-25倍加速优势，并揭示Q-learning智能体存在传统指标无法检测的协调失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量现实环境需要智能体重复竞争有限资源，需要跨越整个交互历史的时间公平性概念，传统公平划分指标存在盲区，无法检测智能体的协调失败。

Method: 将时间公平性分解为两个子度量：旋转评分（RS）和等待周期评估（WPE），实现O(nu+n)时间复杂度；对比ALT的O(nu*n)复杂度。

Result: 实验发现三个关键结果：（1）Q-learning智能体在RP上比随机策略差10-73%，在CALT上差7-35%，而传统Reward Fairness指标仍高于0.92，存在误导性；（2）RP相比ALT实现12-25倍计算加速；（3）两个度量家族互补：小规模场景ALT区分度更丰富，大规模场景RP可扩展性更强。

Conclusion: RP与ALT互补，共同构成时间公平划分的诊断工具集。RP在大规模场景中更高效（12-25倍加速），ALT在小规模场景中区分度更高。实验发现Q-learning智能体存在协调失败问题，传统指标无法检测。

Abstract: A plethora real-world environments require agents to compete repeatedly for the same limited resource, calling for a temporal notion of fairness judged across entire interaction histories. This paper advances the theory of temporal fair division by introducing Rotational Periodicity (RP), a family of lightweight metrics, alongside the ALT family of sliding-window measures, within a unified framework for repeated multi-agent resource competition.
  We formalise the Multi-Agent Battle of the Exes (MBoE) as a repeated fair division instance and establish Perfect Alternation (PA) as its canonical temporally fair solution, drawing connections to proportionality, envy-freeness, and n-periodic round-robin allocation. RP decomposes temporal fairness into two complementary sub-measures: Rotational Score (RS) and Waiting Periods Evaluation (WPE), achieving O(nu+n) time complexity versus the O(nu*n) of ALT, where nu is the episode count and n the agent count.
  Empirical evaluation across n in {2,3,5,8,10} reveals three findings. First, both RP and ALT expose a coordination failure invisible to traditional metrics: Q-learning agents perform worse than random policies by 10-73% on RP and 7-35% on CALT, while Reward Fairness remains misleadingly high (above 0.92 for n>=3). Second, RP achieves 12-25x computational speedup over ALT, growing with n. Third, the two families are complementary: ALT provides richer discrimination for small populations; RP scales reliably where ALT becomes intractable. Together they form a diagnostic toolkit for temporal fair division.

</details>


### [74] [Multi-Agentic Approach for History Matching of Oil Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15028)
*Linar Samigullin,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PetroGraph是一个用于智能油藏历史拟合的多智能体框架，通过将工作流分解为专业智能体（模型审查、实验规划、参数化、优化、模拟、总结），结合LLM、领域工具、RAG文档检索、ECLIPSE验证和OPM Flow后端，实现通过自然语言启动和引导历史拟合过程。在SPE1、SPE9和Norne三个油藏模型上的实验验证了框架有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油藏历史拟合是油藏工程中的核心反问题，尽管自动化方法可减少人工工作量，但实际部署仍面临挑战：工程师需配置异构工作流（参数选择、物理可行边界、优化器选择、超参数调优、模拟器执行、诊断报告等），复杂度高且需领域专业知识。

Method: 提出PetroGraph多智能体框架，将油藏历史拟合工作流分解为模型审查、实验规划、参数化、优化、模拟和总结等专业智能体；集成LLM智能体、领域专用工具、基于RAG的模拟器文档检索、ECLIPSE输入卡验证、人在回路检查点，以及OPM Flow模拟后端。

Result: 在SPE1合成模型上降低不匹配度95%，SPE9断层模型上降低69%，Norne实际油田模型上降低13%。

Conclusion: PetroGraph 通过多智能体编排实现油藏历史拟合自动化，在三个不同复杂度的油藏模型上验证有效，降低了专业门槛，提供了可扩展的领域感知油藏模型适配基础。

Abstract: History matching is a central inverse problem in reservoir engineering, where uncertain reservoir parameters must be calibrated against observations. Although automated history matching can reduce manual effort, practical deployment remains difficult because engineers must still configure heterogeneous workflows involving parameter selection, physically admissible bounds, optimizer choice, hyperparameter tuning, simulator execution, and diagnostic reporting. We propose PetroGraph, a multi-agent framework for intelligent reservoir history matching that decomposes this workflow into specialized agents for model review, experimental planning, parameterization, optimization, simulation, and summarization. The system combines large language model agents with domain-specific tools, retrieval-augmented access to simulator documentation, validation of modified ECLIPSE input decks, human-in-the-loop checkpoints, and an OPM Flow-based simulation backend. This design enables users to initiate and steer history matching through natural language while preserving explicit control over selected parameters and optimization settings. We evaluate PetroGraph on three reservoir models of increasing complexity: the synthetic SPE1 model, the faulted SPE9 benchmark, and the real-field Norne model. Using weighted normalized root mean square error as the objective, PetroGraph reduces the mismatch by 95% on SPE1, 69% on SPE9, and 13% on Norne. These results demonstrate that multi-agent orchestration can automate key decisions in history matching, lower the expertise barrier for operating complex simulation workflows, and provide a flexible foundation for extensible, domain-aware reservoir model adaptation.

</details>

### [75] [Non-Redundancy of Low-Arity Symmetric Boolean CSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14007)
*Amatya Sharma,Santhoshini Velusamy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对布尔对称CSP中非冗余性参数进行了系统性研究。通过引入t平衡性概念建立了上界判据，利用OR归约建立下界，完成了arity≤4的完整分类，arity=5仅余2个谓词待定，其精确分类归约为极值集合系统问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非冗余性是控制CSP核化、稀疏化和流复杂度的重要结构参数，但此前缺乏对具体谓词（非冗余性具体值）的系统分类研究。

Method: 结合计算实验与代数方法：上界通过t平衡性（与次数t多项式捕获等价）建立；下界通过从k元OR的特殊归约继承Ω(n^k)；未决问题归约为极值集合系统问题。

Result: 给出了arity≤5的对称布尔谓词的近完整分类：所有arity≤4的谓词已解决，arity=5仅余2个候选谓词待定。

Conclusion: 提出了t平衡性概念并建立了与多项式表示的等价关系，为非冗余性上界提供了代数刻画；利用Carbonnel框架建立下界；将未解决的arity-5谓词分类问题归约为极值集合系统问题。完整分类了arity≤4的所有谓词，arity=5仅余2个未决。

Abstract: Non-redundancy, introduced by Bessiere, Carbonnel, and Katsirelos (AAAI 2020), is a structural parameter for Constraint Satisfaction Problems ($\mathsf{CSPs}$) that governs kernelization, exact and approximate sparsification, and exact streaming complexity. It is the largest size of a $\mathsf{CSP}$ instance admitting no smaller subinstance with the same satisfying assignments.
  We study non-redundancy $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for Boolean symmetric $\mathsf{CSPs}$ defined by an $r$-ary relation $R$ whose value depends only on Hamming weight. An instance of $\mathsf{CSP}(R)$ has $n$ variables and constraints given by $r$-tuples; a constraint is satisfied exactly when the induced tuple lies in $R$. This class includes natural predicates such as cuts and $k$-SAT clauses. Our main result is a near-complete classification of the asymptotic growth of $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for symmetric Boolean predicates of arity at most $5$. Using computational experiments and algebraic upper- and lower-bound criteria, we resolve every predicate of arity at most $4$ and all but two predicates of arity $5$.
  For upper bounds, we introduce $t$-balancedness, a lifted, higher-degree version of the balancedness notion of Chen, Jansen, and Pieterse (Algorithmica 2020). We prove that $t$-balancedness is equivalent to the existence of degree-$t$ multilinear polynomials capturing $R$, and hence implies $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)=O(n^t)$. For lower bounds, we use Carbonnel's (CP 2022) framework: predicates admitting a special reduction from $k$-ary OR inherit OR's lower bound $Ω(n^k)$. The only unresolved arity-$5$ predicates in our framework have bounds $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^3)$; we reduce their exact classification to natural extremal set-system questions.

</details>


### [76] [New Algorithms for Parity-SAT and Its Bounded-Occurrence Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14093)
*Sanjay Jain,Junqiang Peng,Frank Stephan,Haoyun Tang,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究变量出现次数受限的Parity-SAT变体，通过随机算法和精细分支技术打破经典指数时间界限，结果优于精确计数问题的已知最好算法，证明奇偶性问题在结构化限制下具有内在的算法优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parity-SAT作为⊕P完全问题，在SETH下无法打破2^n或2^m的经典指数壁垒，论文试图通过结构限制和奇偶性特性的精细利用来突破这一障碍

Method: 1）针对有界出现次数Parity-d-occ-SAT设计了随机算法；2）针对d=2开发了更精细的分支算法；3）利用d=2的结构洞察改进一般Parity-SAT

Result: 三个主要成果：1）随机算法O*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})，对任意固定d打破2^m障碍；2）d=2时算法O*(1.1193^n)或O*(1.3248^m)；3）一般情况O*(1.1052^L)，且均优于对应精确计数的最好界限

Conclusion: 提出比精确计数更好的算法，证明了奇偶性问题在有界出现次数情况下具有内在的算法优势，可系统性地规避经典指数壁垒

Abstract: Parity-SAT is the problem of determining whether a given CNF formula has an odd number of satisfying assignments. As a canonical $\oplus$P-complete problem, it represents a fundamental variant of the exact model counting problem (#SAT). Under the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), Parity-SAT admits no $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^n)$-time or $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^m)$-time algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ and $m$ denote the numbers of variables and clauses, respectively. Thus, breaking the $2^n$ or $2^m$ barrier appears impossible in full generality.
  In this work, we revisit this barrier through structural restrictions and a refined exploitation of parity. We study Parity-$d$-occ-SAT, where each variable appears in at most $d$ clauses, and obtain three main results. First, we design {a randomized} $O^*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})$-time algorithm, thereby breaking the $2^m$ barrier for every fixed $d$. Second, for the special case $d=2$, we develop a significantly sharper branching algorithm running in $O^*(1.1193^n)$ time or $O^*(1.3248^m)$ time. Third, leveraging the structural insights underlying the $d=2$ case, we obtain an $O^*(1.1052^L)$-time algorithm for general Parity-SAT, where $L$ denotes the formula length. All algorithms use only polynomial space. Notably, our running-time bounds are better than the best known bounds for the corresponding exact counting counterparts, highlighting a genuine algorithmic advantage of parity over counting. Conceptually, our results demonstrate that parity admits finer structural reductions and more efficient branching than exact model counting, and that bounded occurrence can be systematically leveraged to circumvent classical exponential barriers.

</details>


### [77] [Fast Leaf-to-Ancestor Minimum Query in the Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14112)
*Aleksey Upirvitskiy,Aleksandr Levin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文在仅支持比较预言机的模型下，为加权有根树的叶到祖先路径最小值查询问题给出了一个静态数据结构，实现了O(n log h)预处理和O(1)零预言机查询，并证明了预处理的比较下界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在仅允许对边（或节点）权值进行预言机比较操作的模型下，如何高效回答叶到祖先路径最小值查询这一基础问题。

Method: 提出了一个静态数据结构，综合运用四种技术：(I) 边到节点权值的确定性转换并引入平局决胜规则以获得全序；(II) 梯子（最长路径）分解；(III) 二进制跳跃技术；(IV) 在梯子数组上构建稀疏表RMQ，预处理时通过预言机选择存储索引。

Result: 给出预处理时间、空间和预言机调用次数均为O(n log h)（其中n为节点数，h为树高）的数据结构，任意叶到祖先查询均可在O(1)最坏情况下时间内完成，且查询时零预言机调用。

Conclusion: 在确定比较模型下，预处理的预言机比较次数下界是紧的。该工作通过组合边转节点权值转换、梯子分解、二进制跳跃和稀疏表RMQ四种技术，在O(1)查询时间内实现了零预言机调用的查询效率，同时预处理时间/空间为O(n log h)。

Abstract: We study leaf-to-ancestor path-minimum queries on a rooted, weighted tree in the oracle model, where the only allowed value operation is a comparison oracle on edge (or node) weights. We give a static data structure that, after O(n log h) preprocessing time, space, and oracle calls (where $n$ is the number of nodes and $h$ is the tree height), answers any leaf-to-ancestor query in O(1) worst-case time with zero oracle calls at query time. The method combines (I) an edge-to-node weight conversion with a deterministic tie-break to obtain a total order; (II) ladder (longest-path) decomposition; (III) binary lifting; and (IV) sparse-table RMQ built over ladder arrays, storing indices selected via the oracle during preprocessing. We also show that the preprocessing oracle-comparison bound is tight in the deterministic comparison model.

</details>


### [78] [Stochastic Matching via Local Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14195)
*Sara Ahmadian,Edith Cohen,Mohammad Roghani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究去中心化系统中局部通信带宽受限的匹配问题，提出两阶段局部稀疏化框架，理论证明在足够spread条件下保持期望最大匹配规模，实验验证接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线匹配问题假设匹配决策必须即时且不可撤销，但在去中心化系统（实时拼车、分布式云计算）中，真正的瓶颈是局部通信带宽而非匹配时机，需要在局部信息约束与全局优化效用之间寻找"中间地带"。

Method: 提出一种参数化的局部选择策略，基于期望实例的分数解来指导修剪决策；理论分析建立近似比与spread之间的量化关系。

Result: 在纽约市真实数据和对抗性合成基准测试中，即使局部预算高度受限，仍能实现接近最优的全局匹配性能，显著优于标准在线基线方法。

Conclusion: 在局部通信带宽受限场景下，所提出的局部稀疏化框架能够以较小的信息损失换取全局匹配质量的保障，理论证明了在足够spread条件下保持期望最大匹配规模，实验验证了即使在高度受限的局部预算下也能接近最优。

Abstract: The classic online stochastic matching problem typically requires immediate and irrevocable matching decisions. However, in many modern decentralized systems such as real-time ride-hailing and distributed cloud computing, the primary bottleneck is often local communication bandwidth rather than the timing of the match itself. We formalize this challenge by introducing a two-stage local sparsification framework. In this setting, arriving requests must prune their realized compatibility sets to a strict budget of $k$ edges before a central coordinator optimizes the global matching. This creates a "middle ground" between local information constraints and global optimization utility.
  We propose a local selection strategy, parametrized by a fractional solution of the expected instance. Theoretically, we quantify the approximation ratio as a function of the solution's {\em spread}. We prove that under sufficient spread, our sparsifier globally preserves the expected size of the maximum matching. Empirically, we demonstrate the robustness of our approach using the New York City ride-hailing datasets and adversarial synthetic benchmarks. Our results show that near-optimal global matching is achievable even with highly constrained local budgets, significantly outperforming standard online baselines.

</details>


### [79] [Hybrid Sketching Methods for Dynamic Connectivity on Sparse Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15173)
*Quinten De Man,Gilvir Gill,Michael A. Bender,Laxman Dhulipala,David Tench*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文提出混合草图方法解决动态图连通性空间问题：对图的密集核心使用草图，对稀疏外围使用无损存储。核心创新是BalloonSketch（l0采样器），实现8倍草图压缩。算法空间复杂度为O(min{V+E, V log V log(2+E/V)})，在稀疏、密集及中间密度图上均达最优。实现系统HybridSCALE首次证明草图方法可在现实世界图上实现显著空间节省（最高97%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于草图的动态图算法虽理论上有空间优势，但每顶点草图成本高达数千字节，仅在图极其密集时才优于无损结构。因此此前没有实用的动态连通性算法能将理论突破转化为现实图的节省空间。

Method: 提出混合草图方法：对密集核心使用草图编码，对稀疏外围使用无损存储。核心组件BalloonSketch是一种新的l0采样器，可将每顶点草图大小减少最多8倍。设计具有模块化架构的系统HybridSCALE，将无损和基于草图的组件作为子程序。

Result: 获得带高概率的空间复杂度O(min{V+E, V log V log(2+E/V)})，同时在稀疏图上匹配无损界，在密集图上匹配草图界，并在中间 regime 下同时改进两者。

Conclusion: HybridSCALE是首个在常见现实世界图上节省空间的基于草图的动态连通性系统。实验表明，与最先进的无损基线相比，在稀疏图（平均度<100）上节省高达15%空间，在中等密度图（平均度~100-1000）上节省高达92%空间，在密集图（平均度>1000）上节省高达97%空间。

Abstract: Dynamic connectivity is a fundamental dynamic graph problem, and recent algorithmic breakthroughs on dynamic graph sketching have reshaped what is theoretically possible: by encoding the graph as per-vertex linear sketches, these algorithms solve dynamic connectivity in only $Θ(V \log^2 V)$ space, independent of the number of edges,outperforming lossless $Θ(V+E)$-space structures that grow as the graph becomes denser. Prior to this work, no practical dynamic connectivity algorithm has been able to translate these theoretical breakthroughs into space savings on real-world graphs. The main obstacle is that per-vertex sketches cost thousands of bytes per vertex, so sketching only pays off once the graph becomes extremely dense.
  We observe that sparse real-world graphs are often not uniformly sparse, these graphs can contain dense cores on a small subset of vertices that account for a large fraction of edges. We exploit this structure via hybrid sketching: sketch only the dense core, and store the sparse periphery losslessly.
  We design new hybrid algorithms for fully-dynamic and semi-streaming connectivity with space $O(\min\{V+E, V \log V \log(2+E/V)\})$ w.h.p., simultaneously matching the lossless bound on sparse graphs, the sketching bound on dense graphs, and improving on both in an intermediate regime. A key component is BalloonSketch, a new l0-sampler reducing per-vertex sketch sizes by up to 8x. We implement HybridSCALE, a modular system treating the lossless and sketch-based components as subroutines. HybridSCALE is the first sketch-based dynamic connectivity system to save space on common real-world graphs. Compared to the state-of-the-art lossless baseline, HybridSCALE saves up to 15% space on sparse graphs (average degree < 100), up to 92% on intermediate density graphs (average degree ~ 100-1000), and up to 97% on dense graphs (average degree > 1000).

</details>


### [80] [Semi-Streaming Algorithms for Submodular Maximization under Random Arrival Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14296)
*Niv Buchbinder,Moran Feldman,Siyue Liu,Sherry Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机顺序半流算法在多种组合约束下求解次模最大化问题，通过开发将离线算法转化为随机顺序半流算法的通用方法和针对拟阵约束的半流变体算法，显著改善了已知结果，特别是在拟阵约束上实现了指数级的通过次数改进，并证明了随机顺序与对抗顺序之间的分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在随机顺序模型下，次模函数最大化问题在多种组合约束类（包括拟阵、拟阵p-奇偶性、p-交换系统、p-系统）下的高效算法。旨在突破之前对抗顺序模型下的已知结果，探索随机顺序带来的性能提升潜力。

Method: 开发了两个技术工具：(1) 将离线算法转化为随机顺序半流算法的一般性方法；(2) 针对拟阵约束的半流变体离线算法。

Result: 首次在大多数约束类上超越对抗顺序结果；拟阵约束下实现指数级通过次数改进，获得(1-1/e-ε)近似；证明了p-系统的随机顺序与对抗顺序之间类似分离的困难下界。

Conclusion: 对大多数约束类，首次在随机顺序模型下超越对抗顺序的结果；对于拟阵约束，实现了指数级改进的通过次数；证明了p-系统的类似分离结果。这些成果显著推进了随机顺序半流算法在次模最大化领域的研究。

Abstract: We study random order semi-streaming algorithms for submodular maximization under a wide range of combinatorial constraint classes, including matroids, matroid $p$-parity, $p$-exchange systems and $p$-systems. For most of these classes of constraints, our results are the first improvement over what is known to be achievable for adversarial order. For matroids, matching and $p$-matchoids, previous random order results were known, and we improve over some of these as well. In the case of matroids, our improved results show a separation between adversarial and random order semi-streaming algorithms, and exponentially improve the number of passes necessary for getting $1 - 1/e - \varepsilon$ approximation for maximizing a monotone submodular function subject to a matroid constraint. We also prove a new hardness result showing a similar separation for $p$-systems. Our results are based on two new technical tools. One tool provides a general way to translate offline algorithms for many classes of constraints into random order semi-streaming algorithms. The other tool is a semi-streaming variant of a recently proposed offline algorithm for matroid constraints.

</details>


### [81] [zSort: Stable Distribution Sort using Z-Score Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14419)
*Hriday Jain,Ketan Sabale,Aditya Shastri,Hiren Kumar Thakkar,Ashutosh Londhe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出zSort算法，通过自适应z分数分布排序方法解决了排序领域长期存在的"稳定性税"问题——即稳定排序必须牺牲性能的困境。该算法在保证稳定性的同时避免了传递复杂度随键宽增长的问题，微架构分析显示其错误推测开销仅19.7%，IPC达1.44；在10^7个64位元素的测试中相比稳定排序算法实现3-4.5倍加速，且在重复率高和部分有序输入上表现优于LSD Radix，达到了与非稳定高性能算法相当的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前排序算法领域存在"稳定性税"问题：实践者必须在保持顺序的高吞吐量和保持执行速度的稳定性之间做出取舍，无法同时兼顾两者。

Method: 提出了一种基于自适应z分数的分布排序算法zSort，该算法通过避免随键宽度的增加而增长的传递复杂度来实现高效稳定排序。

Result: 实验结果表明，zSort相比现有稳定排序算法实现3-4.5倍加速，在重复元素多和部分有序的输入上优于LSD Radix，吞吐量与Skasort等非稳定算法相当，在自适应工作负载上表现良好，且无极端最坏情况。

Conclusion: zSort有效地弥合了稳定排序与非稳定排序之间的传统性能差距，提供了一种高效、稳定的排序替代方案，在保持稳定性的同时达到了与高性能非稳定算法相当的吞吐量，且在重复率高和部分有序的输入上表现尤为突出。

Abstract: Sorting is a foundational primitive in modern data processing, influencing the execution speed of high-performance data pipelines. However, the algorithmic landscape is currently bifurcated by a pervasive "Stability Tax": practitioners must sacrifice either order preservation for high throughput or execution speed for stability. To address these limitations, this paper introduces, zSort, an adaptive z-score based distribution sorting algorithm that guarantees stability while avoiding pass complexity that scales with key-width. The performance of the proposed technique is evaluated using Microarchitectural analysis and experimental results. Microarchitectural analysis shows that zSort achieves a lower bad-speculation overhead (19.7%) than both stable baselines and several high-performance unstable algorithms and sustains a competitive IPC of 1.44. Empirical evaluation across diverse input distributions and datasets of up to 10^7 elements (64 bit) demonstrates that zSort consistently outperforms widely used comparison based stable sorting algorithms, achieving up to 3x-4.5x speedups, and a relatively better performance compared to LSD Radix, with larger gains on duplicate heavy and partially ordered inputs. Despite providing stability, zSort achieves comparable throughput as compared to high-performance unstable algorithms such as Skasort. It also maintains this performance on adaptive workloads where methods like Pdqsort typically excel and doesn't exhibit any extreme worst case. These results indicate that zSort substantially narrows the traditional performance gap between stable and unstable sorting and provides an efficient, stable sorting alternative.

</details>


### [82] [Branch-width of represented matroids in matrix multiplication time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14428)
*Mujin Choi,Tuukka Korhonen,Sang-il Oum*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出一个在有限域上给定矩阵表示的拟阵的分支宽度算法，时间复杂度 O(n²) + O(n^ω)，改进自之前的 Ω(n³)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知算法（如 Hliněný-Oum 2008, Jeong-Kim-Oum 2021）的复杂度至少为 Ω(n³)，希望通过改进算法设计将复杂度降低到接近矩阵乘法最优时间。

Method: 使用矩阵表示的标准形转换结合分支分解搜索算法，通过矩阵乘法指数 ω 优化矩阵运算。

Result: 在 n×n 矩阵表示下达到 (O_{k,𝔽}(n²) + O(n^ω)) 时间，对标准形输入达到 O_{k,𝔽}(n²)。推论包括有向图秩宽和有限域上拟阵路径宽的更快算法，以及对无限域上含有限个不同条目的标准形矩阵的近似算法。

Conclusion: 该算法首次将时间复杂度降至 O(n²) 量级（标准形式输入），并在条件假设下证明了 O(n^ω) 开销的最优性下界。

Abstract: For an $n$-element matroid $M$ given by an $n \times n$ matrix representation over a finite field $\mathbb F$ and an integer $k$, we present an $(O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^ω))$-time algorithm that either finds a branch-decomposition of $M$ of width at most $k$, or confirms that the branch-width of $M$ is more than $k$, where $ω< 2.3714$ is the matrix multiplication exponent, and the $O_{k,\mathbb F}(\cdot)$-notation hides factors that depend on $k$ and $\mathbb F$ in a computable manner. All previous algorithms including Hliněný and Oum [SIAM J. Comput. (2008)] and Jeong, Kim, and Oum [SIAM J. Discrete Math. (2021)] run in at least $Ω(n^3)$ time. Moreover, if the input matrix representation is given by a standard form, our algorithm runs in $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$-time, since $O(n^ω)$-time is only needed for finding a standard form of the input matrix. When $M$ is given by an $m \times n$ matrix, the overhead for finding a standard form is $O(mn \min(m,n)^{ω-2})$.
  As corollaries, we obtain faster algorithms for rank-width of directed graphs and path-width of matroids represented over a fixed finite field. Furthermore, we also present an approximation algorithm for finding branch-width that works on infinite fields provided that the input matrix is of a standard form and contains a bounded number of distinct values of entries.
  To suggest that our algorithm is optimal, we observe that for every field $\mathbb F$, deciding whether the branch-width of a matroid represented over $\mathbb F$ is $0$ is as hard as deciding whether a square matrix over $\mathbb F$ is singular. Under the assumption that singularity testing requires $Ω(n^ω)$-time, this implies that the overhead of $O(n^ω)$ is unavoidable. We also show strengthenings of this observation to rule out some approximations under this assumption.

</details>


### [83] [Hardness of Burning Number Problem on Regular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14730)
*Dhanyamol Antony,L. Sunil Chandran,Anita Das,Shirish Gosavi,Dalu Jacob,Shashanka Kulamarva*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了燃烧数问题（BNP）在连通正则图上是NP完全的且APX-难的，确立了问题的计算复杂性下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BNP已被证明在路径森林等受限图类上是NP难的，但连通正则图作为一类自然且重要的图结构此前未被研究，需要探明BNP在此类图上的计算复杂性。

Method: 通过归约技术将已知的NP难问题归约到连通正则图上的BNP；利用分布参数构造问题实例建立APX-难性。

Result: 证明了BNP在连通三次图上是NP完全的且是APX-难的；对于任意固定d≥4，连通d正则图上BNP仍保持APX-难性。

Conclusion: BNP在连通正则图类上是NP完全的，且对任意固定度d≥3的连通d正则图都是APX-难的。这确立了该问题在此类图上的计算复杂性下界。

Abstract: The Burning Number Problem (BNP) models the spread of information or contagion in a network through a discrete-time process on a graph. At each step, one new vertex is selected as a burning source, while fire simultaneously spreads from previously burned vertices to their neighbors. The burning number of a graph is the minimum number of steps required to burn all vertices. The decision version asks whether the burning number is at most a given integer $k$. BNP is known to be NP-complete even on restricted graph classes such as path forests. We study BNP on connected regular graphs, a natural and previously unexplored graph class. We prove that BNP is NP-complete on connected cubic graphs, and moreover APX-hard under this restriction. We further show that BNP remains APX-hard on connected $d$-regular graphs for every fixed $d \geq 4$.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Distributed Statistical Zero-Knowledge Proofs via Sumcheck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14015)
*Benjamin Jauregui,Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文将经典Sumcheck协议lift为分布式统计零知识证明的模块化原语，并将其应用于非k-着色和子图计数问题，获得首个针对这些问题的分布式统计零知识证明，同时建立了额外的轮数压缩下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式交互式证明近年来发展迅速，但通用的分布式零知识技术仍然有限且多为问题专用型。该论文旨在弥合这一差距，通过引入分布式统计零知识（要求每个节点的视图在可忽略的统计距离内可模拟）并lift经典的Sumcheck协议到分布式零知识证明中。

Method: 将经典的Sumcheck协议扩展到分布式环境中。给定在有限域F上具有N个变量的多项式F的预言机访问，设计了一个验证∑_{x∈F}F(x)=a的协议，产生了O(N)轮和O(log|F|)比特消息，同时实现了统计零知识性和小的soundness error。通过模块化设计使该协议可作为其他分布式零知识证明的构建块。

Result: 对于非k-着色问题，获得了首个分布式统计零知识证明，对任意常数k可在O(n)轮内判断图是否不可k-着色，使用O(log^{1+o(1)}n)比特消息。对于子图计数问题，获得了O(k log n)轮、O(k log n)比特的分布式统计零知识证明，改进了之前的分布式交互式证明并额外提供统计零知识保证。此外，对于常数度图上的非3-着色问题，证明了在多项式时间局部计算假设下排除o(n/log n)轮的下界。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用的分布式零知识证明框架，将经典Sumcheck协议提升为分布式零知识证明的基本构件。该工作不仅为非k-着色和子图计数问题提供了首个分布式统计零知识证明，还通过下界证明揭示了协议轮数压缩的问题依赖性，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study distributed zero-knowledge proofs, introduced by Bick, Kol, and Oshman (SODA 2022). While distributed interactive proofs have advanced rapidly, general-purpose techniques for distributed zero-knowledge remain limited and mostly problem-specific. We address this gap by introducing distributed statistical zero-knowledge, requiring that each node's view be simulatable within negligible statistical distance, and by lifting the classical Sumcheck protocol (Lund, Fortnow, Karloff, and Nisan, FOCS 1990) into a modular primitive for distributed zero-knowledge proofs.
  Our main contribution is a distributed zero-knowledge implementation of Sumcheck. Given oracle access to a polynomial F over a finite field $\mathbb{F}$ with N variables, we design a protocol verifying claims of the form $\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}} F(x)=a$ using $O(N)$ rounds of $O(\log |\mathbb{F}|)$-bit messages, while achieving statistical zero-knowledge and small soundness error.
  We apply this primitive to two problems. For non-k-colorability, we obtain an $O(n)$-round distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof deciding whether a graph is not k-colorable, for any constant k, using $O(log^{1+o(1)} n)$-bit messages. This is the first nontrivial distributed interactive proof for this problem, even without zero-knowledge guarantees. For Subgraph Counting, we obtain an $O(k \log n)$-round, $O(k \log n)$-bit distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof for counting copies of a given k-node pattern, improving previous distributed interactive proofs while additionally providing statistical zero-knowledge. Finally, we show that additional round compression of Sumcheck is problem-dependent: for non-3-colorability on constant-degree graphs, we prove a lower bound excluding $o(n/\log n)$ rounds under polynomial-time local computation.

</details>


### [85] [Semi-Synchronous Exploration in Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14375)
*Ashish Saxena,Anisur Rahaman Molla,Kaushik Mondal,Gokarna Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究1-区间连通动态图中的多智能体协同探索问题，在半同步调度器下建立了探索可行性的精确临界条件：当敌手每轮停用智能体数量超过 ⌈k/(n-2)⌉ - 1 时探索不可能，而不超过 ⌈k/(n-2)⌉ - 2 时存在可行算法，且该算法在能力需求上是最优的（必须同时具备1跳视野和1跳通信）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动态图（拓扑每轮可能任意变化但保持连通）中的自主探索问题，核心挑战在于敌手可随时停用部分智能体。

Method: 建立了探索不可能性的组合下界，证明达到该阈值需要智能体同时具备1跳视野和1跳通信能力，并设计了相应算法。

Result: 证明当敌手每轮停用至少 ⌈k/(n-2)⌉ - 1 个智能体时探索不可能；若每轮停用不超过 ⌈k/(n-2)⌉ - 2 个智能体则探索可行，且实现此阈值需同时具备1跳视野和1跳通信。

Conclusion: 提出了针对给定阈值的探索算法，验证了上界可达；当敌手每次停用最多 ⌈k/(n-2)⌉ - 2 个智能体时，使用具备1跳视野和全局通信的k个智能体可实现探索。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of graph exploration in dynamic graphs using mobile agents. We consider $1$-interval connected dynamic graphs, where the topology may change arbitrarily from round to round as long as the graph remains connected, and edges are assigned with the dynamic port labeling at each round. The execution follows a semi-synchronous scheduler, under which an adversary may deactivate an arbitrary subset of agents in each round. For a graph with $n$ nodes and $k$ agents, we show that exploration is impossible if the adversary can deactivate at least $ \left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 1$ agents per round, even when agents are equipped with unbounded memory, have global communication and full visibility. This yields an upper bound, implying that exploration is solvable only when the adversary deactivates at most $\left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 2$ agents per round. We further establish that achieving exploration at this threshold requires agents to have both $1$-hop visibility and $1$-hop communication. Finally, we present the exploration algorithm using $k$ agents when the adversary deactivates at most $ \left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 2$ agents, assuming agents are equipped with $1$-hop visibility and global communication, and matches the adversarial deactivation bound implied by the impossibility results.

</details>


### [86] [Analysis of wireless network access logs for a hierarchical characterization of user mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14540)
*Francisco Talavera,Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: A Wi-Fi-based hierarchical user mobility modeling method using access point clustering, transition matrices, and time vectors. The hierarchical approach (3 levels) reduces complexity over flat models, performs well for transitions but needs refinement for timing predictions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generate hierarchical user mobility models from Wi-Fi connection data to reduce model complexity in high-scale scenarios and improve adaptability across different geospatial environments (e.g., fog computing applications).

Method: Proposed a hierarchical user mobility modeling approach using: (1) recursive grouping of Wi-Fi access points by geospatial features at multiple granularity levels; (2) user profiling via clustering algorithms; (3) user type definition using transition matrices between coverage areas and time length vectors.

Result: Transition matrices achieved good accuracy; time vector definition needs improvement; hierarchical model (3 levels, one area per building) demonstrated lower complexity compared to non-hierarchical model (single area for entire campus).

Conclusion: The hierarchical mobility model effectively reduces complexity in high-scale scenarios, with three-level hierarchical models (one area per building) showing lower complexity than non-hierarchical single-area models. The transition matrix method performs well, but time vector definitions require further improvement.

Abstract: This paper presents a method that generates a hierarchical user mobility model from the analysis of the data available from Wi-Fi connections. The data obtained from the Wi-Fi infrastructure is defined in terms of the coverage areas of the access points that the users move through. These access points are recursively grouped into different levels of granularity based on their geospatial features. The track of a user is defined as a sequence of Wi-Fi access points, which is enough to simulate user mobility in, for example, fog scenarios. The hierarchical definition of the region under study is proposed to reduce the complexity of the model in high-scale scenarios and to increase the adaptability between scenarios with different geospatial features. The model creation is based on a user profiling method that uses a clustering algorithm and each user type is defined with a transition matrix between coverage areas and a time length vector for the areas. The method is applied to the case of the campus of the University of the Balearic Islands. From the analysis of the mean square error of the results, we determined that the proposed method obtains good results for the transition matrices, but that the time vector definition should be improved. The results also show lower complexity in the case of the hierarchical model, with one area for each building and three levels, in regard to a non-hierarchical model, with only one area and one level for the whole campus.

</details>


### [87] [Multi-objective application placement in fog computing using graph neural network-based reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14649)
*Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper presents a DRL-based framework using graph neural networks and actor-critic mechanisms for multi-objective application placement in fog computing, achieving real-time optimization (milliseconds) with quality comparable to genetic algorithms.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional optimization methods (integer linear programming, genetic algorithms) for fog computing application placement require substantial computation time (hours) and cannot adapt to real-time scenarios. There is a need for faster, adaptable solutions that can be applied in real-time.

Method: A deep reinforcement learning approach featuring a graph neural network and dual actor-critic architecture to model service dependencies and priorities for multi-objective application placement in fog computing environments.

Result: The DRL framework achieves a comparable Pareto set to genetic algorithms while reducing execution time from hours to milliseconds. The approach effectively captures service interdependencies, prioritizing services with higher dependencies in location selection.

Conclusion: The proposed DRL framework with graph neural network and actor-critic architecture effectively solves fog computing application placement in real-time, achieving comparable Pareto-optimal solutions with millisecond-level execution compared to hours required by traditional optimization methods.

Abstract: We propose a framework designed to tackle a multi-objective optimization challenge related to the placement of applications in fog computing, employing a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach. Unlike other optimization techniques, such as integer linear programming or genetic algorithms, DRL models are applied in real time to solve similar problem situations after training. Our model comprises a learning process featuring a graph neural network and two actor-critics, providing a holistic perspective on the priorities concerning interconnected services that constitute an application. The learning model incorporates the relationships between services as a crucial factor in placement decisions: Services with higher dependencies take precedence in location selection. Our experimental investigation involves illustrative cases where we compare our results with baseline strategies and genetic algorithms. We observed a comparable Pareto set with negligible execution times, measured in the order of milliseconds, in contrast to the hours required by alternative approaches.

</details>


### [88] [Malleable Molecular Dynamics Simulations with GROMACS and DMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14655)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Berk Hess,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Static resource allocations in high-performance computing (HPC) lead to inefficiencies for time-varying workloads, causing idle resources, queue delays, and higher node-hour costs. The Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm via a simple API decoupled from scheduler internals. In this work, we integrate DMR into the GROMACS molecular dynamics engine to obtain a malleable variant that can dynamically adapt its MPI process count by combining communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration with GROMACS' native checkpoint/restart mechanism. We evaluate this design on the MareNostrum~5 supercomputer, comparing dynamic runs against static executions and quantifying reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads.

</details>


### [89] [Supervised Distributed Computing: Efficiency and Robustness under a Majority of Adversarial Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14784)
*John Augustine,Henning Hillebrandt,Manish Kumar,Christian Scheideler,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出监督式分布式计算范式，将任务验证外包给 workers，解决了 master-worker 范式的检查瓶颈问题，首次实现在任意常数恶意 workers 比例下接近单次执行的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 master-worker 范式中，master 需要检查 workers 是否正确执行任务，造成严重瓶颈；同时此前监督式方案仅在 β 为足够小的常数时有解。

Method: 提出监督式分布式计算范式，将任务正确性验证工作外包给 workers；通过设计轻量级验证机制，使诚实 workers 能够检查任务输出的正确性。

Result: 在任意常数 β < 1 的恶意 workers 比例下实现了高效且健壮的监督式解决方案，诚实 workers 的期望工作量接近单次任务执行。

Conclusion: 提供了首个能在任意常数比例恶意 workers 下保持高效且健壮的监督式计算方案，显著优于现有 master-worker 和 peer-to-peer 方法。

Abstract: We consider a recently proposed \emph{supervised distributed computing} paradigm \cite{augustine2025supervised} that extends and refines the standard master-worker paradigm for parallel computations. In this paradigm, there is a supervisor, a source, a target, and a collection of workers. The distributed computation is given as an acyclic task graph that is known to the supervisor. The source initially stores the input and the target is supposed to store the output of the computation. The individual tasks of the computation are supposed to be executed by the workers under the guidance of the supervisor. The source, target and supervisor are assumed to be reliable, while a $β$-fraction of the workers might be adversarial, for some $β\in [0,1)$. This covers, for example, the case where a supervisor has to work with untrusted volunteers. In the standard master-worker approach, the master checks whether the workers correctly execute the assigned tasks, creating a severe bottleneck, whereas in the supervised approach, the supervisor outsources this checking to the workers. Prior to this work, only supervised solutions were known for the case that $β$ is a sufficiently small constant. We show that robust and efficient supervised solutions are possible for \emph{any} constant $β<1$ while the expected work for the honest workers is close to a \emph{single} execution per task, given that there is a lightweight verification mechanism that allows honest workers to check the correctness of task outputs, which is significantly better than all robust master-worker as well as peer-to-peer approaches known so far.

</details>


### [90] [Embedded Made Easy -- Rethinking Embedded + Cloud Software Development (WIP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14863)
*Anthony Arnold,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一个统一的编程语言和运行时系统愿景，旨在解决边缘/嵌入式应用开发中的异构性问题。该系统允许应用在边缘设备和云端之间无缝扩展，开发者可以在单一环境中开发测试，然后部署到从资源受限控制器到大型云服务器的任意设备，并提供分布式事件的记录与回放调试能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Edge/embedded application engineering is complicated by heterogeneous software stacks requiring different languages/runtimes for different components, forcing designers to make irreversible deployment decisions. Additionally, multi-stack debugging and limited debugging support complicate distributed application analysis.

Method: Proposes a unified language and runtime system with integrated diagnostics tools supporting distributed event recording and replay for local analysis.

Result: A work-in-progress vision demonstrating that applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, then deployed to any system component—from resource-limited controllers to cloud servers—with integrated debugging capabilities.

Conclusion: The work presents a vision for a retargetable stack enabling unified development, seamless deployment from edge to cloud, and integrated distributed debugging capabilities.

Abstract: The process of engineering and deploying applications in the edge/embedded space is massively complicated by the non-homogeneous nature of the software stack and the complexity of diagnostics & debugging. Often different languages and runtimes are used for different components of the system forcing designers to, irrevocably, make decisions about what components run on the periphery and what components run in the cloud. Further complications arise when handling and diagnosing failures in the system. Multiple stacks and, often, limited support for debugging complicate the already difficult task of analyzing distributed applications.
  This paper presents a work-in-progress vision for a unified language and runtime system that allows applications to scale seamlessly across the edge and cloud. Using a single language and runtime, applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, and then deployed to any component of the system -- from resource limited controllers to large cloud servers. Further, we outline how this retargetable stack can provide integrated diagnostics and debugging tools that allow developers to record and replay distributed events locally for analysis and debugging.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Modeling AI-TPACK in Practice Insights from Teachers Multi-Agent Workflow Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13906)
*Yimeng Sun,Haiyang Xin,Shuang Li,Qiannan Niu,Ching Sing Chai,Lingyun Huang,Gaowei Chen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过行为日志、聚类分析和访谈，识别出教师设计多智能体教学流程的三种行为原型，并揭示AI-TPACK整合需要系统思维、教学信念和自我效能感的动态整合而非离散知识积累，建议采用差异化支架支持策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索教师在设计多智能体教学流程时的设计行为及认知基础，理解AI-TPACK（人工智能技术教学知识）的整合机制。

Method: 采用混合方法：行为日志分析（N=61）结合聚类分析和马尔可夫链分析识别行为模式；artifact分析（n=15）和访谈（n=12）探究认知基础。

Result: 识别出三类教师原型：系统性优化者（迭代优化复杂架构）、多产创造者（快速原型设计工具）和被动观察者（呈现专家-新手两极分化）。AI-TPACK整合源于系统思维、 pedagogical beliefs和自我效能感的动态交互，而非单纯拥有离散知识。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁提供差异化支架支持，以适应教师认知-行为多样性，而非简单传递离散知识。

Abstract: This study investigates teachers design behaviors and cognitive underpinnings when designing multi-agent instructional workflows. Analyzing behavioral logs (N=61), cluster and Markov analyses identified three archetypes: Systematic Optimizers iteratively refining complex architectures; Prolific Creators rapidly prototyping pragmatic tools via scaffolding; and Passive Observers exhibiting polarized expert-novice profiles. Subsequent artifact (n=15) and interview (n=12) analyses reveal AI-TPACK integration emerges from a dynamic interplay of systems thinking, pedagogical beliefs, and self-efficacy, not merely from the possession of discrete knowledge. These findings call for differentiated scaffolding responsive to teachers cognitive-behavioral diversity.

</details>


### [92] [Measuring Google AI Overviews: Activation, Source Quality, Claim Fidelity, and Publisher Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14021)
*Haofei Xu,Umar Iqbal,Jacob M. Montgomery*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究通过40天内对55,393个查询的分析，发现Google AI概览(AIO)对问题类查询的激活率达64.7%，引用的域名可信度更高但30%为新来源，11%的事实主张缺乏引用支持，超过50%的AIO页面包含展示广告，给出版商造成广告收入损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address the unprecedented editorial control Google gains through AI-generated answers that synthesize and deliver single responses to 2+ billion users who may not realize they are AI-generated, replacing traditional ranked-source information retrieval.

Method: Large-scale longitudinal measurement study using 55,393 trending queries across 19 categories over 40 days, with claim-level analysis of 98,020 atomic claims.

Result: AIO activation reaches 64.7% for questions; cited sources are more credible but 30% are new sources; 11% of claims unsupported; 50%+ pages carry display ads.

Conclusion: AIOs represent a "rapid transformation" of online information with significant implications for "epistemic security," while simultaneously impacting publisher economics through ad revenue loss.

Abstract: Google AI Overviews (AIOs) are arguably the most widely encountered deployment of generative AI, reaching over 2 billion users who may not realize the answers they see are AI-generated. Where search engines have traditionally surfaced ranked sources and left users to evaluate them, AIOs synthesize and deliver a single answer - giving Google unprecedented editorial control over what users read and know. We present a large-scale longitudinal measurement study, issuing 55,393 trending queries across 19 topical categories over a 40-day window (March 13 - April 21, 2026). We report four main findings. First, overall AIO activation is 13.7%, rising to 64.7% for question-form queries, while politically sensitive topics see markedly lower rates. Second, AIO-cited domains are more credible than co-displayed first-page results, yet nearly 30% do not appear in those results at all, indicating a source selection mechanism distinct from Google's ranking algorithm. Third, decomposing responses into 98,020 atomic claims, 11.0% are unsupported by the cited pages - with omission the dominant failure mode - and source quality and claim fidelity are largely independent. Fourth, well over half of AIO-cited pages carry display advertising, meaning publishers lose revenue when AIOs suppress the click-through, even as Google's own sponsored ads continue to appear on the same page. Together, these findings document a rapid transformation of the online information ecosystem whose consequences for epistemic security remain poorly understood.

</details>


### [93] [Synthetic Sociality: How Generative Models Privatize the Social Fabric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14090)
*Ana Dodik,Moira Weigel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个批判性理论框架，用于描述性和规范性分析生成式模型。核心论点认为生成式模型自动化的不仅是智能劳动，更是更广泛的"社会性实践"（social doing）。论文通过历史化分析数字经济中社会性商品化过程，界定"使用/交换社会性"区分，引入"合成社会性"（Synthetic Sociality）概念描述由硅谷私有生成式模型部分制造的社会现实，最终提出规范性分析和设计改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型日益普及，但现有研究缺乏对这类模型所生产之物的系统性理论化理解。论文指出，生成式模型不仅自动化了智力劳动或智能生产，更广泛地自动化了人类的社会性实践（social doing）。然而这一核心转变——即社会性本身的商品化与自动化——尚未得到充分的理论审视和分析框架。因此需要构建一个既具描述性又具规范性的理论框架，以理解生成式模型在社会生活中扮演的根本性角色。

Method: 采用跨学科理论整合方法：1）历史化分析：将生成式模型置于数字经济中社会性商品化的历史进程中考察，追溯社交数据成为模型训练前提条件的演变路径；2）概念辨析：明确区分"使用社会性"与"交换社会性"两种形态，以及生成式模型"替代"与"中介"社会关系的不同机制；3）经验归纳：整合现有关于用户使用生成式模型产品及其影响的经验研究；4）规范分析：在上述基础上提出规范性的批判与设计建议。

Result: 1）构建了"社会性商品化"的历史分析框架，揭示社交数据成为生成式模型前提条件的历史过程；2）提出并界定了"社会性实践"（social doing）概念，区分了"使用社会性"与"交换社会性"；3）提出"Synthetic Sociality"（合成社会性）概念，描述由硅谷私有、非民主治理的生成式模型部分制造的社会现实；4）基于理论框架与经验研究，提出了关于生成式模型社会影响的规范性判断及设计改进建议。

Conclusion: 论文提出了从"社会性" commodification角度分析生成式模型的创新框架，认为生成式模型自动化的不仅是智能劳动，而是更广泛的"社会性实践"（social doing）。这一视角揭示了生成式AI对人类社交生活进行商品化重构的本质，为理解和规范生成式模型的社会影响提供了重要的分析工具。提出了"Synthetic Sociality"（合成社会性）概念作为描述这一现象的核心范畴，并从规范性视角探讨了设计改进方向。

Abstract: We put forth a critical theoretical framework for analyzing generative models both descriptively and normatively. Our thesis is that generative models automate the production not only of intellectual labor or intelligence, but of a broader set of human social capacities we name "social doing." We do this by historicizing the commodification of sociality in the digital economy, leading to the availability of social data as the precondition for generative models. We elaborate our definition of "social doing" by drawing a distinction between "use" and "exchange" sociality and further differentiate between the ways that generative models either substitute for or mediate existing social relations and processes. We then turn to existing empirical research on how people use generative model-based products and the effects that their use has upon them. In this, we introduce the concept of Synthetic Sociality, a social reality in part fabricated by Silicon Valley's privately owned and undemocratically governed generative models. Lastly, we offer a normative analysis based on our findings and framework, and discuss future design opportunities.

</details>


### [94] [Computational Thinking Development in AI Agent Creation_A Mixed-Methods Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14330)
*Yimeng Sun,Haiyang Xin,Qiannan Niu,Shuang Li,Lingyun Huang,Gaowei Chen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了93名初中生前在五天AI智能体创建工作坊中的计算思维发展，发现初始CT水平为中等的学生的学习效果最佳（最优发展区效应），迭代测试行为可预测自我效能提升。研究为CT教育中的差异化教学策略提供了实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索计算思维（CT）的发展机制，以及初始CT水平如何影响学习轨迹。研究旨在为差异化教学提供实证依据，而非假设所有学生以相同方式学习。

Method: 混合方法研究，整合了前-后测评估、行为日志和访谈。采用分层回归分析识别学习轨迹预测因子，通过定性分析探索不同初始CT水平学生的学习特征。使用了CocoFlow无代码平台进行为期五天的AI智能体创建工作坊。

Result: 1. 抽象思维和算法思维显著提升（效应量分别为d=0.71和d=0.70）；2. 迭代测试参与度可预测自我效能提升（β=0.20, p=0.05）；3. 中等初始CT水平学生增益最大，存在"最优发展区"效应（η²=0.55）；4. 高初始CT学生存在过度工程化风险，低初始CT学生在任务分解上存在困难。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了线性学习假设，提供了CT教育中差异化脚手架设计的证据。Moderate-CT学生表现出适应型专长，而High-CT学生存在过度工程化风险，Low-CT学生则在任务分解上存在困难。这一"最优发展区"效应（η²=0.55）表明，并非所有学生都从同一教学中获益相同，差异化支持策略至关重要。

Abstract: This mixed-methods study examined computational thinking (CT) development among 93 pre-high school students in a five-day AI agent creation workshop using CocoFlow, a no-code platform. Integrating pre-post assessments, behavioral logs, and interviews, we investigated CT development and how initial CT levels shape learning trajectories. Results revealed significant improvements in abstract thinking (effect size d = 0.71) and algorithmic thinking (effect size d = 0.70). Hierarchical regression identified iterative testing engagement as a predictor of self-efficacy gains (beta = 0.20, p = 0.05). Notably, students with moderate initial CT levels demonstrated substantially greater gains than both high-CT and low-CT peers, revealing an Optimal Development Zone effect (eta squared = 0.55). Qualitative analysis showed moderate-CT students exhibited adaptive expertise, while high-CT students risked over-engineering and low-CT students struggled with task decomposition. These findings challenge linear learning assumptions and provide evidence for differentiated scaffolding in CT education.

</details>


### [95] [The Racial Character of Computer Graphics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14835)
*Theodore Kim,Alexa Schor,Julian Posada,Alka V. Menon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文系统性审查了计算机图形学顶会（SIGGRAPH等）中的人类描绘算法，发现标榜"通用"的皮肤和头发渲染算法实则偏向白人族群特征，提出"McDaniels Methods"批判此类隐性种族偏见，并指出多个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑计算机图形学算法是否真正具有通用性；验证关于3D人类描绘算法偏向历史上霸权特征（白人特征）的假设。

Method: 系统性文献综述，分析SIGGRAPH和ACM Transactions on Graphics上发表的人类描绘相关论文；案例研究包括皮肤渲染算法和头发渲染算法；历史时间线分析（特别是2020年George Floyd事件后Type 4头发算法才出现）。

Result: 确认了假设：皮肤渲染算法实际针对"高反照率"的半透明白色皮肤材料；头发算法针对直发的"杆状"、"线状"、"线程状"模型；存在概念二元化问题——白色皮肤算法被视为所有皮肤的计算基础，形成等级假设；直到2020年George Floyd事件后才出现首个计算机生成的Type 4（卷曲）头发算法。

Conclusion: 作者引入了"McDaniels Methods"标签来批判以多样性掩盖种族等级强化的算法，并提出"Durald Methods"作为反例。还指出了多个未来研究的空白领域，如针对不同肤色的专门算法开发、头发渲染技术的改进等。

Abstract: Computer graphics algorithms for generating photorealistic imagery are widely perceived to be universal, and capable of conjuring anything that a filmmaker or game designer can imagine. However, recent works have suggested that 3D algorithms for depicting synthetic humans are far from generic, and instead favor historically hegemonic characteristics. We present the first systematic review of human depiction in the top computer graphics conference and the journal of record (SIGGRAPH and ACM Transactions on Graphics) that confirms previous hypotheses. Algorithms that claim to be generically rendering "human skin'' are in fact imagined and formulated for translucent, "high albedo" materials such as white skin. Algorithms claiming to apply generically to "human hair" are formulated for "rods", "wires" and "threads" which are analogous to straight hair. Our analysis reveals conceptual binarization, where algorithms for white skin are treated as computational substrate for "all" skin, imposing a hierarchical assumption that all skin descends from the math and physics of white skin. Hair algorithms follow a similar historical pattern, with the first examples of computer-generated Type 4 hair only appearing after the murder of George Floyd in 2020. We offer a new conceptual label, McDaniels Methods, for characterizing and critiquing computer graphics algorithms that reinforce racial hierarchy under a false cover of diversity. We also offer an inverse label, Durald Methods, for algorithms that were closely co-designed with the people being depicted. Our analysis points the way towards several neglected avenues for future research.

</details>


### [96] [Tradeoffs are Domain Dependent: Improving Accuracy and Fairness in Property Tax Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15020)
*Evelyn Smith,Emma Harvey,Christopher Berry,Jacob Goldin,Daniel E. Ho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文挑战了算法公平性研究中准确性-公平性必然权衡的假设，通过2600万房产数据证明，在房产税评估这一公共部门场景中，精心设计的模型改进可同时提升准确性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法公平性研究普遍认为公平性与准确性之间存在权衡，但这种权衡可能并非普遍存在。研究以美国房产税评估为背景，探究系统性评估错误导致低价房产所有者承担不成比例的高税负这一不公平现象。

Method: 利用涵盖美国95%县的2600万笔房产销售数据，采用三种互补性分析方法：现状实践分析、模拟评估模型测试，以及Census数据整合效果评估。

Result: 研究发现：（1）现状实践下，评估准确性与公平性高度正相关；（2）模拟模型中添加房产特征大多提升准确性，且准确性提升时公平性几乎必然改善；（3）整合Census数据可同时显著提升准确性和公平性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在大规模公共部门系统中，精心设计的建模改进可以同时提升公平性和准确性，挑战了人们对准确性-公平性权衡的普遍假设。

Abstract: Algorithmic fairness research often assumes a tradeoff between fairness and accuracy. Yet this tradeoff may not be universal. We test this assumption in the context of U.S. property tax assessment - a setting in which the output of predictive algorithms directly determines the distribution of tax obligations among homeowners. Currently, systematic assessment errors cause owners of lower-valued properties to face disproportionately high tax burdens, creating regressivity in the property tax system. Using data on 26 million property sales spanning 95% of U.S. counties, we conduct three complementary analyses. First, we find that assessment accuracy and fairness - measured using domain-relevant metrics - are strongly correlated across counties under status quo practices. Second, in simulated assessment models, we show that adding property features improves accuracy in most cases, and that when accuracy improves, fairness almost always improves as well. Third, we show that incorporating publicly available Census data into assessment models - a feasible reform in most counties - would significantly improve both accuracy and fairness relative to status quo assessments. Together, these results challenge the presumed universality of the fairness-accuracy tradeoff and demonstrate that well-designed modeling improvements can advance both fairness and accuracy in large-scale public sector systems.

</details>


### [97] [Due Process on Hold: A Queueing Framework for Improving Access in SNAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15165)
*Andrew Daw,Chloe Pache,Angela Zhou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对美国社会安全网呼叫中心拥堵导致程序性拒绝的问题，运用排队论构建考虑重拨和放弃行为的服务访问评估框架，通过法律案例数据和流体近似模型，为改善服务获取机会和政策制定提供分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国社会保障系统通过拥堵的呼叫中心提供服务，导致大量程序性拒绝（而非实质性资格问题），现有 Erlang-A 模型未能考虑内生性拥堵问题，可能导致人员配置不足。

Method: 采用排队论方法构建包含重拨和放弃现象的队列模型，通过流体近似推导稳态性能指标，用法庭披露的呼叫中心数据进行参数拟合，分析人员配置和服务交付变化的组合效应。

Result: 标准排队论指导未考虑内生性拥堵时会根本性地低估人力需求；系统动态与访问需求交互产生的"紧急任意性"需从系统层面诊断和修复。

Conclusion: 研究提出的排队模型可为社会服务系统的访问评估和政策制定提供工具，支持事前评估和系统设计，为判断申请人是否获得实质性服务机会提供证据。

Abstract: The U.S. social safety net delivers essential services at mass scale, but access burdens persist, as congested contact or call centers serve as a primary mode of application completion and assistance. In Holmes v. Knodell, Missouri's SNAP call centers were so congested that nearly half of all application denials were procedural, caused by applicants' inability to complete required interviews, rather than underlying ineligibility. The judge ruled these system failures led to a violation of procedural due process. We propose a performance evaluation framework based on queueing models from operations research and management to assess and improve access in such systems. Operational access failures of call centers are distinct from prior automation failures in benefits provision. Emergent arbitrariness arises from interactions between system dynamics and access demand, rather than from an explicit algorithmic rule, making diagnosis and repair inherently system-level. We develop a queueing model that incorporates phenomena that distinguish social services from standard service domains, redials and abandonment, through which backlogs generate endogenous congestion. Standard queueing guidance from Erlang-A that does not address endogenous congestion fundamentally understaffs, which could lead to persistent shortfalls in practice. Using a fluid approximation, we derive steady-state performance metrics to analytically characterize the impacts of bundled staffing and service delivery changes. We fit model parameters to call-center data disclosed in court documents. Our queueing model can support ex-ante evaluation and design of access systems, inform policy levers for improving access, and provide evidence about whether applicants are afforded a meaningful opportunity to be served at scale.

</details>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [SemaTune: Semantic-Aware Online OS Tuning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15026)
*Georgios Liargkovas,Mihir Nitin Joshi,Hubertus Franke,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: SemaTune是一个用于稳态OS调优的主机端框架，通过约束性的LLM指导将操作系统控制语义纳入优化过程，采用快速/慢速双循环架构和类型化验证机制，在保持低成本低延迟的同时显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线OS调优控制器与运行中的主机匹配度差：将其视为黑箱变量并优化标量奖励，导致以下问题——忽略跨旋钮策略结构、在应用指标不可用时失效、可能将运行中的服务推入移除错误设置后仍持续存在的性能降级区域。

Method: SemaTune采用双循环架构：快速循环负责提出低延迟更新，慢速循环定期修订搜索策略。核心设计包括：(1)将knob schemas、遥测数据、当前配置、历史动作-响应及检索到的先前运行记录整合为紧凑决策上下文；(2)所有提议变更在到达kernel或sysctl接口前均需通过类型化验证；(3)使控制器能够理解OS控制语义和间接性能信号，同时保持模型成本、延迟和权限约束。

Result: 在13个真实工作负载（来自5个基准测试套件）、最多41个Linux参数的评估中：(1)稳定阶段性能相比默认设置提升72.5%，相比最强非LLM基线提升153.3%；(2)30窗口会话的模型调用成本仅约$0.20；(3)即使仅使用主机级指标，SemaTune仍比直接应用目标的基线高出93.7个百分点，同时避免了结构盲探索导致的严重性能降级区域。

Conclusion: SemaTune通过双层优化循环和类型化验证机制，成功将LLM推理能力与OS级控制相结合，在仅需$0.20成本的情况下实现了显著的性能提升（相比默认设置提升72.5%，相比最强非LLM基线提升153.3%），同时有效避免了结构盲探索导致的服务降级问题。

Abstract: Online OS tuning can improve long-running services, but existing controllers are poorly matched to live hosts. They treat scheduler, power, memory, and I/O controls as black-box variables and optimize a scalar reward. This view ignores cross-knob policy structure, breaks down when application metrics are unavailable, and can send a running service into degraded regions that persist after the bad setting is removed. We present SemaTune, a host-side framework for steady-state OS tuning with bounded language-model guidance. SemaTune turns knob schemas, telemetry, current configuration, recent action--response history, and retrieved prior runs into a compact decision context. A fast loop proposes low-latency updates, a slower loop periodically revises the search strategy, and every proposed change passes through typed validation before reaching kernel or sysctl interfaces. This lets the controller reason about OS-control meaning and indirect performance signals while keeping model cost, latency, and authority constrained. We evaluate SemaTune on 13 live workloads from five benchmark suites while tuning up to 41 Linux parameters. Across the suite, SemaTune improves stable-phase performance by 72.5\% over default settings and by 153.3\% relative to the strongest non-LLM baseline. A 30-window session costs about \$0.20 in model calls. With only host-level metrics, SemaTune still outperforms baselines given direct application objectives by 93.7 percentage points, while avoiding severe degraded regions reached by structure-blind exploration.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究可复用的past-time STL认证运行时监控器，证明语义基线是单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口中的最小预测目标，单次校准可认证整个公式片段，实验验证在长时域场景下语义基线监控器比滚动预测监控器精确度提升4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决部分可观测环境下（视觉输入）的安全认证监控问题，要求监控器具备有限样本保证和跨公式复用能力，无需针对每个公式重新训练。

Method: 1. 证明语义基线（原子鲁棒性分数向量）在单调、1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中是最小预测目标；2. 利用解析树导出的确定性解码器评估任意公式；3. 通过单次conformal校准过程认证整个公式片段，避免联合边界问题；4. 提出滚动预测监控器，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重建时序历史。

Result: 1. 行人过街基准测试：滚动预测在短时域更紧，语义基线在长时域精确度提升达4倍；2. Waymo驾驶数据验证：两种监控器均满足 conformal coverage 实证保证。

Conclusion: 语义基线方法为可复用认证运行时监控提供了理论完备且实践有效的解决方案，在长时域场景下比滚动预测方法精确度高4倍，且在Waymo真实驾驶数据上验证了 conformal coverage 保证的有效性。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [100] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对三种EEG Transformer模型应用稀疏自编码器进行可解释性分析，建立了一套跨架构的稳健可解释性框架，揭示了模型表征中的关键缺陷（如wrecking-ball效应和年龄-病理纠缠），并将潜在空间操作转化为可解释的生理频谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG foundation models achieve strong clinical performance, but their internal computations remain opaque, creating a barrier to clinical trust.

Method: Applied TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers (SleepFM, REVE, LaBraM) to extract sparse feature dictionaries from embeddings; introduced a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric for quantifying steering selectivity; developed a spectral decoder to map latent interventions back to amplitude spectrum.

Result: Identified three operational regimes (selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, non-encoded), discovered "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse model performance, and found clinical entanglements like age-pathology confounding where suppressing one concept corrupts the other; translated latent manipulations into physiological frequency signatures like pathological slow-wave suppression and α-band restoration.

Conclusion: The work successfully establishes a robust, architecture-agnostic framework for interpreting EEG foundation models, revealing critical representational failures (wrecking-ball effects, age-pathology confounding) and translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable spectral signatures.

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [101] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对dMRI采集差异导致的结构连接组变异性问题，提出一种基于架构退火的无监督框架，通过Joint-VAE自适应平衡离散和连续潜变量，消除手动调参需求，在大规模数据集上验证了该方法能有效分离采集相关效应与生物变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI跨站点、扫描仪和协议的采集差异引入了变异性，使结构连接组分析复杂化。传统降维方法将所有方差建模为连续的，导致采集效应被吸收到连续潜空间中。虽然近期混合潜空间模型尝试结合离散和连续成分，但通常需要手动调整容量以确保离散成分捕获目标变异性。

Method: 提出一种无监督框架，通过架构退火（architectural annealing）技术，在解码前对编码器输出进行退火处理，使模型能够在训练过程中自适应平衡离散和连续潜变量，从而无需手动调整容量参数。方法基于Joint-VAE架构，实现混合连续-离散潜空间。

Result: 架构退火产生更强的站点学习效果（ARI=0.53, p<0.05），优于标准VAE、PCA结合k-means聚类以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。结果表明混合连续-离散潜空间能够恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模平滑和分类结构，Joint-VAE恢复了与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类，证明架构退火而非基于损失的退火是一种有效的无监督机制，可用于捕获dMRI中的采集变异性。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [102] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于联邦学习的框架，在不交换私有数据的情况下跨数据孤岛联合微调LLM。实验表明联邦微调接近集中式训练效果，QLoRA和IA3是平衡效率与精度的有效PEFT策略，为隐私约束环境下的LLM适配提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM发展的重要方向是利用私有数据，但医疗和金融等高价值数据受隐私和法规约束无法共享，且机构数据通常是非独立同分布的（non-IID），如何在保护数据隐私的同时利用分布式数据提升模型能力是一个关键挑战。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台，在非IID设置下对比了三种参数高效微调策略（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3）在预训练模型上的表现，跨医疗和金融领域的四个问答和分类数据集进行评估。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单机构隔离学习；QLoRA和IA3在有限精度损失下提升了效率。

Conclusion: QLoRA和IA3在保持精度的同时提高了效率，证明联邦PEFT是解决数据隐私隔离问题的可行方案，可用于在无法共享数据的环境中适配LLM。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [103] [EMA: Efficient Model Adaptation for Learning-based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13942)
*Daiyang Yu,Xinyu Chen,Yihan Zhang,Yan Liang,Yaqi Qiao,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is increasingly applied to optimize system performance in tasks such as resource management and network simulation. Unlike traditional ML tasks (e.g., image classification), networked systems often operate in heterogeneous, long-running, and dynamic environment states, where input conditions (e.g., network loads) and operational objectives can shift over time and across settings. Existing learning-based systems offer little support for adaptation, resulting in costly model training, extensive data collection, degraded system performance, and slow responsiveness.
  This paper presents EMA, the first model adaptation system supporting learning-based systems to adapt to evolving environments with minimal operational overhead. EMA takes a system-driven, data-centric approach that accommodates diverse system and model designs while addressing two key deployment challenges. First, it reduces expensive model training by introducing state transformers that align the input state of a new environment with previously similar states, allowing models to warm-start adaptation. Second, it addresses the often-overlooked yet costly process of data labeling--collecting ground truth for exploring and training on various system decisions--by prioritizing labeling high-utility data while balancing the tradeoff between training and labeling cost. Evaluations on eight representative learning-based systems show that EMA reduces adaptation costs (e.g., GPU training time) by 14.9-42.4% while improving system performance (e.g., network throughput) by 6.9-31.3%.

</details>


### [104] [The Geometry of LLM Quantization: GPTQ as Babai's Nearest Plane Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18553)
*Jiale Chen,Yalda Shabanzadeh,Elvir Crnčević,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示 GPTQ 与经典格论中 Babai 最近平面算法的数学等价性，赋予其几何解释和误差上界，并据此设计改进的量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ 作为 LLM 规模的一次性后训练量化标准方法，其内部工作原理被描述为一系列代数更新，缺乏几何解释或最坏情况保证。

Method: 通过反向执行 GPTQ（从最后维度到第一维度）线性层，证明了其与 Babai 最近平面算法在由 Hessian 矩阵定义的格上的数学等价性。设计避免裁剪的后训练量化方法并提供高效 GPU 推理核。

Result: 两个分析成果：1) GPTQ 误差传播步骤获得直观几何解释；2) GPTQ 在不裁剪权重的假设下继承 Babai 算法的误差上界。基于此上界设计的避免裁剪量化方法优于原始 GPTQ。

Conclusion: 放置 GPTQ 于坚实的理论基础上，并开辟了将数十年的格算法进展应用于十亿参数模型量化算法设计的道路。

Abstract: Quantizing the weights of large language models (LLMs) from 16-bit to lower bitwidth is the de facto approach to deploy massive transformers onto more affordable accelerators. While GPTQ emerged as one of the standard methods for one-shot post-training quantization at LLM scale, its inner workings are described as a sequence of algebraic updates that obscure geometric meaning or worst-case guarantees. In this work, we show that, when executed back-to-front (from the last to first dimension) for a linear layer, GPTQ is mathematically identical to Babai's nearest plane algorithm for the classical closest vector problem (CVP) on a lattice defined by the Hessian matrix of the layer's inputs. This equivalence is based on a sophisticated mathematical argument, and has two analytical consequences: first, the GPTQ error propagation step gains an intuitive geometric interpretation; second, GPTQ inherits the error upper bound of Babai's algorithm under the assumption that no weights are clipped. Leveraging this bound, we design post-training quantization methods that avoid clipping, and outperform the original GPTQ. In addition, we provide efficient GPU inference kernels for the resulting representation. Taken together, these results place GPTQ on a firm theoretical footing and open the door to importing decades of progress in lattice algorithms towards the design of future quantization algorithms for billion-parameter models. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/GPTQ-Babai.

</details>


### [105] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将加权InfoNCE目标解释为距离几何问题，揭示了对比学习的几何本质：权重方案决定了嵌入空间是否能几何实现、是退化还是不一致。具体而言，监督分类中平衡SupCon产生正则单纯形但类不平衡破坏对称性，而Soft SupCon始终保持正则单纯形；连续标签中y-Aware CL存在几何不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对比学习（CL）生成的嵌入空间的几何性质。
