Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标解释为距离几何问题，权重方案指定了表示需要实现的目标几何结构。

Result: 1）监督分类中，SupCon和Soft SupCon都将同类样本坍缩为单一原型；2）平衡SupCon恢复经典正则单纯形几何，但类不平衡会破坏对称性，而Soft SupCon无论类别是否平衡都保持正则单纯形；3）在连续标签设置中，y-Aware CL通常无法达到熵最优，除非标签位于超球面上，暴露了欧氏标签权重与球面潜在相似性之间的不匹配；4）几何一致的选择如欧氏-欧氏加权或X-CLR允许唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 对比学习中的权重方案决定了几何可实现性、退化或不一致性，为设计对比目标提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [106] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: First work to provide non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for score matching when learning exponential families of polynomials, improving upon prior asymptotic-only results.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maximum likelihood estimation is computationally intractable for unbounded support exponential families; score matching offers computational advantages but lacked non-asymptotic theoretical understanding.

Method: Score matching estimation approach applied to exponential families of polynomials.

Result: Derived sample complexity bounds with polynomial dependence on model dimension.

Conclusion: First non-asymptotic sample complexity bounds established for score matching with polynomial exponential families, with polynomial dependence on model dimension.

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum Advantage in Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14235)
*Simranjeet Singh Dahia,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过CHSH博弈（经典上限0.75 vs Tsirelson极限0.854）和CoopNav任务，系统评估了纠缠在QMARL中的作用。结果表明纠缠（而非量子电路本身）是量子协调的关键机制，混合配置表现最佳，为量子优势提供了可证明的实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多智能体强化学习（QMARL）近年来受到广泛关注，但先前工作缺乏可证明的基准来严格区分量子优势与算法巧合，无法确认量子策略的真正效果。

Method: 采用去中心化QMARL框架，使用变分量子电路（VQC）参与者与共享纠缠态；在CHSH博弈和合作导航（CoopNav）两个任务中进行实验评估。

Result: 1. CHSH游戏中：纠缠QMARL智能体接近Tsirelson极限（0.854），明显优于经典上限（0.75）；非纠缠量子电路匹配经典基线，证明纠缠是关键。2. CoopNav任务中：无纠缠QMARL成功率约0.85，经典MAA2C约0.40，提升约2倍。3. 混合配置（量子参与者+经典评论家）优于纯量子和纯经典方案。4. 不同贝尔态对协调产生不同影响，部分贝尔态可提升性能，部分则会损害性能。

Conclusion: entanglement是量子多智能体协调的关键机制，而非量子电路本身；混合配置（量子参与者配对经典评论家）可能优于全量子或全经典方案；研究为QMARL提供了可证明的基准，有助于区分量子优势与算法巧合。

Abstract: We present an empirical evaluation of quantum entanglement in agent coordination within quantum multi agent reinforcement learning (QMARL). While QMARL has attracted growing interest recently, most prior work evaluates quantum policies without provable baselines, making it impossible to rigorously distinguish quantum advantage from algorithmic coincidence. We address this directly by evaluating a decentralized QMARL framework with variational quantum circuit (VQC) actors with shared entangled states. In the CHSH game, which has a mathematically proven classical performance ceiling of 0.75 win rate, we show that entangled QMARL agents approach the Tsirelson limit of 0.854, providing clear evidence of their quantum advantage. We show that unentangled quantum circuits match the classical baseline, confirming that entanglement and not the quantum circuit itself is the active coordination mechanism. We also explore the effect of specific entanglement structures, as some Bell states enable coordination gains while others actively harm performance. On cooperative navigation (CoopNav), QMARL without entanglement achieves $\sim2\times$ improvement in success rate over classical MAA2C ($\sim$0.85 versus $\sim$0.40), with the hybrid configuration, quantum actor paired with a classical centralised critic, outperforming both fully classical and fully quantum solutions. We present our experimental analysis and discuss future work.

</details>


### [108] [Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14379)
*JB Lanier,Nathan Monette,Pierre Baldi,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非完美信息零和博弈的稀疏奖励和长时域探索难题，提出DAGS方法，利用人类离线演示数据采样中间状态初始化RL训练，加速探索战略相关子游戏，显著降低可利用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模非完美信息竞争游戏（如StarCraft、Dota、CS）因奖励稀疏和长时域探索挑战，计算求均衡仍不可行。

Method: 提出Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS)方法：利用离线人类演示数据采样中间状态，初始化强化学习数据收集，促进对战略相关子游戏的探索。实验采用库恩扑克、Goofspiel及反例游戏的长期控制变体。

Result: 在固定计算预算下，DAGS使正则化策略梯度方法在更具挑战性的游戏中实现更低的可利用性。

Conclusion: DAGS augmentation可引入偏差，但可通过多任务观察标志缓解；团队发布了新的基准测试环境，显著增加现有OpenSpiel游戏的探索挑战和状态规模。

Abstract: Finding approximate equilibria for large-scale imperfect-information competitive games such as StarCraft, Dota, and CounterStrike remains computationally infeasible due to sparse rewards and challenging exploration over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent starting-state sampling strategy designed to substantially accelerate online exploration in regularized policy-gradient game methods for two-player zero-sum (2p0s) games. Motivated by an assumption that offline demonstrations from skilled humans can provide good coverage of high-level strategies relevant to equilibrium play, we propose the initialization of reinforcement learning data collection at intermediate states sampled from offline data to facilitate exploration of strategically relevant subgames. Referring to this method as Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS), we perform experiments using synthetic datasets and analytically tractable, long-horizon control variants of two-player Kuhn Poker, Goofspiel, and a counterexample game designed to penalize biased beliefs over hidden information. Under fixed computational budgets, DAGS enables regularized policy gradient methods to achieve lower exploitability in games with significantly more challenging exploration. We show that augmenting starting state distributions when solving imperfect information games can lead to biased equilibria, and we provide a straightforward mitigation to this in the form of multi-task observation flags. Finally, we release a new set of benchmark environments that drastically increase exploration challenges and state counts in existing OpenSpiel games while keeping exploitability measurements analytically tractable.

</details>


### [109] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，一种利用近期动作历史构建的简单时序先验，替代标准高斯噪声用于扩散/流动匹配的生成式机器人策略。实验证明该方法在行为克隆和先验空间强化学习中均能持续提升任务成功率，核心原因是生成路径变得更直（类似最优传输耦合特性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略（基于扩散/流动匹配）已成为视觉运动机器人控制的主流范式，但此前源分布设计未被充分探索。作者假设源分布的选择会影响生成质量和路径特性。

Method: 用WarmPrior（从近期动作历史构建的时序接地先验）替代标准高斯噪声作为扩散/流动匹配的源分布。核心改进机制在于使概率路径更直（类似Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果）。实验验证涵盖行为克隆和先验空间强化学习两种范式。

Result: 在机器人操作任务中，持续提升成功率；强化学习中同时改善样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: WarmPrior通过简单替换噪声源分布即可持续提升机器人操作任务的成功率，且在行为克隆和先验空间强化学习中均有效，揭示了源分布设计是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但尚未充分探索的设计维度。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个全面的能量核算框架，用于测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本。研究通过对基于logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调两种方法进行系统性能耗测量，揭示了此前被忽视的教师侧能耗，构建了能量-质量Pareto前沿，并推导出能量约束下的实用设计规则，同时开源了测量工具和核算协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署激增导致GPU需求和数据中心规模扩张，引发电力消耗、电网压力和AI工作负载影响方面的担忧。蒸馏虽被宣传为获取更便宜、更高效模型的有效途径，但这些说法通常未计入完整端到端能耗，包括教师端的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等关键工作负载成本。

Method: 通过详细阶段式追踪GPU设备功耗，构建了涵盖教师端数据生成、logit缓存和评估等完整蒸馏管道的能耗测量框架；对两种常见蒸馏方法（基于logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调）进行系统性能耗与排放测量，并构建能量-质量Pareto前沿曲线。

Result: 实验成功分离并记录了各阶段的实证能耗数据，构建了能量-质量Pareto前沿，揭示了此前被忽视的教师侧成本，并从中推导出在能量和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了蒸馏方法的真实能耗成本，并提供了实用的设计规则和开源工具，使后续研究能够在能量约束下选择最优的蒸馏方法和超参数配置。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [111] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一款专为表格数据设计的基础模型，通过合成数据预训练和测试时计算扩展，在TabArena等基准测试中实现领先性能，支持100万行数据规模，速度提升20倍，并扩展到时间序列、关系型和表格-文本等多模态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据支撑着大多数高价值预测问题，TabPFN虽已引领该领域的Foundation Model革命，但需扩展到更大规模数据集（100万训练行）并显著降低训练和推理时间以满足实际应用需求。

Method: TabPFN-3完全基于先验分布生成的合成数据进行预训练，引入了测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）机制，并采用减少KV缓存和行分块技术实现规模化。TabPFN-3-Plus通过Thinking模式进一步利用测试时计算扩展能力。

Result: 在TabArena标准基准测试中，TabPFN-3单次前向传播显著超越所有其他模型（包括调优和集成基线），并在速度/性能前沿上实现帕累托支配。TabPFN-3-Plus在TabArena上领先所有非TabPFN模型超过200 Elo（在最大数据子集上达420 Elo），比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍且性能更优。在RelBenchV1上实现关系型数据新SOTA，在TabSTAR上通过TabPFN-3-Plus达到表格-文本数据SOTA。时间序列专用模型TabPFN-TS-3在fev-bench上排名第二。SHAP值计算速度提升最高达120倍，相比TabPFN-2.5速度提升高达20倍，可在单个H100上扩展至100万行数据。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3显著推动了表格预测的前沿发展，在表格、时间序列、关系型和表格-文本数据上均带来实质性改进，同时速度提升高达20倍。该模型成功将最先进性能扩展到100万行训练数据规模，并实现了高效的可扩展推理能力。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [112] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明离散扩散模型的去噪目标会诱导粗粒度数据支撑信息与细粒度频率信息之间的层级结构。均匀扩散表现为有效性改善/保持/恶化的三元分类，而吸收扩散将主导质量集中于有效性改善的移动。实验证实支撑结构定位早于频率排序习得，为离散扩散模型的学习组织机制提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中越来越具竞争力，但人们尚不清楚其去噪目标如何组织学习过程。虽然这些目标针对完整数据分布，但需要理解不同层次信息（粗粒度的支撑信息 vs 细粒度的频率信息）是如何被学习的。

Method: 理论证明与实验验证相结合。理论方面，在小噪声极限下对均匀扩散和吸收扩散的单令牌反向编辑进行了数学分析。实验方面，使用掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务进行验证。

Result: 证明在最终去噪步骤的小噪声区域，每个单令牌反向编辑分解为两个层次：主导尺度由是否移向数据支撑（如语法有效的句子）决定；更细的系数决定同一尺度内的相对概率。研究发现支撑定位比尺度内频率排序更早习得，均匀扩散和吸收扩散之间存在可预测的速率差异。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的学习遵循从数据支持（支撑结构）到数据频率的层级顺序。不同扩散机制（均匀 vs 吸收）展现出不同的行为模式：均匀扩散呈现三元分类（有效性改善/保持/恶化），而吸收扩散将主导质量集中在有效性改善的移动上。这一发现为理解和设计离散扩散语言模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [113] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dywave是首个针对IoT传感信号设计的动态tokenization框架，通过小波分层分解识别语义事件边界，实现自适应压缩，在保持精度的同时大幅提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT传感信号具有内在的非平稳性与多尺度特性，传统token化方法难以有效捕捉其时序结构与物理事件的语义边界，导致信息冗余与表示低效，制约了下游智能应用的性能与计算效率。

Method: 提出基于小波的分层分解框架，利用小波变换对非平稳、多尺度信号进行多分辨率分解；设计语义事件驱动的动态边界识别算法，定位隐式时间分界线；采用自适应压缩策略，在保留时间一致性的前提下压缩冗余区间；最终生成与内在时序结构对齐的紧凑token表示。

Result: 在5个真实IoT数据集上，Dywave在活动识别、压力评估、目标检测三项任务中准确率提升最高达12%；输入token长度缩减最高达75%，显著降低了计算开销；同时展现了更强的领域迁移鲁棒性与长度泛化能力。

Conclusion: Dywave通过小波分解与自适应压缩的联合设计，在IoT传感信号领域实现了token化范式的突破。实验验证涵盖5个真实数据集、3类应用场景，证明其兼具精度增益与效率优势，并展现了良好的跨域鲁棒性，为IoT智能感知的轻量化建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [114] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中密集数据重用导致的过拟合问题，提出R2R2正则化方法，通过非中心化目标设计解决SPL框架中零中心化与谱特性的冲突，可在高UTD比（20）下为TD7带来约22%性能提升，并能与其他先进算法正交集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的现实机器人领域，强化学习需要密集重用数据以提高效率，但这会引发过拟合问题。现有工作主要关注评论家（critic）的偏差问题，而Self-Predictive Learning（SPL）在高更新数据比（UTD）下表示层面的不稳定性问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出R2R2（Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction）方法，包括：1）从理论上识别标准零中心化与SPL谱特性之间的冲突；2）设计非中心化目标函数以减少表示层面的冗余和过拟合；3）将SPL模块集成到非原生算法SimbaV2中形成SimbaV2-SPL，验证R2R2的正交性。

Result: R2R2有效缓解了高UTD场景下的过拟合问题：在UTD=20时，TD7性能提升约22%；SimbaV2-SPL本身也成为新的最优方法，而R2R2在其基础上进一步带来额外增益，验证了其正交性优势。

Conclusion: R2R2提出了一种针对SPL框架的正则化方法，通过非中心化目标设计解决了标准零中心化与SPL谱特性之间的冲突，理论上具有严谨性，实验上在11个连续控制任务中验证了其有效性（UTD=20时TD7提升约22%），并可与现有算法正交集成。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [115] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SP-KV通过轻量级utility predictor动态预测KV对的未来效用，实现KV缓存的细粒度动态剪枝，在保持模型性能的同时将缓存压缩3-10倍，并揭示了可用于混合注意力架构设计的层级和头级别稀疏性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-sequence processing under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms is constrained by Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth in transformer architectures.

Method: Jointly train LLM and a lightweight utility predictor end-to-end via next-token prediction loss; use local window for recent KVs while selectively caching older pairs based on predicted utility threshold; adapted from pretrained checkpoints.

Result: Achieves 3-10x KV cache size reduction with little to no degradation in validation loss or downstream task performance; longer sequences tend to be more compressible.

Conclusion: SP-KV enables dynamic KV cache compression (3-10x reduction) with minimal performance degradation; the method reveals layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns useful for designing hybrid local-global attention architectures.

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold. The LLM and the utility predictor are trained jointly end-to-end exclusively through next-token prediction loss, and are adapted from pretrained LLM checkpoints.
  Rather than enforcing a fixed compression ratio, SP-KV performs dynamic sparsification: the mechanism adapts to the input and typically reduces the KV cache size by a factor of $3$ to $10\times$, longer sequences often being more compressible. This leads to vast improvements in memory usage and decoding speed, with little to no degradation of validation loss nor performance on a broad set of downstream tasks. Beyond serving as an effective KV-cache reduction mechanism, our method reveals structured layer- and head-specific sparsity patterns that we can use to guide the design of hybrid local-global attention architectures.

</details>


### [116] [Reliability-Gated Source Anchoring for Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14063)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Weicong Chen,Sabyasachi Sahoo,Sreehari Sankar,Biyao Zhang,Mohsen Harir,Shouren Wang,Osama Zafar,Christian Gagné,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CTTA中源退化导致的"盲目锚定"问题，提出基于归一化熵的可靠性门控方法RMemSafe，动态衰减源耦合项，在严重源退化下实现优雅降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源锚定CTTA方法在源模型严重退化（如ResNet-50在CCC-Hard上仅1.3%准确率）时仍应用固定锚定强度，导致性能崩溃，作者将此定义为"盲目锚定"问题，亟需根据源可靠性动态调整锚定强度。

Method: RMemSafe使用源模型的归一化预测熵作为可靠性门控信号，当后验分布趋近均匀时关闭门控，移除目标函数中的源耦合项（源锚定和一致性过滤器），退化为仅包含ROID基础损失和边缘校准的源无关备选方案。

Result: 结合ASR后，RMemSafe在9个匹配分割持续腐蚀单元中的8个取得最低错误率，在全部9个单元中均为最佳重置方法；在ResNet-50上提升1.05pp，ViT-B/16上提升0.48pp；在受控源退化实验中，危害斜率比ROID+ASR浅1.13倍。

Conclusion: RMemSafe通过熵门机制实现了源模型可靠性与适应强度的动态耦合，在严重的源退化场景下仍能保持有效适应，显著优于固定锚定强度的基线方法，证明了自适应锚定对持续测试时适应的必要性。

Abstract: Continual test-time adaptation (CTTA) updates a pretrained model online on an unlabeled, non-stationary stream while anchoring it to a frozen source checkpoint. This anchor is useful only when the source remains reliable. On CCC-Hard, however, a ResNet-50 source falls to approximately $1.3\%$ top-$1$ accuracy, while existing source-anchored CTTA methods continue applying the same anchor strength. We call this failure mode blind anchoring and propose RMemSafe, a reliability-gated extension of ROID that uses the frozen source's normalized predictive entropy to attenuate all explicit source-coupled uses in the objective. When the source posterior approaches uniformity, the gate closes: the source anchor and agreement filter vanish, and the objective reduces to a source-agnostic fallback comprising ROID's base losses plus marginal calibration. Combined with ASR, RMemSafe achieves the lowest error on $8$ of $9$ matched-split continual-corruption cells and is the best reset-based method on all $9$, improving ROID+ASR by $1.05$~pp on ResNet-50 and $0.48$~pp on ViT-B/16. A controlled source-degradation sweep shows a $1.13{\times}$ shallower harm slope than ROID+ASR, consistent with the graceful-decay prediction. The entropy gate detects high-entropy source collapse, not confidently wrong low-entropy sources; this scope is explicitly evaluated and discussed.

</details>


### [117] [SurF: A Generative Model for Multivariate Irregular Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14069)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Tejas Balaji,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SurF——一种利用时间重缩放定理的可学习双射特性进行异构事件流生成的模型，通过三种高效的累计强度参数化和Transformer编码器实现跨数据集预训练，在六个基准上达到或接近最优性能，并向异步事件流基础模型迈出重要一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则采样的多元事件流是生成式建模中的一个难点领域。现有方法存在局限性：基于分词的方法在事件间隔跨数量级变化时失效；神经时间点过程受限于窗口级数值求积的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出SurF模型，利用时间重缩放定理(TRT)作为事件序列与独立同分布单位速率指数噪声之间的可学习双射；三种可扩展到长序列的累计强度参数化方法；基于Transformer的编码器用于多数据集预训练。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准数据集上，SurF在Earthquake、Retweet和Taobao上取得最佳时间RMSE，在其余三个数据集上与最强专家模型相当。在严格的留一协议下，检查点模型在5/6数据集上超越所有经典和神经自回归基线，在Amazon和Earthquake上超越所有基线。

Conclusion: SurF代表了向异步事件流基础模型迈出的初始一步，在多数据集场景下展现出良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled multivariate event streams remain a stubbornly difficult modality for generative modeling: tokenization-based approaches break down when inter-event intervals vary by orders of magnitude, and neural temporal point processes are bottlenecked by window-level numerical quadrature. We (i) propose SurF, a generative model that uses the Time Rescaling Theorem (TRT) as a learnable bijection between event sequences and i.i.d.\ unit-rate exponential noise, enabling a single model to be trained across heterogeneous event-stream datasets; (ii) three efficient parameterizations of the cumulative intensity that scale to long sequences; and (iii) a Transformer-based encoder for multi-dataset pretraining. On six real-world benchmarks, SurF achieves the best reported time RMSE on Earthquake, Retweet, and Taobao, and is within trial-level noise of the strongest specialist on the remaining three. Under a strict leave-one-out protocol, the held-out checkpoint beats every classical and neural-autoregressive baseline on 5/6 datasets and beats every baseline on Amazon and Earthquake, an initial step toward foundation models over asynchronous event streams.

</details>


### [118] [Fair and Calibrated Toxicity Detection with Robust Training and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14074)
*Mokshit Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了毒性分类公平性的三轴评估框架（排名、校准、弃权），通过比较多种训练方法和后处理机制的组合效果，发现校准差异是隐藏的公平性违反、训练干预会重塑而非消除差距、后处理方法继承训练失败模式，且弃权本身也存在不公平问题。研究强调了多轴公平性评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对公平性干预的评估存在碎片化问题：训练时干预和事后安全机制的效果无法独立评估，因为前者决定了后者的有效性；需要建立一个整合排名、校准和弃权三个维度的多轴公平性框架。

Method: 比较ERM、实例级重加权、Group DRO三种训练方法，结合温度缩放、基于置信度的弃权和身份阈值优化三种后处理机制；使用子群AUC、BPSN/BNSP AUC、误差差距和自助法置信区间（n=1000）的子群级ECE进行评估。

Result: （1）ERM聚合校准近乎完美（0.013）但各身份子群存在显著校准偏差（+0.029至+0.134）；（2）训练干预重塑而非消除差距：重加权ERM改善排名但加剧校准差距（+0.232），Group DRO消除校准差距但导致全局校准错误（0.118）；（3）后处理方法继承训练失败模式：温度缩放因非均匀误校准而失效，弃权在ERM下有效但在DRO下因风险-覆盖曲线上升而失效；（4）弃权本身存在不公平：基于置信度的推迟对背景内容帮助远大于身份提及内容。

Conclusion: 公平性分类需要多轴评估框架；不同方法在聚合排名指标上可能相似，但在决定实际伤害的失败模式上存在显著差异；现有的训练干预和后处理方法都不能独立解决所有公平性问题。

Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

</details>


### [119] [Mini-JEPA Foundation Model Fleet Enables Agentic Hydrologic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14120)
*Mashrekur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We propose Mini-JEPAs — a fleet of five 22M-parameter sensor-specialized joint embedding predictive architecture models (Sentinel-2 optical, Sentinel-1 SAR, MODIS thermal, multi-temporal phenology, topography-soil) with a routing LLM that dynamically selects appropriate sensors for specialized environmental questions. The approach achieves high reconstruction accuracy (R² up to 0.97), outperforms the generalist AlphaEarth baseline on physics-matched queries, and remains computationally affordable for local deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Planetary-scale generalist models like Google AlphaEarth handle broad characterization but compromise on specialized hydrologic signals and are inaccessible, expensive, and compute-intensive; there is a need for specialized yet efficient models for sensor-specific environmental reasoning tasks.

Method: Pretrained five 22M-parameter Mini-JEPAs with identical Vision Transformer backbone, JEPA recipe, and 64-d output space; built a router LLM for sensor selection; evaluated through cross-validated R², manifold geometry analysis, LLM-as-Judge paired comparison, and incremental R² improvement over AlphaEarth baseline.

Result: Five sensor-specialized Mini-JEPAs achieved R² up to 0.97 for elevation/temperature and 0.81 for precipitation; the five manifolds have distinct geometric structures (participation ratios 8.9-20.2, intrinsic dimensionalities 2.3-9.0); joint models add incremental predictive value beyond AlphaEarth alone (ΔR² up to 0.031); router LLM achieves perfect hit rate for sensor selection; dual retrieval with routing significantly outperforms AlphaEarth alone on physics-matched questions.

Conclusion: Mini-JEPA fleet with routing mechanism achieves perfect sensor selection and outperforms AlphaEarth alone on physics-matched questions (Cohen's d=1.10, p=0.031), while being operationalizable with modest compute for local hydrologic intelligence.

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models compress multispectral observations into dense embeddings increasingly used in natural-language environmental reasoning systems. A single planetary-scale model, e.g. Google AlphaEarth, handles broad characterization well but may compromise on specialized hydrologic signals. Such generalist models are also often inaccessible, expensive, and require large-scale compute. We propose Mini-JEPAs: a fleet of small sensor-specialized Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) foundation models consulted by a routing agent for specialized questions. We pretrained five 22M-parameter Mini-JEPAs sharing an identical Vision Transformer backbone, JEPA recipe, and 64-d output space, using Sentinel-2 optical, Sentinel-1 SAR, MODIS thermal, multi-temporal Sentinel-2 phenology, and a topography-soil stack. Each Mini-JEPA reconstructs the variable matched to its sensor, with cross-validated $R^2$ reaching 0.97 for elevation, 0.97 for temperature, and 0.81 for precipitation. The five manifolds differ in geometric structure, with global participation ratios from 8.9 to 20.2 and local intrinsic dimensionalities from 2.3 to 9.0. Joint topography-soil and phenology models add predictive value beyond AlphaEarth alone for soil moisture, aridity, and precipitation ($ΔR^2$ up to 0.031). A router LLM reads per-modality references and selects appropriate sensors with a perfect hit rate over a curated question set. In paired LLM-as-Judge evaluation, dual retrieval over AlphaEarth and the routed fleet outperforms AlphaEarth alone on physics-matched questions (Cohen's $d = 1.10$, $p = 0.031$). Locally-trained Mini-JEPAs can be operationalized for hydrologic intelligence with modest compute.

</details>


### [120] [Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Calling Agents in Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14126)
*Marius S. Knorr,Robert Müller,Jan P. Bremer,Nils Schweingruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对FHIR医疗图谱推理任务，提出了基于强化学习后训练的多轮CodeAct智能体方案，在FHIR-AgentBench上实现了50%到77%的性能提升，仅需使用Qwen3-8B这样的小型模型即可超越GPT-4o mini。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHIR是医疗数据互操作的主流标准，医疗记录形成有向资源图谱。回答临床问题需要跨多资源类型的多步推理、筛选和聚合。即使是配备了工具的LLM智能体也常常选择错误的资源或违反遍历约束，这表明现有方法在处理结构化医疗数据时存在明显不足。

Method: 研究在FHIR-AgentBench基准上展开，将FHIR推理建模为在可查询结构图上的序列决策问题；实现了多轮CodeAct智能体；使用自定义工具和RL后训练框架进行强化学习训练；引入LLM Judge提供基于执行结果的奖励信号。

Result: RL后训练显著提升了性能，同时保证了数据完整性约束。相比o4-mini基线的50%正确率，使用更小更便宜的Qwen3-8B模型通过RL后训练达到了77%的正确率。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个端到端的RL后训练流水线，包括环境构建、测试框架构建、模型训练和自定义评估，能够可靠地提升模型在结构化临床图谱上的多轮推理能力。

Abstract: Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) is the dominant standard for interoperable exchange of healthcare data. In FHIR, electronic health records form a directed graph of resources. Answering clinically meaningful questions over FHIR requires agents to perform multi-step reasoning, filtering, and aggregation across multiple resource types. Prior work shows that even tool-augmented LLM agents (retrieval, code execution, multi-turn planning) often select the wrong resources or violate traversal constraints. We study this problem in the context of FHIR-AgentBench, a benchmark for realistic question answering over real-world hospital data, and frame reasoning on FHIR as a sequential decision-making problem over a queryable structured graph. We implement a multi-turn CodeAct agent and post-train it with reinforcement learning using a custom harness and tools. A LLM Judge provides execution-grounded rewards. Compared to prompt-based, closed-model baselines, RL post-training improves performance while enforcing data-integrity constraints. Empirically, our approach improves answer correctness from 50% (o4-mini) to 77% on FHIR-AgentBench using a smaller and cheaper Qwen3-8B model. We present an end-to-end post-training pipeline (environment building, harness construction, model training and custom evaluation) that reliably improves multi-turn reasoning over structured clinical graphs.

</details>


### [121] [bde: A Python Package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles via MILE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14146)
*Vyron Arvanitis,Angelos Aslanidis,Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: bde is a user-friendly Python package enabling Bayesian deep ensembles for tabular data using JAX-based MILE method with scikit-learn compatibility.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bayesian deep ensembles offer principled uncertainty quantification but lack user-friendly tools, especially for tabular data applications.

Method: Built on JAX-based implementation of Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE) with scikit-learn compatible estimators for sampling-based inference.

Result: Provides estimators for fast training, efficient MCMC sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

Conclusion: bde provides an accessible, efficient, and practical solution for implementing Bayesian deep ensembles on tabular data, lowering the barrier to uncertainty quantification in machine learning workflows.

Abstract: bde is a user-friendly Python package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles with a particular focus on tabular data. Built on an efficient JAX implementation of the sampling-based inference method Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE), it provides scikit-learn compatible estimators for fast training, efficient Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Uncovering Trajectory and Topological Signatures in Multimodal Pediatric Sleep Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14156)
*Scott Ye,Harlin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了儿科睡眠分析中多模态掩码自编码器嵌入的潜在结构，通过整合几何/拓扑特征（PHATE坐标、持续同调、运动描述符）和电子病历信息，显著提升了低信噪比二分类任务的预测性能与模型校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中展现潜力，但其多模态嵌入的潜在结构尚不明确；需探究如何有效利用嵌入中的诊断信息。

Method: 使用简单线性和MLP模型（注重可解释性而非SOTA性能）进行预测；通过PHATE衍生的每epoch坐标、全夜运动描述符、嵌入云持续同调摘要及EHR特征增强嵌入；采用AUPRC、Brier分数和ECE评估指标。

Result: 四类二分类任务的AUPRC均有显著提升（去饱和0.26→0.34，EEG唤醒0.31→0.48，低通气0.09→0.22，呼吸暂停0.05→0.14）；完整融合模型在所有任务上获得最佳校准。

Conclusion: 几何/拓扑特征和EHR在多模态嵌入之外提供了互补且可解释的信号，提升了模型的校准能力和对极端类别不平衡的鲁棒性；更复杂的晚期融合模型通常表现更优。

Abstract: While generative models have shown promise in pediatric sleep analysis, the latent structure of their multimodal embeddings remains poorly understood. This work investigates session-wide diagnostic information contained in the sequences of 30-second pediatric PSG epochs embedded by a multimodal masked autoencoder. We test whether augmenting embeddings with PHATE-derived per-epoch coordinates and whole-night movement descriptors, persistent homology summaries of the embedding cloud, and EHR yields task-relevant signals. Simple linear and MLP models, chosen for interpretability rather than state-of-the-art performance, show that geometric, topological, and clinical features each provide complementary gains. For binary predictions, feature importance is task-dependent, and more expressive late-fusion models generally perform better, with AUPRC improving from 0.26 to 0.34 for desaturation, 0.31 to 0.48 for EEG arousal, 0.09 to 0.22 for hypopnea, and 0.05 to 0.14 for apnea. We also report Brier score and Expected Calibration Error, where the full fusion model yields the best calibration across all four binary tasks. Our study reveals that latent geometry/topology and EHR offer complementary, interpretable signals beyond embeddings, improving calibration and robustness under extreme imbalance.

</details>


### [123] [LLMs Know When They Know, but Do Not Act on It: A Metacognitive Harness for Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14186)
*Qi Cao,Yufan Wang,Peijia Qin,Shuhao Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种元认知控制框架，利用LLM的自我监控信号（FOK/JOL）主动控制推理过程，在无需参数更新的情况下显著提升固定模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通常展现出自我监控信号（如解决前预估成功率、解决后判断答案正确性），但这些信号通常孤立测量或 elicitation，未被用于控制推理过程。

Method: 受Nelson--Narens理论启发，提出元认知控制框架，将监控与推理分离。流程包括：解决问题前输出"知道感"(FOK)信号，每次尝试后输出"学习判断"(JOL)信号，并利用这些信号作为控制接口决定何时信任当前解、何时使用简洁元认知反馈重试、何时将多次尝试交给最终聚合器。

Result: 在文本、代码和多模态推理基准测试中，无需参数更新或基准特定微调，即可将固定Claude Sonnet-4.6的准确率从48.3%提升至56.9%，并在HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6和R-Bench-V三个主要评估设置上超越最强榜单条目。

Conclusion: 强LLM可能已经具备有用的元认知能力，但需要在推理过程中通过显式控制机制来发挥这些能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often expose useful signals of self-monitoring: before solving a problem, they can estimate whether they are likely to succeed, and after solving it, they can judge whether their answer is likely to be correct. However, these signals are typically measured or elicited in isolation, rather than used to control inference. In this work, we ask whether LLMs possess latent metacognitive ability that can be turned into effective test-time control. Inspired by the Nelson--Narens theory from cognitive psychology, we propose a metacognitive harness that separates monitoring from reasoning. For each problem, the model first reports a pre-solve feeling-of-knowing (FOK) signal; after each solve attempt, it reports a post-solve judgment-of-learning (JOL) signal. Rather than treating these signals as passive confidence estimates, the harness turns them into an explicit control interface for reasoning: it decides when to trust the current solution, when to retry with compact metacognitive feedback, and when to pass multiple attempts to a final aggregator. Across text, code, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, our harness substantially improves a fixed Claude Sonnet-4.6 base model without parameter updates or benchmark-specific fine-tuning. On the evaluated public benchmark snapshots, it raises pooled accuracy from 48.3 to 56.9 and exceeds the strongest listed leaderboard entries on the three primary evaluation settings: HLE-Verified, LiveCodeBench v6, and R-Bench-V. These results suggest that strong LLMs may already possess useful metacognitive ability, but require an explicit control harness to act on it during reasoning.

</details>


### [124] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

</details>


### [125] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT方法，通过仅在预填充阶段应用适配器来解决个性化LLM服务中的吞吐量瓶颈问题。该方法在vLLM上实现，能在服务512个适配器时提升1.9倍吞吐量，同时在RL任务中保持与标准PEFT相当的性能，为大规模个性化LLM服务提供了更优的准确率-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化LLM服务面临吞吐量瓶颈：尽管PEFT方法能高效地进行大规模个性化微调，但在实际服务时，即使使用专用内核和内存管理技术，多用户PEFT服务仍会损害吞吐量。原因在于预填充（批量处理）和解码（自回归生成）之间存在理论与实践上的不匹配——后者在服务多个适配器时吞吐量极低。现有方法优化目标偏离实际需求，应针对服务吞吐量而非参数数量进行优化。

Method: 提出PreFT（仅预填充微调）方法，仅在预填充阶段应用适配器，之后丢弃。在vLLM推理引擎上实现了LoRA和ReFT两种PEFT的预填充-only版本。

Result: 在Serving 512个适配器时实现1.9倍吞吐量提升；SFT任务中PreFT评估损失略高，但可通过增加rank补偿且几乎无吞吐量损失；RL任务中PreFT与标准PEFT几乎持平。

Conclusion: PreFT 提供了一种更优越的准确率-吞吐量权衡方案，通过仅在预填充阶段应用适配器来显著提升服务效率，同时保持与标准PEFT相当的任务性能，特别是在强化学习任务中几乎达到同等水平。这为大规模个性化LLM服务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [126] [Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220)
*Tianle Zhong,Neiwen Ling,Yifan Pi,Zijun Wei,Tianshu Yu,Geoffrey Fox,Peng Wu,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM RL系统中的训练-推理不匹配(TIM)问题。通过设计零不匹配的诊断环境(VeXact)，发现即使在理想条件下，token级别的微小数值分歧也能独立导致训练崩溃。TIM不仅引入噪声，而是系统级别的扰动，应作为分析LLM RL稳定性的首要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM RL系统将 rollout 生成与策略优化分离，这两个阶段在相同模型权重下应对同一序列产生相同的token概率。但实现差异会导致对同一序列赋予不同的值，引发训练-推理不匹配(TIM)。TIM难以检查，因为它与离策略漂移和常见稳定机制纠缠在一起。

Method: 通过零不匹配的诊断环境(VeXact)隔离TIM效应，发现微小的token级数值分歧即可独立导致训练崩溃；进一步识别出一组可缓解TIM的补救措施。

Result: 微小的token级数值分歧可以独立地导致训练崩溃；TIM改变了有效的优化问题；存在可缓解TIM的补救措施。

Conclusion: TIM应该被视为分析LLM RL稳定性的首要因素，而非良性的数值噪声。

Abstract: Modern LLM RL systems separate rollout generation from policy optimization. These two stages are expected to produce token probabilities that match exactly. However, implementation differences can make them assign different values to the same sequence under the same model weights, inducing Training-Inference Mismatch (TIM). TIM is difficult to inspect because it is entangled with off-policy drift and common stabilization mechanisms. In this work, we isolate TIM in a zero-mismatch diagnostic setting (VeXact), and show that small token-level numerical disagreements can independently cause training collapse. We further show that TIM changes the effective optimization problem, and identify a set of remedies that could mitigate TIM. Our results suggest that TIM is not benign numerical noise, but a systems-level perturbation that should be treated as a first-order factor in analyzing LLM RL stability.

</details>


### [127] [AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231)
*Hanxun Huang,Qizhou Wang,Xingjun Ma,Cihang Xie,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AudioMosaic是一种基于对比学习的音频编码器，通过结构化时频掩码策略解决音频自监督学习中对比方法的内存和设计难题，在多个基准上取得最优性能，并可迁移至音频-语言多模态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频自监督学习主要依赖生成式重建目标，对比学习方法因音频增强设计困难和对比学习对大批量训练的依赖而较少被探索。

Method: AudioMosaic采用对比学习框架，通过对频谱图patch应用结构化时频掩码策略构建正样本对，降低了内存占用，支持大批量高效训练。

Result: AudioMosaic学习到更具判别性的 utterance 级表示，在数据集、领域和声学条件间展现出强迁移能力；在多种音频基准测试的线性探测和微调设置下均达到最优性能；且可有效集成到音频-语言模型中提升多模态任务表现。

Conclusion: AudioMosaic通过结构化掩码策略实现了高效的对比学习预训练，在多个音频基准测试上达到最优性能，且可有效迁移至音频-语言多模态任务，验证了对比学习方法在通用音频理解中的可行性。

Abstract: Audio self-supervised learning (SSL) aims to learn general-purpose representations from large-scale unlabeled audio data. While recent advances have been driven mainly by generative reconstruction objectives, contrastive approaches remain less explored, partly due to the difficulty of designing effective audio augmentations and the large batch sizes required for contrastive pre-training. We introduce \textbf{AudioMosaic}, a contrastive learning-based audio encoder for general audio understanding. During pre-training, AudioMosaic constructs positive pairs by applying structured time-frequency masking to spectrogram patches, which reduces memory usage and enables efficient large-batch training. Compared with generative approaches, the AudioMosaic encoder learns more discriminative utterance-level representations that demonstrate strong transferability across datasets, domains, and acoustic conditions. Extensive experiments show that AudioMosaic achieves state-of-the-art performance on several standard audio benchmarks under both linear probing and fine-tuning. We further show that integrating the pretrained AudioMosaic encoder into audio-language models improves performance on audio-language tasks. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [128] [Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236)
*Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann,Juan Kaplan,Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo,Juan Wisznia,Luciano del Corro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LLM排序重框架为噪声比较的主动学习问题，引入随机方向预言机将位置偏差转换为零均值噪声，在保持单次LLM调用效率的同时实现了噪声鲁棒的排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRP方法存在三个核心问题：LLM判断存在噪声干扰、排序结果对输入顺序敏感、以及判断可能存在不可传递性。传统排序算法的全排列恢复假设与实际场景不匹配，且截断排序无法产生可靠top-K结果。

Method: 将PRP重框架化为噪声比较的主动学习问题，并引入随机方向预言机（randomized-direction oracle），每次比较仅需单次LLM调用，将系统性位置偏差转换为零均值噪声，从而实现无偏聚合排序。

Result: 在调用受限场景下，主动排序器可作为直接替代方案，显著提升NDCG@10指标；随机方向预言机实现无偏聚合排序，同时避免了双向调用的成本开销。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将系统性的位置偏差转换为零均值噪声，实现了无偏的聚合排序，同时避免了双向调用的成本。在调用受限场景下，主动排序器可作为直接替代方案，显著提升NDCG@10指标。

Abstract: Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) elicits pairwise preference judgments from an LLM, which are then aggregated into a ranking, usually via classical sorting algorithms. However, judgments are noisy, order-sensitive, and sometimes intransitive, so sorting assumptions do not match the setting. Because sorting aims to recover a full permutation, truncating it to meet a call budget does not produce a dependable top-K. We thus reframe PRP reranking as active learning from noisy pairwise comparisons and show that active rankers are drop-in replacements that improve NDCG@10 per call in the call-constrained regime. Our noise-robust framework also introduces a randomized-direction oracle that uses a single LLM call per pair. This approach converts systematic position bias into zero-mean noise, enabling unbiased aggregate ranking without the cost of bidirectional calls.

</details>


### [129] [Paraphrasing Attack Resilience of Various AI-Generated Text Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14240)
*Andrii Shportko,Inessa Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了AI文本检测领域"性能与韧性"的二元困境：当前最强检测方法反而最脆弱，表明现有SOTA技术的可靠性评估需要重新审视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs大规模涌现导致plagiarism和虚假信息传播问题加剧，加之AI检测规避工具兴起，对可靠的机器生成文本检测技术需求日益迫切。

Method: 研究评估了三种机器生成文本检测方法（微调RoBERTa、Binoculars、文本特征分析）及其与随机森林分类器的集成方案在 paraphrase 攻击下的韧性表现。

Result: Binoculars集成方法取得最强检测性能，但在攻击中遭受最显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的AI文本检测技术存在"性能-韧性"权衡困境：表现最佳的检测方法（如Binoculars集成方法）在面对 paraphrase 攻击时反而最为脆弱，挑战了对现有技术的可靠性认知。

Abstract: The recent large-scale emergence of LLMs has left an open space for dealing with their consequences, such as plagiarism or the spread of false information on the Internet. Coupling this with the rise of AI detector bypassing tools, reliable machine-generated text detection is in increasingly high demand. We investigate the paraphrasing attack resilience of various machine-generated text detection methods, evaluating three approaches: fine-tuned RoBERTa, Binoculars, and text feature analysis, along with their ensembles using Random Forest classifiers. We discovered that Binoculars-inclusive ensembles yield the strongest results, but they also suffer the most significant losses during attacks. In this paper, we present the dichotomy of performance versus resilience in the world of AI text detection, which complicates the current perception of reliability among state-of-the-art techniques.

</details>


### [130] [Latency-Quality Routing for Functionally Equivalent Tools in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14241)
*Kexin Chu,Dawei Xiang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LQM-ContextRoute，一种用于同功能工具提供商路由的上下文赌博机路由器。核心创新是将延迟视为服务容量而非加性惩罚项，结合容量感知评分、查询特定质量估计和LLM-as-judge反馈实现运行时自适应。在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，尤其在高异质性场景下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型LLM智能体经常需要访问功能等效但性能特征（延迟、可靠性、答案质量）差异显著的多个提供商。在部署时往往缺乏金标准标签，现有的加性奖励方法在提供商异质性高时会出现"奖励崩溃"问题，无法有效平衡延迟与质量。

Method: LQM-ContextRoute采用上下文赌博机框架进行在线适配。核心设计是"延迟-质量匹配"：按服务周期内的预期答案质量对提供商排序，而非将低延迟作为加性奖励的抵消项。方法融合了容量感知评分（将延迟建模为服务容量约束）、查询特定质量估计（基于上下文特征预测提供商表现）以及LLM-as-judge反馈（用于无标签环境下的在线学习）。

Result: 主网页搜索基准上，LQM-ContextRoute的F1比SW-UCB提升+2.18pp；在高异质性StrategyQA设置中，避免了加性奖励崩溃，准确率比SW-UCB提升达+18pp；在异质性检索器池中，NDCG比SW-UCB提升+2.91至+3.22pp。

Conclusion: LQM-ContextRoute通过将延迟视为服务容量而非简单惩罚项，在高负载、高异质性的工具路由场景中显著优于传统加性奖励方法。消融实验表明容量感知评分、查询特定质量估计和LLM-as-judge反馈三个组件缺一不可。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents increasingly access the same tool type through multiple functionally equivalent providers, such as web-search APIs, retrievers, or LLM backends exposed behind a shared interface. This creates a provider-routing problem under runtime load: the router must choose among providers that differ in latency, reliability, and answer quality, often without gold labels at deployment time. We introduce LQM-ContextRoute, a contextual bandit router for same-function tool providers. Its key design is latency-quality matching: instead of letting low latency offset poor answers in an additive reward, the router ranks providers by expected answer quality per service cycle. It combines this capacity-aware score with query-specific quality estimation and LLM-as-judge feedback, allowing it to adapt online to both load changes and provider-quality differences. On the main web-search load benchmark, LQM-ContextRoute improves F1 by +2.18 pp over SW-UCB while staying on the latency-quality frontier. In a high-heterogeneity StrategyQA setting, LQM-ContextRoute avoids additive-reward collapse and improves accuracy by up to +18 pp over SW-UCB; on heterogeneous retriever pools, it improves NDCG by +2.91--+3.22 pp over SW-UCB. These results show that same-function tool routing benefits from treating latency as service capacity, especially when runtime pressure and provider-quality heterogeneity coexist.

</details>


### [131] [Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242)
*Xiaohua Wang,Kai Yu,XuXiao Liang,Liang Wang,Chao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种AI驱动的胎心率监测模型，通过大规模预训练和专家标注微调实现了高精度的FHR信号重建与分类，在关键指标检测上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统胎心率监测方法受限于设备性能、数据传输及医生主观评估，亟需更客观、精准的监测手段以预防胎儿窘迫和不良结局。

Method: 开发了定制化AI-FHrCTG模型，使用558,412条未标注数据预训练，7,266条专家标注数据微调；引入IOL方法将胎心率分析转化为分类判断。

Result: 关键FHR减速检测灵敏度89.13%、特异度87.78%；加速检测灵敏度62.5%、特异度92.04%；FHR周期性验证AUC=0.7214，振幅变异验证AUC=0.9643。

Conclusion: 该AI-FHrCTG模型有效降低了噪声干扰，精确重建信号，在关键FHR减速和加速检测中展现出良好的敏感性和特异性，且根据Fischer标准验证达到临床应用要求。

Abstract: The monitoring of fetal heart rate (FHR) and the assessment of its variability are crucial for preventing fetal compromise and adverse outcomes. However, traditional methods encounter limitations arising from equipment performance, data transmission, and subjective assessments by doctors. We have developed a tailored AI-based FHrCTG model specifically for FHR monitoring, which effectively mitigates noise interference and precisely reconstructs signals. Our model was pre-trained on a massive dataset consisting of 558,412 unlabeled data points and further refined using 7,266 expert-reviewed entries. To validate FHR, we introduced the Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL) approach, which transforms rate analysis into categorical judgments. Testing revealed that our model demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity in detecting critical FHR decelerations (89.13% and 87.78%, respectively) and accelerations (62.5% and 92.04%, respectively). Furthermore, based on Fischer's criteria for clinical application, our model achieved impressive AUC scores of 0.7214 and 0.9643 for verifying FHR periodicity and amplitude variation, respectively.

</details>


### [132] [Not All Timesteps Matter Equally: Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14252)
*Kai Sun,Peibo Duan,Yongsheng Huang,Guowei Zhang,Benjamin Smith,Nanxu Gong,Levin Kuhlmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对SNN中知识蒸馏忽略时间步差异的问题，提出SeAl-KD方法，通过选择性对齐类别知识和时序知识（等化错误时间步的竞争logits+基于置信度和相似性的时序重加权）提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN知识蒸馏方法对所有时间步施加均匀对齐约束，隐含假设每个时间步的预测应被同等对待。但实践中SNN预测随时间演变，且最终聚合输出正确时中间时间步未必各自正确，有效的蒸馏应为错误时间步提供纠正指导同时保留有用的时序动态。

Method: 提出选择性对齐知识蒸馏(SeAl-KD)，通过等化错误时间步的竞争logits实现类别级知识对齐，以及基于置信度和时间步相似性的重加权策略实现时序对齐。

Result: 在静态图像和神经形态事件驱动数据集上验证，SeAl-KD相比现有蒸馏方法获得一致改进。

Conclusion: SeAl-KD consistently improves SNN performance across both static image and neuromorphic event-based datasets compared to existing distillation methods.

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs), which are brain-inspired and spike-driven, achieve high energy efficiency. However, a performance gap between SNNs and artificial neural networks (ANNs) still remains. Knowledge distillation (KD) is commonly adopted to improve SNN performance, but existing methods typically enforce uniform alignment across all timesteps, either from a teacher network or through inter-temporal self-distillation, implicitly assuming that per-timestep predictions should be treated equally. In practice, SNN predictions vary and evolve over time, and intermediate timesteps need not all be individually correct even when the final aggregated output is correct. Under such conditions, effective distillation should not force every timestep toward the same supervision target, but instead provide corrective guidance to erroneous timesteps while preserving useful temporal dynamics. To address this issue, we propose Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation (SeAl-KD), which selectively aligns class-level and temporal knowledge by equalizing competing logits at erroneous timesteps and reweighting temporal alignment based on confidence and inter-timestep similarity. Extensive experiments on static image and neuromorphic event-based datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over existing distillation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/KaiSUN1/SeAl

</details>


### [133] [Architecture-Aware Explanation Auditing for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14255)
*Sibo Jia,Zihang Zhao,Kunrong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于"原生读取假说"的架构感知解释审计协议，证明了ViT+Attention Rollout在扰动保真度上优于CNN+Grad-CAM，且读取结构兼容性比架构家族更具决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测系统中，深度分类器的热图解释可能视觉上合理但无法真实反映模型决策依据，存在"看似可信但失效"的风险，亟需系统性地审计解释方法的保真度。

Method: 采用零填充扰动协议（3个随机种子）在WM-811K晶圆地图数据集上对比ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout、Swin-Tiny、ResNet18+CBAM、DenseNet121等模型结合Grad-CAM的扰动基删除AUC；同时设置RISE作为模型无关控制组，并进行模糊填充敏感性分析；另在MVTec AD上做探索性边界条件研究。

Result: ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout的Deletion AUC为0.211，显著优于其他模型的0.432-0.525（|Cohen's d|>1.1）；Swin-Tiny虽为Transformer架构但因空间特征图层级结构与Grad-CAM兼容；模型无关方法RISE将所有架构压缩至~0.1的Deletion AUC；模糊填充扰动下家族排序发生逆转，证实保真度排名是(model, explainer, perturbation operator)三元组的联合属性。

Conclusion: 实验表明解释方法的保真度取决于其与模型原生决策机制的读取结构兼容性，而非架构家族本身；建议将解释通路与模型架构协同设计，并要求热图部署时附带定量保真度指标。该审计协议在跨数据集场景下存在依赖性，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection systems increasingly rely on deep classifiers whose heatmap explanations may appear visually plausible while failing to identify the image regions that actually drive model decisions. This paper operationalizes an architecture-aware explanation audit protocol grounded in the native-readout hypothesis: the perturbation-based faithfulness of an explanation method is bounded by its structural distance from the model's native decision mechanism. On WM-811K wafer maps (9 classes, 172k images) under a three-seed zero-fill perturbation protocol, ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout attains Deletion AUC 0.211 against 0.432-0.525 for Swin-Tiny / ResNet18+CBAM / DenseNet121 + Grad-CAM (abs(Cohen's d) > 1.1), despite lower classification accuracy. Swin-Tiny disentangles architecture family from readout structure: despite being a Transformer, its spatial feature-map hierarchy makes it Grad-CAM compatible, showing that the operative factor is readout structure rather than architecture family. A model-agnostic control (RISE) compresses all families to Deletion AUC about 0.1, indicating the gap arises from the explainer pathway; notably, RISE outperforms all native methods, so native readout is a compatibility principle rather than an optimality guarantee. A blur-fill sensitivity analysis shows that the family ordering reverses under a different perturbation baseline, reinforcing that faithfulness rankings are joint properties of (model, explainer, perturbation operator) triples. An exploratory boundary-condition study on MVTec AD (pretrained models) indicates that audit results are dataset/task dependent and identifies conditions requiring qualification. The protocol yields actionable guidance: explanation pathways should be co-designed with model architectures based on readout structure, and deployed heatmaps should be accompanied by quantitative faithfulness metrics.

</details>


### [134] [TILT: Target-induced loss tilting under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14280)
*Kakei Yamamoto,Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TILT is a novel unsupervised domain adaptation method under covariate shift that decomposes the predictor and penalizes an auxiliary component on target data, implicitly inducing self-adaptive importance weighting with theoretical guarantees and empirical improvements over existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unsupervised domain adaptation under covariate shift requires weighting source samples to match target distribution. Existing methods like importance weighting can be unstable, especially when source and target supports are disjoint. The paper seeks a more robust approach with guaranteed boundedness across all source-target pairs.

Method: TILT decomposes the source predictor as f+b, where f is the final target predictor. It fits f+b on labeled source data while simultaneously penalizing the auxiliary component b on unlabeled target inputs. This target-side penalty implicitly induces relative importance weighting using an estimand b*_f that self-localizes to the current error.

Result: Theoretically, TILT maintains uniform boundedness for any source-target pair (even with disjoint supports) and achieves a finite-sample oracle inequality on excess risk with end-to-end guarantees for sparse ReLU networks. Empirically, TILT improves target-domain performance over baselines with stable dependence on regularization parameters.

Conclusion: TILT provides a theoretically grounded approach for domain adaptation with stable regularization behavior, achieving empirical improvements over source-only training, importance weighting, and density-ratio baselines on both synthetic and real-world tasks.

Abstract: We introduce and analyze Target-Induced Loss Tilting (TILT) for unsupervised domain adaptation under covariate shift. It is based on a novel objective function that decomposes the source predictor as $f+b$, fits $f+b$ on labeled source data while simultaneously penalizing the auxiliary component $b$ on unlabeled target inputs. The resulting fit $f$ is deployed as the final target predictor. At the population level, we show that this target-side penalty implicitly induces relative importance weighting at the population level, but in terms of an estimand $b^*_f$ that is self-localized to the current error, and remains uniformly bounded for any source-target pair (even those with disjoint supports). We prove a general finite-sample oracle inequality on the excess risk, and use it to give an end-to-end guarantee for training with sparse ReLU networks. Experiments on controlled regression problems and shifted CIFAR-100 distillation show that TILT improves target-domain performance over source-only training, exact importance weighting, and relative density-ratio baselines, with a stable dependence on the regularization parameter.

</details>


### [135] [Smooth Multi-Policy Causal Effect Estimation in Longitudinal Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14284)
*Wenxin Chen,Weishen Pan,Kyra Gan,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对动态治疗策略评估中孤立估计导致方差膨胀的问题，提出PEQ-Net通过策略编码器与核均值嵌入实现联合估计，结合LTMLE校正证明可稳定有限样本方差，实验验证RMSE大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纵向因果推断方法孤立估计各动态治疗策略，无法跨反事实共享信息，导致即使使用标准去偏的LTMLE仍存在结构未受控的二阶偏差，使有限样本方差膨胀。

Method: 提出PEQ-Net架构，采用策略感知重参数化的ICE Q函数，通过共享策略编码器实现联合估计；使用核均值嵌入训练编码器确保表示空间反映群体层面策略差异；结合LTMLE校正步骤证明对二阶余项施加结构约束。

Result: 在半合成数据集上，PEQ-Net一致优于现有ICE方法，RMSE显著降低，在评估相近策略时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: PEQ-Net通过策略编码器和核均值嵌入实现联合估计，经LTMLE校正后对二阶余项施加结构约束，有效稳定有限样本方差。在半合成数据集上显著优于现有ICE方法，RMSE大幅降低，尤其在评估相近策略时表现突出。

Abstract: Comparative evaluation of multiple dynamic treatment policies is essential for healthcare and policy decisions, yet conventional longitudinal causal inference methods estimate each in isolation, preventing information sharing across counterfactuals. We demonstrate that this separate estimation paradigm induces a structurally uncontrolled second-order bias, inflating finite-sample variance even after standard debiasing with longitudinal targeted maximum likelihood estimation(LTMLE). To address this, we propose a policy-aware reparameterization of Iterative Conditional Expectation (ICE) Q-functions that enables joint estimation through shared representations. We implement this approach in the Policy-Encoded Q Network (PEQ-Net), an architecture centered on a shared policy encoder. The encoder is trained using kernel mean embeddings, ensuring that the learned representation space reflects population-level policy dissimilarities. After applying an LTMLE correction step, we prove this design imposes a structural constraint on the second-order remainder, thereby stabilizing finite-sample variance. Experiments on semi-synthetic datasets demonstrate that PEQ-Net consistently outperforms existing ICE-based methods, achieving substantial reductions in root-mean-square error, particularly when evaluating closely related policies.

</details>


### [136] [Minimal-Intervention KV Retention: A Design-Space Study and a Diversity-Penalty Survivor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14292)
*Libo Sun,Po-wei Harn,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在KV-cache压缩的小预算场景下，系统评估了七种机制后均被否定，继而提出α机制——对TriAttention评分器的单函数修改，用贪心设施选址启发式替代argmax-top-k。在匹配内存条件下，通过预注册协议和Bonferroni校正验证了其在特定模型和预算配置下的有效性，揭示了轻量级评分调整优于复杂结构重新设计的不对称现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前KV-cache压缩研究在小预算场景下存在多种设计选择（缓存表示、逐头路由、压缩节奏、解码行为、预算内评分），但缺乏系统的对比评估和标准化实验协议。

Method: 将TriAttention的argmax-top-k保留评分机制替换为贪心设施选址启发式选择，引入V空间冗余惩罚项，通过单一权重λ控制。在固定开发集上调整λ，在分离的held-out测试集上确认效果。

Result: α在四个(model, budget)实验单元中通过Bonferroni校正的有两个（Qwen b=128和Llama b=64），且无一显著负向结果。发现具有非对称性：最小评分修改优于更复杂的结构性重新设计。

Conclusion: α通过单个参数λ(设为0.5)实现简单修改，在KV-cache压缩领域取得显著进展，在Qwen b=128和Llama b=64两个实验条件下通过Bonferroni校正，表明轻量级评分修改优于结构性重新设计。实验采用匹配内存、sympy评分、held-out确认的标准化协议。

Abstract: KV-cache compression at small budgets is a crowded design space spanning cache representation, head-wise routing, compression cadence, decoding behavior, and within-budget scoring. We study seven mechanisms across these five families under matched mean cache on long-form mathematical reasoning (MATH-500~\cite{hendrycks2021math}) with two distilled-reasoning models (Qwen-7B and Llama-8B variants of DeepSeek-R1-Distill~\cite{deepseek2025r1}) at budgets $b \in \{64, 128\}$. All seven were rejected. We then propose $α$, a one-function modification to the TriAttention~\cite{mao2026triattention} retention scorer that replaces argmax-top-$k$ with greedy facility-location-inspired selection under a V-space redundancy penalty controlled by a single weight $λ$. A pre-registered protocol tunes $λ$ on a frozen development split and confirms on a disjoint held-out split; with $λ= 0.5$, $α$ clears Bonferroni on two of the four (model, budget) cells (Qwen $b{=}128$ and Llama $b{=}64$), no cell is significantly negative, and the pre-registered Branch~A triggers. The finding is asymmetric: a minimal scoring modification beat heavier structural redesigns in this regime, and the combined matched-memory, sympy-graded, held-out confirmation protocol is the evidence standard that made the asymmetry visible.

</details>


### [137] [Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297)
*Matias Alvo,Daniel Russo,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合离散-连续动作空间的强化学习问题，提出HPO算法利用可微模拟与SF估计器的混合梯度策略，在保持无偏性的同时提升梯度质量，并在库存控制和切换LQR任务上验证了优于PPO的性能，同时理论揭示了接近最优时梯度结构的可分离特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无模型策略梯度方法依赖score-function(SF)估计器，在高维设置中存在严重的信用分配问题导致梯度质量差；而可微模拟虽然避免此问题，但离散动作或不光滑动力学导致梯度有偏或信息不足。

Method: 提出混合策略优化(HPO)，通过simulator在可光滑处反向传播，使用结合pathwise梯度和SF梯度的混合梯度估计器，同时保持无偏性；针对动作不连续问题提供重构方法以扩展适用性。

Result: 在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上显著优于PPO，性能差距随连续动作维度增加而增大；理论分析表明在离散最优响应附近，交叉项可忽略，实现连续和离散分量的近似去中心化更新并降低方差。

Conclusion: HPO框架成功结合了 differentiable simulation 的精度和SF估计器的灵活性，交叉项理论揭示了可分离近似的有效性，为大规模混合动作空间问题提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces, such as settings where the discrete component selects a regime (or index) and the continuous component optimizes within it -- a structure common in robotics, control, and operations problems. Standard model-free policy gradient methods rely on score-function (SF) estimators and suffer from severe credit-assignment issues in high-dimensional settings, leading to poor gradient quality. On the other hand, differentiable simulation largely sidesteps these issues by backpropagating through a simulator, but the presence of discrete actions or non-smooth dynamics yields biased or uninformative gradients. To address this, we propose Hybrid Policy Optimization (HPO), which backpropagates through the simulator wherever smoothness permits, using a mixed gradient estimator that combines pathwise and SF gradients while maintaining unbiasedness. We also show how problems with action discontinuities can be reformulated in hybrid form, further broadening its applicability. Empirically, HPO substantially outperforms PPO on inventory control and switched linear-quadratic regulator problems, with performance gaps increasing as the continuous action dimension grows. Finally, we characterize the structure of the mixed gradient, showing that its cross term -- which captures how continuous actions influence future discrete decisions -- becomes negligible near a discrete best response, thereby enabling approximate decentralized updates of the continuous and discrete components and reducing variance near optimality. All resources are available at github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl.

</details>


### [138] [Language-Induced Priors for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14301)
*Qiyuan Chen,Jiayu Zhou,Raed Al Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用LLM从专家文本描述中学习先验知识（LIP），结合EM算法实现冷启动场景下的源域自适应选择，在理论上保证鲁棒性和一致性，实验覆盖描述性、预测性和处方性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动场景下目标数据稀缺，统计方法难以区分相关与无关源域，导致负迁移问题。现有方法忽视了专家文本描述这一易获取资源。

Method: 提出Language-Induced Prior (LIP)框架：通过预训练LLM将专家文本描述转化为概率选择模型，并将其融入EM算法迭代识别源域相关性。该框架兼容任意有似然函数的参数模型，初期依赖LIP引导源选择，随样本积累逐步精炼。

Result: 理论证明：在正确先验下估计量逼近oracle冷启动MSE下界；无论LIP质量如何，估计量保持渐近一致。实验验证：高斯估计、CMAPSS预测任务、MuJoCo跳跃处方任务均验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: LIP框架在目标数据稀缺时显著优于传统方法，且理论保证其鲁棒性（即使先验质量不佳也能保持渐近一致性）。该工作为冷启动领域的域适应提供了一种利用LLM先验知识的新范式，兼具方法灵活性和理论保证。

Abstract: Domain adaptation faces a fundamental paradox in the cold-start regime. When target data is scarce, statistical methods fail to distinguish relevant source domains from irrelevant ones, which often leads to negative transfer. In this paper, we address this challenge by leveraging expert textual descriptions of the target domain, a resource that is often available but overlooked. We propose a probabilistic framework that translates these semantic descriptions into a choice model, namely a Language-Induced Prior (LIP), that learns the preferences from a pretrained Large Language Model (LLM). The LIP is then integrated into an Expectation-Maximization algorithm to identify source relevance. Methodologically, this framework is compatible with any parametric model where a likelihood is available. It allows the LIP to guide the selection of sources when target signals are weak, while gradually refining these choices as samples accumulate. Theoretically, we prove that the estimator roughly matches an oracle cold-start MSE under a correct prior, while remaining asymptotically consistent regardless of the quality of the LIP. Empirically, we validated the framework on a descriptive (Gaussian estimation), a predictive (C-MAPSS dataset), and a prescriptive task (MuJoCo hopper).

</details>


### [139] [Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning for Reusing Local Transition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14304)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出矩阵空间强化学习(MSRL)，通过正半定矩阵描述符表示轨迹片段，捕获一阶和二阶转移统计量，揭示隐结构并支持代数组合，实现跨任务迁移学习，在有限预算目标任务上达到0.73平均AUC，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for compositional generalization in sequential decision-making reuse skills or predictive models but overlook rich local transition geometry and dynamics. There's a need to identify which parts of prior rollouts remain useful for new tasks.

Method: Proposes Matrix-Space RL (MSRL), using positive semidefinite matrix descriptors to represent trajectory segments by aggregating first- and second-order statistics of lifted one-step transitions. These descriptors capture hidden structure, support algebraic composition in matrix space, and enable source-learned matrix-to-value mappings to bootstrap new tasks. Includes obstruction filtering to reject implausible compositions.

Result: MSRL achieves best average finite-budget target AUC of 0.73, outperforming baselines: MSRL from scratch (0.65), TD-MPC-PT+FT (0.63), and TD-MPC (0.57).

Conclusion: MSRL provides a principled geometric framework for transfer learning in RL, with theoretical guarantees on completeness, additivity, and minimal sufficiency of trajectory-segment descriptors, and achieves state-of-the-art performance with 0.73 average AUC on finite-budget tasks.

Abstract: Compositional generalization in sequential decision-making requires identifying which parts of prior rollouts remain useful for new tasks. Existing methods reuse skills or predictive models, but often overlook rich local transition geometry and dynamics. We propose Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning (MSRL), a geometric abstraction that represents trajectory segments through positive semidefinite matrix descriptors aggregating first- and second-order statistics of lifted one-step transitions. These descriptors expose shared hidden structure, support algebraic composition in an abstract matrix space, and reveal opportunities for transfer. We prove that the descriptor is well defined up to coordinate gauge, complete for the induced low-order additive signal class, additive under valid segment composition, and minimally sufficient among admissible additive descriptors. We further show that conditioning value functions on the trajectory-segment matrix yields a first-order smooth approximation of action values, enabling source-learned matrix-to-value mappings to bootstrap learning in new tasks. MSRL is plug-in compatible with standard model-free and model-based methods, while obstruction filtering rejects implausible compositions. Empirically, MSRL achieves the best average finite-budget target AUC of 0.73, outperforming MSRL from scratch (0.65), TD-MPC-PT+FT (0.63), and TD-MPC (0.57).

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311)
*Yuchen Sun,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Anan Du,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBCritic，一种超越二分类的GUI Critic新范式，通过两阶段对比学习在Affordance Space中对齐指令和动作，解决现有二分类模型中有效动作和干扰项得分不可区分的问题。BBCritic-3B在无需额外标注的情况下超越7B参数的最优二分类模型，并展现强跨平台零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
